中子星磁层中的QCD轴子转化

QCD Axion Conversion in Magnetospheres of Neutron Stars

Shubham Yadav, Sachin Dev Kushwaha, Kamni Pawar · hep-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：该文研究强磁场对中子星轴子发射性质的影响，并讨论轴子转化为光子的通量。作者采用APR、FPS和SLY状态方程，在考虑强磁场效应后修改Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff（TOV）方程并生成中子星轮廓。他们假设中子星核心发生Cooper对破坏形成（PBF）和轫致辐射过程，并使用在中心磁场允许范围内随径向距离变化的磁场位形。工作考察了10 meV、15 meV和20 meV轴子质量作为球对称磁化中子星冷却源时的变化，并给出轴子能谱及其转化为光子的结果。分析表明，磁场会显著改变中子星的冷却率和轴子观测量光度；轴子光度对轴子质量和状态方程较敏感，SLY状态方程下与FPS、APR相比观测到的分离较小。对于PSR J1357-6429，在可能轴子质量范围内，低轴子能量时轫致辐射过程的轴子谱主导于PBF过程。结果还显示磁场在较低轴子能量处影响较小，说明在轴子-光子转化机制中有必要纳入磁场。10 meV和15 meV轴子质量的结果相对于20 meV确认了已有的16 meV报告。

English: The axion-converted-photons flux plays a key role in searches of QCD axions as a dark matter (DM) candidate. In addition to solving the strong CP problem, these may explain the properties of the mysterious DM. Neutron star (NS) cooling by neutrino/axion emissions rate constrains the astrophysical properties of superdense matter. We attempt to analyse the impact of strong magnetic fields on the emission properties of axions by employing the APR, FPS, and SLY equation of State (EoS). We modify the Tolman Oppenheimer Volkoff (TOV) equations by considering effects of strong fields' and generate profiles. We assume Cooper-pair-breaking formation (PBF) and the Bremsstrahlung process occurring in the core of NS. We use the radial distance-dependent magnetic field configuration within the permissible limit of the central magnetic field. Our work explores the variations in the axion masses of 10 meV, 15 meV, and 20 meV as a cooling source for spherically symmetric magnetised NSs. We have also shown the energy spectrum of axions and their conversion to photons. Our analysis shows that the cooling rate and the luminosity of observables (axions) for NSs change significantly due to the magnetic field. The results for the luminosity of axions is quite sensitive to its mass and EoS, as the less separation is observed for SLY EoS in comparison to the FPS and APR EoS. We report that the spectrum of axions from the Bremsstrahlung process dominates over the PBF process at lesser axion energies, within the possible axion mass range for PSR J1357-6429 NS. Our results reveal that the impact of the magnetic field is less at lower axion energies, indicating the necessity for including a magnetic field in axion-to-photon conversion mechanisms. The results for the axions mass 10 meV and 15 meV as compared to 20 meV confirms the existing report of the 16 meV.

一句话导读：用修改TOV方程和中子星冷却过程计算强磁场下QCD轴子发射及轴子-光子转化谱。

来自不稳定 ALP catastrogenesis 的可观测引力波

Observable gravitational waves from unstable ALP catastrogenesis

Graciela B. Gelmini, Jonah Hyman, Yu Watanabe · hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：我们关注在脉冲星计时阵列和干涉仪搜索中可观测的引力波（GWs），这些引力波由轴子样粒子（ALP）模型中宇宙弦-壁网络湮灭产生。我们将此过程称为“catastrogenesis”。它产生具有特征峰谱的GWs。如果ALPs稳定，这些GWs将不可观测。因此我们假设ALPs在BBN之前衰变为两个光子。所需的大ALP-光子耦合可通过与辐射背景相互作用引起场耗散和宇宙壁上的摩擦。我们研究这些效应可忽略的条件，并将产生可观测GW信号的参数区域映射到通常的ALP质量-耦合参数空间。我们发现，在这些区域中，ALPs在衰变前主导宇宙能量密度，导致一个中间物质主导时期，这会在GW谱上留下清晰印记。

English: We focus on gravitational waves (GWs) observable in pulsar timing array and interferometric searches that are produced by the annihilation of cosmic string-wall networks in axion-like particle (ALP) models. We called this process "catastrogenesis." It produces GWs with a characteristic peaked spectrum. These GWs would not be observable if the ALPs were stable. We thus assume that ALPs decay into two photons before Big-Bang nucleosynthesis (BBN). The large ALP-photon coupling required could induce field dissipation and friction on cosmic walls through interactions with the radiation background. We study the conditions under which these effects are negligible and map the parameter regions yielding observable GW signals onto the usual ALP mass-coupling parameter space. We find that, in these regions, ALPs dominate the energy density of the Universe before they decay, leading to a period of intermediate matter domination, which would leave a clear imprint on the GW spectrum.

相关度：low — 主题为 ALP 宇宙学与拓扑缺陷产生的引力波，不涉及 AdS/CFT、全息对偶或用户重点的全息超流/畴壁等；仅‘畴壁/引力波’表面相关。

旋转黑洞背景下矢量介子自旋排列的全息研究

Holographic spin alignment of vector mesons in a spinning black-hole background

Fei Wang, Zi-qiang Zhang · hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：论文将 J/ψ 与 φ 置于同一软墙模型的五维等自旋 Myers–Perry AdS 黑洞平面极限背景中，用同一流关联函数框架计算谱函数和自旋排列，并在 a=0 时回到 Sheng 等人的非旋转结果。在 T=0.15 GeV 下，J/ψ 保留清晰共振峰，φ 无窄峰而呈与不稳定 s\bar{s} 对一致的宽分布。对沿 z 的 J/ψ，旋转在低/中动量增强 ρ00^h 相对 1/3 的正偏离、在高动量抑制；沿 x 效应小。对 φ，旋转增强负偏离，沿 z 比沿 x 更明显。相反螺旋度符号及整体排列相反的方位角和快度趋势为解释数据提供参考；与此不符的趋势会挑战最简单的单一均匀旋转介质解释。作者区分模型依赖数值细节与该模型类中稳定的定性特征。

English: Holographic predictions for vector-meson spin alignment are difficult to compare between charmonium and light vector mesons, as the two sectors are typically treated in different rotating backgrounds and spectral prescriptions. Consequently, it remains unclear whether a single rotating medium can generate the opposite polarization preferences observed in data. We address this by placing \(J/\psi\) and \(\phi\) in one soft-wall model on the planar limit of a five-dimensional equal-spin Myers--Perry (MP) AdS black hole, computing spectral functions and spin alignment within the same current-correlator framework; the nonrotating results of Sheng et al. [Phys. Rev. D 110, 056047 (2024)] are recovered at \(a=0\). At \(T=0.15~\mathrm{GeV}\), \(J/\psi\) retains a clear resonance peak, whereas \(\phi\) shows no narrow peak and instead behaves as a broad distribution consistent with unstable \(s\bar{s}\) pairs. For \(J/\psi\) along \(z\), rotation enhances the positive deviation of \(\rho_{00}^{h}\) from \(1/3\) at low and intermediate momenta but suppresses it at high momenta; along \(x\), the effect is small. For \(\phi\), rotation enhances the negative deviation, more along \(z\) than along \(x\). The opposite helicity-frame signs and the opposing azimuthal and rapidity trends of the global alignment provide a reference for interpreting data: trends inconsistent with these would challenge the simplest single homogeneous rotating-medium interpretation. We distinguish model-dependent numerical details from qualitative features stable within this model class.

一句话导读：在单一软墙模型和五维等自旋 Myers–Perry AdS 黑洞背景中统一计算 J/ψ 与 φ 的谱函数和自旋排列，表明同一旋转介质可产生相反极化偏好。

高温下 SYK 模型的快速混合

Fast mixing of the SYK model at high temperatures

Yiyi Cai, Yongtao Zhan, Alexander Zlokapa · quant-ph, cond-mat.dis-nn, cs.DS, math-ph, math.MP · v2 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) 模型是强相互作用费米子系统。由于其全连接无序相互作用，其动力学难以用 Lieb-Robinson 界和集团展开分析。我们证明，在足够高的常数温度下，以对无序的高概率，SYK 模型允许一个谱隙与系统尺寸无关的量子 Gibbs 采样器。这给出了在量子计算机上多项式时间制备 SYK Gibbs 态。此前的非严格计算暗示 SYK 热化，但仅局限于局域关联函数；我们强调我们的结果在任意逆多项式迹距离下成立。我们的证明先引入费米子的伪 Lindbladian 方法，然后应用两个关键新想法：量子动力学的 Bochner 不等式，以及一种基于与经典动力学比较的特别简单的 SYK 分析。

English: The Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model is a strongly interacting fermionic system. Its dynamics are difficult to analyze with Lieb-Robinson bounds and cluster expansions due to its all-to-all disordered interactions. We prove that, with high probability over the disorder, the SYK model admits a quantum Gibbs sampler with a system-size-independent spectral gap at sufficiently high constant temperatures. This yields polynomial-time preparation of SYK Gibbs states on a quantum computer. While prior non-rigorous computations that suggested SYK thermalization were limited to studying local correlators, we emphasize that our result holds up to arbitrary inverse polynomial trace distance. Our proof introduces the pseudo-Lindbladian approach to fermions then applies two key new ideas: a Bochner inequality for quantum dynamics, and an especially simple SYK analysis based on a comparison to classical dynamics.

一句话导读：证明高温 SYK 模型存在系统尺寸无关谱隙的量子 Gibbs 采样器，从而多项式时间制备其 Gibbs 态。

双中子星并合中的多极中微子辐射：角结构、旋转变异性及对电子分数的启示

Multipolar neutrino radiation in binary neutron star mergers: Angular structure, rotational variability, and implications for electron fraction

Kutay Ar{\i}n\c{c} \c{C}okluk, Albino Perego, Federico Maria Guercilena, Kadri Yakut · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc, hep-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文利用完全广义相对论模拟，并结合能量积分的 M1 中微子输运，在一组具有代表性的状态方程、总质量和质量比下，刻画双中子星并合产生的中微子辐射的角结构和时间变异性。主要分析限于在模拟时间间隔内未坍缩为黑洞的并合遗迹。作者在遗迹外的球面上提取依赖角度的中微子能流，并将其分解为球谐函数，以量化其多极内容和演化。在并合后的初始瞬变之后，中微子辐射通量趋于轴对称位形，并由强四极成分主导，由于环面遮挡产生持续的极区通量增强和赤道区抑制。偶极贡献仍处于次要地位，表明不存在持续的单侧发射。各向异性程度随质量不对称性增加以及状态方程变软而增大，反映了遗迹—盘系统的致密性和形态。在时间平均几何之上，还识别出与 m=1 模式相关的相干方位角调制。相关的半频率 f_dom/2≈0.6–0.7 kHz 与模拟遗迹—盘系统中的局域旋转速率进行比较，暗示遗迹结构与中微子变异性之间可能存在动力学关联。最后，作者量化了同一四极主导的辐射几何如何导致依赖纬度的平衡电子分数。极区物质被驱动至接近中微子平衡目标，而赤道区物质则系统性地更富中子并低于平衡。

English: The angular structure and temporal variability of neutrino emission from binary neutron star mergers are characterized using fully general-relativistic simulations with energy-integrated M1 neutrino transport across a representative set of equations of state, total masses and mass ratios. The main analysis is restricted to merger remnants that do not collapse to a black hole within the simulated time intervals. The angle-dependent neutrino energy flux is extracted on a spherical surface outside the remnant and decomposed into spherical harmonics to quantify its multipolar content and evolution. Following the initial post-merger transient, the neutrino radiation flux approaches an axisymmetric configuration dominated by a strong quadrupolar component, producing persistent polar flux enhancement and equatorial suppression due to torus shadowing. The dipolar contribution remains subdominant, indicating the absence of sustained one-sided emission. The degree of anisotropy increases with mass asymmetry and for softer equations of state, reflecting the compactness and morphology of the remnant--disk system. Superimposed on this time-averaged geometry, coherent azimuthal modulations associated with the $m=1$ mode are identified. The associated half frequency, $f_{\mathrm{dom}}/2\simeq0.6$--$0.7\,\mathrm{kHz}$, is compared with local rotation rates in the simulated remnant--disk systems, suggesting a possible dynamical association between the remnant structure and neutrino variability. Finally, we quantify how the same quadrupole-dominated radiation geometry induces a latitude-dependent equilibrium electron fraction. The polar material is driven close to the neutrino-equilibrium target, whereas equatorial material remains systematically more neutron rich and below equilibrium.

一句话导读：完全广义相对论模拟显示，双中子星并合遗迹的中微子辐射在瞬变后呈四极主导的轴对称角结构，并带有 m=1 方位调制，进而造成纬度依赖的电子分数。

Type-D 黑洞的完整准正规模。II. 极端 Kerr 黑洞

Complete quasinormal modes of Type-D black holes. II. Extremal Kerr black holes

Changkai Chen, Xiaohua Zhang, Zhoujian Cao, Jiliang Jing · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：我们提出一种双合流 Heun 方法，将此前关于 Type-D 黑洞完整准正规模（QNM）谱的研究推广到极端 Kerr 黑洞。径向边界条件与角向正则性决定了极端情况下的引力频率。与以往局限于常规黎曼面的谱相比，跨越频率分支割线的解析延拓揭示了非常规黎曼面上的额外 QNM。自旋延拓沿我们此前给出的 Schwarzschild 模式族趋向极端极限，并在指定角向分支与黎曼面上扩展到第 41 阶 overtone。我们首次表明，由非常规 Schwarzschild 模式延拓而来、满足 m<0 的非常规 Class I 模式，在极端极限下形成趋近超辐射边界 Mω=m/2 的零阻尼序列。在固定角向扇区内，有限阻尼序列与零阻尼序列在 Schwarzschild overtone 排序中交错出现，因此单一 overtone 截断无法将它们分开。超辐射边界附近的匹配将积累于临界频率的非极端序列与严格极端情况下的孤立 QNM 区分开来。散射振幅计算验证了代表性根及其黎曼面归属。扩展后的谱澄清了 Schwarzschild 模式族在极端极限下如何重组；完备性通过在指定角向分支与黎曼面上的有界频率域内进行围道计数来确立。

English: We develop a double-confluent Heun method that extends our previous study of complete quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectra of Type-D black holes to extremal Kerr black holes. Radial boundary conditions and angular regularity determine the gravitational frequencies at extremality. Compared with earlier spectra restricted to the conventional Riemann sheet, analytic continuation across frequency branch cuts reveals additional QNMs on unconventional Riemann sheets. Spin continuation follows our Schwarzschild mode families toward extremality, with the survey extending through the 41st overtone on specified angular branches and Riemann sheets. We show for the first time that unconventional Class I modes with $m<0$, continued from unconventional Schwarzschild modes, form zero-damping sequences approaching the superradiant bound $M\omega=m/2$ in the extremal limit. Within a fixed angular sector, finite-damping and zero-damping sequences are interspersed in the Schwarzschild overtone ordering, so a single overtone cutoff cannot separate them. Matching near the superradiant bound distinguishes nonextremal sequences accumulating at the critical frequency from isolated QNMs at exact extremality. Scattering-amplitude calculations verify representative roots and their sheet assignments. The expanded spectrum clarifies how Schwarzschild mode families reorganize at extremality, with completeness established by contour counting within a bounded frequency domain on a specified angular branch and Riemann sheet.

一句话导读：用双合流 Heun 方法计算极端 Kerr 黑洞的完整 QNM 谱，揭示跨黎曼面的额外模式及趋近超辐射边界的零阻尼序列。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典黑洞微扰与 QNM 计算，直接关联用户的 QNM 与经典引力/广义相对论重点标签，但不涉及全息、凝聚态或非线性演化。

新 string-cloud 几何中非对称薄壳虫洞观测外观的正则化效应

Regularization effects on the observational appearance of asymmetric thin-shell wormholes from a new string-cloud geometry

Zijun Zhong, Jonathan A. Rebou\c{c}as, Edson Otoniel, Minyong Guo · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：我们研究新 Letelier–Alencar string-cloud 几何的有理正则化如何改变反射不对称薄壳虫洞的光学外观。在固定领头渐近质量、喉半径、弦强度和正则化分数下，比较两个非正则外部、一个正则观察者外部配一个非正则外部、以及两个正则外部的接合。结合临界尺度、光线类别、轨道数和转移函数与三种发射剖面，并与黑洞对照。正面和倾斜图像采用相同规定发射体运动：最内稳定圆轨道外的圆类时测地线，以及内部保持比能和角动量的向内坠落。尽管核心被切除，正则化改变保留外部和喉部匹配。对采样参数，观察者临界屏幕碰撞参数从 10.392 μ1 降至 9.642 μ1；正则化第二外部后反射区间从 3.890 μ1 收缩至 1.248 μ1，而不改变普通观察者侧光线。返回亮度依赖源支撑和速度相关频移：正则-正则图像在外缘发射模型正面几乎与黑洞对照重合，而内部发射显露返回结构；大倾角可解除近简并。这将正则化依赖的传播与决定图像对比度的发射和观测条件分离。

English: We investigate how rational regularization of the new Letelier--Alencar string-cloud geometry changes the optical appearance of reflection-asymmetric thin-shell wormholes. We compare junctions with two nonregular exteriors, a regular observer exterior paired with a nonregular one, or two regular exteriors at fixed leading asymptotic masses, throat radius, string strength and regulator fractions. Critical scales, ray classes, orbit numbers and transfer functions are combined with three emission profiles and matched black-hole controls. Face-on and inclined images use the same prescribed emitter motion: circular timelike geodesics outside the innermost stable circular orbit and an inward plunge conserving its specific energy and angular momentum inside. Although the core is excised, regularization changes the retained exterior and throat matching. For the sampled parameters, the observer critical screen impact decreases from $10.392\mu_1$ to $9.642\mu_1$, where $\mu_1$ is the observer-side asymptotic mass scale. Regularizing the second exterior then contracts the reflected interval from $3.890\mu_1$ to $1.248\mu_1$ without changing ordinary observer-side rays. Returned brightness depends on source support and velocity-dependent frequency shifts: the regular--regular image nearly coincides with its black-hole control for the outer-edge emission model face-on, while inner emission reveals returned structures. At large sampled inclination that near-degeneracy can be lifted. This separates regularization-dependent propagation from the emission and viewing conditions that determine its image contrast.

一句话导读：研究 string-cloud 薄壳虫洞的正则化如何通过测地线和发射模型改变其光学图像，并与黑洞对照区分传播效应与观测条件。

相关度：medium — 属于经典广义相对论与强引力透镜/虫洞成像，但不是全息；与用户经典引力支线相关，与全息超流等主线不直接相关。

来自 ICTS-TIFR 的长周期、偏心且进动双黑洞模拟目录

A catalog of long, eccentric and precessing binary black hole simulations from ICTS-TIFR

Vaishak Prasad, Prayush Kumar, Michael Boyle, Lawrence E. Kidder, Harald P. Pfeiffer, Mark A. Scheel · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文给出 31 个双黑洞数值相对论模拟目录，使用 Spectral Einstein Code（SpEC）在 ICTS-TIFR 的 Sonic 集群上完成，时间范围 2023 年 8 月至 2024 年 12 月，目标是轨道偏心与自旋-轨道进动同时存在的参数空间。其中 17 个构型同时具有偏心和进动，质量比范围 1≤q≤11，初始偏心率最高达 e0=0.40。最长模拟 EccPrecDiff002 旋进 80 圈（159 个引力波周期），是迄今最长的同时偏心且进动的双黑洞演化。针对 AMD Zen-2 架构的编译期优化使初始数据求解器加速 90%、演化加速 40%，其中约一半演化加速来自 pre-2.34 glibc 缓存拓扑报告缺陷的修复。该项目消耗 1.18×10^6 CPU 小时。文章记录了后处理流程并验证了坐标系变换和波形，偏心率从波形中测量并与初始数据比较。对所有 e_ω<0.2 的构型，双黑洞力学第一定律作为近似螺旋对称性的检验满足优于 0.54%；作者还表明，在共进动坐标系中，进动系统也可视为具有近似螺旋对称性。通过校准数值下限，共进动近似的镜像对称性破坏比下限高三到四个数量级，并随张角从 0.7% 增长到 5.3%。对于 M≤62 M⊙，EccPrecDiff002 无需混合即可覆盖 5 Hz 以上频段，在 1 Gpc 处 Cosmic Explorer 单干涉仪信噪比为 961。

English: We present a catalogue of 31 binary black hole simulations performed with the Spectral Einstein Code (SpEC) on the Sonic cluster at ICTS-TIFR, Bengaluru between August 2023 and December 2024, targeting the sparsely covered corner of parameter space in which orbital eccentricity and spin-orbit precession are present simultaneously: 17 configurations are both eccentric and precessing, spanning mass ratios $1 \le q \le 11$ and initial eccentricities up to $\hat{e}_0 = 0.40$. The longest, EccPrecDiff002, inspirals for 80 orbits (159 gravitational-wave cycles), to our knowledge the longest simultaneously eccentric and precessing binary black hole evolution to date. Compile-time optimisation of SpEC and its third-party library stack for the AMD Zen-2 architecture delivered a 90% speedup of the initial-data solver and a 40% speedup of the evolution, roughly half of the latter traceable to a single defect in how pre-2.34 glibc reported cache topology on AMD processors. The campaign consumed $1.18 \times 10^{6}$ CPU-hours. We document the post-processing pipeline and validate the frame transformation and waveforms. Eccentricity is measured from the waveforms and compared against the initial data. The first law of binary black hole mechanics, a test of approximate helical symmetry, is satisfied to better than 0.54% for every configuration with $e_{\omega} < 0.2$, and we show that even precessing systems can be regarded as possessing approximate helical symmetry in the co-precessing frame. Using the aligned-spin simulations to calibrate the numerical floor, the co-precessing approximation's mirror-symmetry violation lies three to four ordersof magnitude above it, growing from 0.7% to 5.3%with opening angle. EccPrecDiff002 spans the band above 5 Hz without hybridisationfor $M \le 62\,M_\odot$, giving a single-interferometer signal-to-noise ratio of 961 in Cosmic Explorer at 1 Gpc.

一句话导读：发布 31 个偏心且进动双黑洞数值相对论模拟目录，包含迄今最长此类演化，并验证波形与共进动近似螺旋对称性。

相关度：high — 论文属于纯经典引力/数值相对论的双黑洞模拟目录，直接对应“经典引力/广义相对论”重点支线；不涉及全息或量子引力。

能量条件对黑洞可观测量的普适限制

Universal Bounds on Black Hole Observables Imposed by Energy Conditions

Rahul Kumar Walia, Boris Georgiev, Chi-kwan Chan · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：能量条件是广义相对论中许多基本结果的基础，但其在强引力场中的观测有效性仍基本未受检验。本文在广义相对论框架内，针对静态球对称黑洞，建立局部应力能量张量、光子轨道动力学与黑洞阴影、光子环及eikonal引力波铃宕等强场可观测量之间的直接对应。我们表明，在不稳定光子球处施加局部能量条件，会给出这些可观测量的解析限制。特别地，连续光子环的缩倍率提供了局部切向零能量条件的精确诊断。这些限制为约束源时空的物质场、并通过强场观测检验能量条件提供了直接途径。将其与这些可观测量的观测测量相结合，会进一步限制允许的黑洞几何，并额外约束原本不可及的光子球几何与局部应力能量内容，展示其在强场区域的观测潜力。

English: Energy conditions (ECs) underpin fundamental results in general relativity, yet their observational validity in strong gravitational fields remains largely untested. Here, we establish a direct correspondence between the local stress-energy tensor, photon-orbit dynamics, and strong-field observables associated with black hole shadows, photon rings, and eikonal gravitational-wave ringdown for static, spherically symmetric black holes within general relativity. We show that imposing local ECs at unstable photon spheres yields analytic bounds on these observables. In particular, the demagnification of successive photon rings provides an exact diagnostic of the local tangential null energy condition. These bounds offer a direct route to constraining the matter fields sourcing spacetimes and testing ECs through strong-field observations. Combining them with observational measurements of these observables further restricts admissible black hole geometries and additionally constrains the otherwise inaccessible photon sphere geometry and local stress-energy content, demonstrating their observational potential in the strong-field regime.

一句话导读：在静态球对称广义相对论黑洞中，光子球处的局部能量条件给出阴影、光子环和eikonal铃宕的解析普适界限，并可诊断切向零能量条件。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究广义相对论强场黑洞可观测量的能量条件约束，属于用户重点标签中的经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及全息对偶。

旋转相对论性星体的准正规模与不稳定性

Quasi-normal Modes and Instabilities of Rotating Relativistic Stars

Yong Gao, Masaru Shibata · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc, hep-ph, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：我们从全广义相对论中的二阶作用量出发，推导适用于耦合流体—时空本征值问题的基本方程，并在出射波边界条件下精确确定多种快速旋转星体的准正规模。对于 MIT 袋模型奇异星，谱表现出 Chandrasekhar 不稳定性图像，具有不同的 Chandrasekhar–Friedman–Schutz 与类 Jacobi 中性条件，并过渡到动力学棒模增长。对于中子星，我们确定 f 模、r 模及其他惯性模谱及其辐射阻尼或增长时标。f 模与惯性模之间的相互作用揭示避免交叉以及辐射阻尼或增长率的交换。我们通过静态稳定性分析定位准径向坍缩阈值，并通过追踪基本 F 模频率至零独立验证这些阈值，澄清在均匀旋转下动力学不稳定性起始时的中心密度总是大于长期不稳定性对应的中心密度。我们的形式体系和数值实现也可分辨高度阻尼的 w 模。

English: We derive the basic equations suitable for the coupled fluid--spacetime eigenvalue problem from the second-order action in full general relativity and accurately determine quasi-normal modes of a variety of rapidly rotating stars with outgoing-wave boundary conditions. For MIT-bag strange stars, the spectrum realizes Chandrasekhar's instability picture, with distinct Chandrasekhar--Friedman--Schutz and Jacobi-like neutral conditions and the transition to dynamical bar-mode growth. For neutron stars, we determine the $f$-, $r$-, and other inertial-mode spectra and their radiative damping or growth times. Interactions between $f$- and inertial modes reveal avoided crossings and exchanges for radiative damping or growth rates. We locate quasiradial collapse thresholds through a static stability analysis and independently verify them by tracking the fundamental $F$-mode frequency to zero, clarifying that the central density at the onset of the dynamical instability is always larger than that of the secular instability for uniform rotation. Our formalism and numerical implementation can also resolve highly damped $w$-modes.

一句话导读：用全广义相对论二阶作用量计算旋转奇异星与中子星的准正规模和不稳定性，并定位坍缩阈值。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力与准正规模/星体不稳定性支线，匹配经典引力/QNM重点标签，但不涉及全息对偶。

用数值相对论方法计算Lorenz规范下的Kerr度规扰动

Kerr metric perturbations in Lorenz gauge via numerical relativity methods

Nami Nishimura, Nils L. Vu, Adri\`a Gispert-Fern\'andez, Cillian Kelly, Lawrence E. Kidder, Benjamin Leather, Thomas Osburn, Jonathan E. Thompson, Samuel D. Upton, Barry Wardell · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文提出一种基于数值相对论的新计算框架，用于计算Kerr时空中的度规扰动，以支持引力自作用力计算。作者将Lorenz规范下的微扰Einstein方程按方位角m模式在频域表述，并用谱间断Galerkin离散、自适应网格加密和预条件迭代求解器求解所得椭圆偏微分方程。该方法对Kerr时空中圆赤道轨道实现指数收敛的一阶引力自作用力计算，自旋可达|a|/M=0.998（Thorne极限）。该框架设计为可推广到二阶：为一阶数据用于非线性源构造提供准确且良态的结果，并可作为二阶度规扰动的求解器。

English: Gravitational self-force calculations are needed to accurately describe the motion of a small body in a spinning (Kerr) black hole spacetime, which is vital to predict gravitational wave signals from asymmetric-mass compact binaries. Accurate calculations for the upcoming LISA mission require a minimum of second perturbative order in the mass ratio, which has thus far only been achieved for nonspinning (Schwarzschild) black holes due to nonlinear complexities of the Einstein equations. In this work, we calculate Kerr metric perturbations with a new computational approach based on numerical relativity methods, extending recent scalar self-force results to full general relativity. We formulate the perturbative Lorenz-gauge Einstein equations in terms of azimuthal $m$-modes in the frequency domain and solve the resulting elliptic partial differential equations using a spectral discontinuous Galerkin discretization, adaptive mesh refinement, and preconditioned iterative solvers. With our new method, we achieve exponentially convergent first-order gravitational self-force calculations for circular equatorial orbits in Kerr spacetime up to spin $|a|/M=0.998$ (the Thorne limit). Our framework is designed to carry over to second order by delivering accurate and well-behaved first-order data for nonlinear source construction, and as a solver for the second-order metric perturbations themselves.

一句话导读：提出基于数值相对论的谱方法，在Lorenz规范下计算Kerr度规扰动并实现近极限自旋的一阶引力自作用力。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的黑洞扰动与引力自作用力数值计算，直接匹配用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”，但不涉及AdS/CFT全息对偶。

爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡理论中的狄拉克星

Dirac stars in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg theory

Salvatore Capozziello, Allah Ditta, M. Yousaf, G. Mustafa · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：研究了带电经典旋量场与引力及截断至四阶的欧拉-海森堡非线性电动力学耦合的自引力位形。旋量场带 U(1) 规范荷，并可含四阶自相互作用。针对静态球对称位形导出场方程，将其约化为旋量频率的无量纲本征值问题并数值求解。无电荷极限下与已知广义相对论狄拉克星一致。电荷增大会提高频率、质量和尺寸；当电荷耦合从 0 增至 0.75 时，最大质量从 0.709 增至 1.027（以 M_Pl^2/μ 为单位）。四阶项改变电场与包围电荷的关系，使星体内部场弱于麦克斯韦情形。核心结果是：对具有相同费米子数因而相同总电荷的星体，四阶项使质量改变小于 0.2%，但使星体更致密、束缚更强，并把族转折点移到更小粒子数；在电荷耦合 0.6、无量纲 EH 耦合从 0 到 200 时，这一移动使最大质量降低 9%（从 0.924 到 0.840）。在 QED 弱场区，相对质量修正为 -1.5×10^-5(E_max/E_c)^2，因此较大耦合对应的是把四阶模型作为有效非线性电动力学，而非受控 QED 修正。所有解满足零、弱、强和主导能量条件，呈现正的压力各向异性，且无事件视界。通过束缚能和质量曲线转折点讨论了稳定性。

English: We study self-gravitating configurations of a charged classical spinor field coupled to gravity and to Euler-Heisenberg (EH) nonlinear electrodynamics, truncated at quartic order. The spinor is gauged under a U(1) field and may carry a quartic self-interaction. For static and spherically symmetric configurations we derive the field equations, reduce them to a dimensionless eigenvalue problem for the spinor frequency and solve it numerically. In the uncharged limit our solutions coincide with the known Dirac stars of general relativity. The electric charge increases the frequency, the mass and the size of the stars, and the maximal mass grows from $0.709$ to $1.027$ (in units of $M_{\rm Pl}^2/\mu$) when the charge coupling rises from zero to $0.75$. The quartic term changes the relation between the electric field and the enclosed charge, so that the field inside the star is weaker than in the Maxwell case. Our central result concerns stars with the same number of fermions, and therefore the same total charge. For them the quartic term changes the mass by less than $0.2\%$, but it makes the stars more compact and more strongly bound and shifts the turning point of the family to a smaller particle number. This shift is what lowers the maximal mass, by $9\%$ (from $0.924$ to $0.840$) at charge coupling $0.6$ as the dimensionless EH coupling goes from zero to $200$. In the weak-field regime of quantum electrodynamics (QED) the relative mass correction is $-1.5\times10^{-5}(E_{\max}/E_c)^2$, so the larger couplings describe the quartic model as an effective nonlinear electrodynamics rather than a controlled QED correction. All solutions satisfy the null, weak, strong and dominant energy conditions, show a positive pressure anisotropy, and remain free of horizons. Their stability is discussed through the binding energy and the turning point of the mass curve.

一句话导读：数值构造了爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡非线性电动力学中的带电狄拉克星，并分析电荷与四阶非线性项对质量、致密度和稳定性的影响。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的自引力星体位形研究，涉及 Einstein 方程与非线性电动力学，但不涉及 AdS/CFT 或全息对偶；与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”相关。

脉冲星计时阵列对随机引力波背景的全斯托克斯响应：有限距离下完全解析重叠约化函数与近脉冲星对相干性

Full-Stokes response to a stochastic gravitational-wave background in pulsar timing arrays: Fully analytic overlap reduction functions at finite distance and coherence of close pulsar pairs

Kun Zhou, Jin Li · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：我们在广义相对论中推导了脉冲星计时阵列对各向异性张量随机引力波背景全斯托克斯响应的完全解析重叠约化函数。响应保留脉冲星距离及其完整传播相位。在每个固定有限天球多极，强度与圆偏振响应化为涉及指数积分与对数的有限组合，而电、磁线偏振响应仅需初等函数。我们计算到 ℓ=8。对称性区分精确零点与传播可抬升的额外地球项零点。即使非偏振背景也能产生虚交叉谱。对于三维间距为 d 的脉冲星对，当 2π f d/c ≲ 1 时，脉冲星项可保持相干，即使两颗脉冲星距地球很多波长。保留全部四个地球项和脉冲星项的均匀近似将该区域与短波极限连接起来，旋转不变关系固定领头横向磁响应。共同距离误差从对齐对相位中抵消，相对深度误差直接影响相干性。在高斯差分平均下，固定低多极范数与全多极平方和随响应移向高多极以不同速率衰减。已发表脉冲星构型说明距离精度、带宽和计时拟合的作用。在固定逐点强度下，偏振协方差界与相对深度噪声预算量化差分何时抑制背景。独立天空积分与匹配精度基准在研究所覆盖参数范围内检验解析表达式。

English: We derive fully analytic overlap reduction functions for the full-Stokes response of a pulsar timing array to an anisotropic tensor gravitational-wave background in general relativity. The response retains both pulsar distances and their complete propagation phases. At each fixed finite sky multipole, the intensity and circular polarization responses reduce to finite combinations involving exponential integrals and logarithms, while the electric and magnetic linear polarization responses require only elementary functions. We evaluate all components through $\ell=8$. Symmetry separates exact nulls from additional Earth-term nulls that propagation can lift. Even an unpolarized background can produce an imaginary cross-spectrum. For a pair with three-dimensional separation $d$, the pulsar terms can remain coherent when $2\pi fd/c\lesssim1$, even if both pulsars are many wavelengths from Earth. A uniform approximation retaining all four Earth and pulsar terms connects this regime to the short-wavelength limit, and a rotationally invariant relation fixes the leading transverse magnetic response. Common-distance errors cancel from the aligned pair phase, while relative-depth errors directly affect coherence. Under Gaussian differential averaging, fixed low-multipole norms and the squared norm summed over all multipoles decay at different rates as the response shifts to higher multipoles. Published pulsar configurations illustrate the roles of distance accuracy, bandwidth, and timing fits. At fixed pointwise intensity, a polarization covariance bound and a relative-depth noise budget quantify when differencing suppresses the background. Independent sky integration and matched-accuracy benchmarks test the analytic expressions over the parameter ranges studied.

一句话导读：推导脉冲星计时阵列对随机张量引力波背景全斯托克斯响应的有限距离解析重叠约化函数，并分析近脉冲星对相干性、偏振与误差预算。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究广义相对论框架下的随机引力波背景与脉冲星计时阵列响应，属于用户列出的经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及 AdS/CFT 或全息。

Kerr-Newman 准正规模的激发因子与例外点

Excitation factors and exceptional points of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes

Jun-Kun Zhao, Tieguang Zi · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文利用精确分离的引力电磁方程研究 Kerr-Newman 准正规模的激发因子，并考察其例外点（EPs）。在固定电荷下，所研究的引力主导模式的激发因子幅度随自旋增大而减小，而电磁主导模式则呈现单调与非单调行为。第一泛音模式的激发因子幅度可与基模相当甚至更大。我们在引力主导和电磁主导扇区中识别出 42 个模式对发生并合的例外点。部分例外点形成涉及相邻泛音对的序列，较高泛音对并合的位置逐渐更接近极端极限。这些序列之外识别出的其他例外点均与序列成员具有相同的模式对标签，但出现在不同的黑洞自旋与电荷取值处。这些结果阐明了旋转与电荷如何塑造不同模式的相对激发强度，以及 Kerr-Newman 黑洞非厄米谱中的模式并合图样。

English: We study excitation factors and investigate exceptional points (EPs) of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes using the exact separated gravitoelectromagnetic equations. At fixed charge, the magnitudes of the excitation factors for the gravitational-led modes studied here decrease with increasing spin, whereas those for electromagnetic-led modes exhibit both monotonic and nonmonotonic behavior. The magnitude of the excitation factor for the first overtone can be comparable to or larger than that for the fundamental mode. We identify 42 EPs at which pairs of modes coalesce in the gravitational-led and electromagnetic-led sectors. Some EPs form sequences involving successive overtone pairs, with higher-overtone pairs coalescing progressively closer to the extremal limit. The additional EPs identified outside these sequences all share the same mode-pair labels as sequence members, but occur at different values of the black hole spin and charge. These findings clarify how rotation and charge shape the relative excitation strengths of different modes and the pattern of mode coalescence in the non-Hermitian spectrum of Kerr--Newman black holes.

一句话导读：利用精确分离方程计算 Kerr-Newman 准正规模的激发因子，并识别出 42 个模式并合例外点及其随自旋、电荷变化的图样。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究黑洞准正规模的激发因子与例外点，属于经典引力/广义相对论及 QNM 相关方向，与用户重点标签中的经典引力和 QNM 支线高度相关；但不涉及全息或量子引力。

Horndeski 理论中的尺度依赖弱场动力学：星系旋转曲线与折返尺度

Scale-Dependent Weak-Field Dynamics in Horndeski Theory: Galaxy Rotation Curves and Turnaround Scales

Hatice \"Ozer · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文在星系和星系际尺度研究 Horndeski 引力中与引力波相容的一个子类的弱场动力学。分析在弱场近似下针对静态且近似球对称的位形进行，保留导致引力场尺度依赖修改的非线性标量导数相互作用。在星系尺度，通过有效势、圆轨道稳定性、星系旋转曲线以及动力学质量与透镜质量之间的关系研究过渡区域。分析表明，标量场修改轨道动力学和旋转速度的径向依赖，同时在动力学质量与透镜质量估计之间产生非零引力滑移。在星系际尺度，研究渐近弱场动力学，并由有效引力吸引与大尺度曲率贡献之间的平衡导出折返尺度。在大距离上，渐近引力场由背景曲率贡献主导。

English: In this work, the weak-field dynamics of a gravitational-wave compatible subclass of Horndeski gravity is investigated on galactic and intergalactic scales. The analysis is performed within the weak-field approximation for stationary and approximately spherically symmetric configurations, retaining the nonlinear scalar derivative interactions responsible for scale-dependent modifications of the gravitational field. On galactic scales, the transition regime is investigated through the effective potential, the stability of circular orbits, galaxy rotation curves, and the relation between dynamical and lensing masses. The analysis shows that the scalar field modifies the orbital dynamics and the radial dependence of the rotational velocity, while generating a nonvanishing gravitational slip between the dynamical and lensing mass estimates. On intergalactic scales, the asymptotic weak-field dynamics is investigated and a turnaround scale is derived from the balance between the effective gravitational attraction and the large-scale curvature contribution. At large distances, the asymptotic gravitational field is governed by the background curvature contribution.

一句话导读：Horndeski 弱场中的尺度依赖修正改变星系旋转曲线并导出折返尺度。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典修改引力与弱场天体尺度现象，与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线相关，但不是全息或量子引力研究。

用于致密双星并合快速准确波形生成的统一AI框架

A Unified AI Framework for Rapid and Accurate Waveform Generation of Compact Binary Coalescences

Mengfei Sun, Jin Li, Yuanhong Zhong, Jie Wu, Borui Wang, Xianghe Ma, Minghui Zhang · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：未来引力波探测器的天体物理发现潜力取决于准确且快速的波形生成。第三代探测器预计将观测到许多长持续时间的致密双星并合信号，这些信号可探测双星形成与演化以及极端条件下的物质性质。我们开发了一个将物理建模与深度学习相结合的统一框架。该框架使用解析残差谱表示、共享学习以及针对每种双星类型的解码器。它为双黑洞、双中子星和中子星-黑洞双星生成波形，为所有致密双星并合提供单一统一生成器。生成的波形包含各参考模型所描述的物理效应，如自旋进动、高阶模式、物质效应以及双中子星的并合后辐射。波形跨越数百到数千个周期，相对于参考波形的平均失配范围为5.89×10^-4到5.34×10^-2。批量生成相对于参考波形生成实现最高约1800倍加速。

English: The astrophysical discovery potential of future gravitational wave detectors will hinge on accurate and rapid waveform generation. Third generation detectors are expected to observe many long duration compact binary coalescence signals that can probe binary formation and evolution as well as the nature of matter under extreme conditions. We develop a unified framework that combines physical modeling with deep learning. The framework uses an analytic residual spectral representation, shared learning, and decoders for each binary type. It generates waveforms for binary black holes, binary neutron stars, and neutron star black hole binaries, providing a single unified generator for all compact binary coalescences. The generated waveforms include the physical effects described by each reference model, such as spin precession, higher order modes, matter effects, and postmerger radiation for binary neutron stars. The waveforms span hundreds to thousands of cycles, with mean mismatches ranging from $5.89\times10^{-4}$ to $5.34\times10^{-2}$ relative to the reference waveforms. Batch generation achieves a maximum speedup of about 1800 over reference waveform generation.

一句话导读：提出一个结合物理建模与深度学习的统一框架，用于快速、准确地生成黑洞、中子星及混合双星并合的引力波波形。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/引力波波形生成，非 AdS/CFT 全息；与用户列出的经典引力/广义相对论支线相关，但与全息主题无关。

关于二阶度规扰动下 f(R) 引力中的 Isaacson 程序

On Isaacson's Procedure in $f(R)$-Gravity with Second Order Metric Perturbations

Petr V. Tretyakov, Alexander N. Petrov · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：在近期论文 arXiv:2504.02253 中，我们将用于描述高频引力波平均能量动量反作用的经典 Isaacson 程序推广到选取 de Sitter 背景的 f(R) 引力情形。为描述该二阶效应，我们使用了度规张量的标准一阶分解。本文考虑度规张量分解中的二阶项对该过程可能带来的贡献。我们发现，在 de Sitter 背景上，该二阶项的所有可能贡献都被强烈抑制，因此 arXiv:2504.02253 中得到的结果保持不变。

English: In the recent paper arXiv:2504.02253 we generalized classical Isaacson's procedure for describing back-reaction of the averaged energy-momentum for high frequency gravitational waves to $f(R)$ gravity case with a choice of the de Sitter background. To describe this second order effect we used standard first order decomposition of metric tensor. In this paper, we take into account possible contribution to this process from second order term in metric tensor decomposition. We find that on the de Sitter background all possible contributions from this term are strongly suppressed and therefore results obtained in the paper arXiv:2504.02253 are left unchanged.

一句话导读：在 de Sitter 背景的 f(R) 引力中检验 Isaacson 程序的二阶度规扰动贡献，发现其被强烈抑制而不改变先前结果。

相关度：medium — 论文研究 f(R) 引力的经典引力波反作用与二阶度规扰动，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及全息对偶。

虚时间作为相对论的复表示

Imaginary Time as a Complex Representation of Relativity

William McConville · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文以虚时间坐标 X^0=ict 为基础，系统发展洛伦兹相对论的复表示。作者不把它视为到欧氏时空的 Wick 旋转，而是视为可逆的表示变换，将物理时空识别为复向量空间的实子空间；构造复双线性型与 Hermitian 型两种度规结构，并说明它们在物理时空上的限制均回到原洛伦兹间隔。文章把该表示推广到固有时间、四动量和 Hamilton-Jacobi 形式，并说明度规、联络、曲率张量、Einstein 方程与守恒律在弯曲时空中保持通常内容；通过对 Kerr 与 FLRW 几何的显式应用验证旋转及时变时空中的一致性。最后讨论相对论波动方程及其非相对论极限，说明 Schrödinger 方程和欧氏时间演化如何在同一复时间框架中出现。该表述不引入新物理预言，但澄清虚时间坐标的几何角色，并揭示相对论几何、作用量、波传播与解析延拓之间的结构联系。

English: We develop a systematic complex representation of Lorentzian relativity based on the imaginary temporal coordinate $X^0=ict$. Rather than treating this substitution as a Wick rotation to Euclidean spacetime, we formulate it as an invertible change of representation in which physical spacetime is identified with a real subspace of a complex vector space. This construction introduces two related metric structures: a complex-bilinear form and a Hermitian form, both of which reproduce the original Lorentzian interval when restricted to physical spacetime. We extend the representation to relativistic dynamics, including proper time, four-momentum, and the Hamilton-Jacobi formulation, and show that the metric, connection, curvature tensors, Einstein equations, and conservation laws retain their conventional content in curved spacetime. Explicit applications to the Kerr and Friedmann-Lema\^itre-Robertson-Walker geometries demonstrate consistency for rotating and time-dependent spacetimes. We further examine relativistic wave equations and their nonrelativistic limit, showing how the Schr\"odinger equation and its relation to Euclidean-time evolution emerge within the same complex temporal framework. The resulting formulation introduces no new physical predictions, but clarifies the geometric role of imaginary temporal coordinates and reveals structural connections among relativistic geometry, action, wave propagation, and analytic continuation.

一句话导读：将 X^0=ict 表述为洛伦兹相对论的可逆复表示，并验证其在弯曲时空与相对论波动方程中的一致性。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/广义相对论方向，涉及 Einstein 方程、Kerr 与 FLRW 几何；但主题是虚时间的复表示形式，未涉及全息对偶，故对全息研究为中等相关。

多信使天体物理推动澳大利亚引力波天文台的建设论证

Multimessenger astrophysics drives the case for an Australian Gravitational-Wave observatory

Fiona H. Panther, Paul D. Lasky, Nikhil Sarin · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：下一代国际引力波天文台网络正处于高级规划阶段。鉴于澳大利亚在该领域的长期贡献、参与平方公里阵列等大型科学项目的经验以及独特的地理位置和条件，它非常适合在全球网络中承办一座天文台。已有研究表明，位于澳大利亚的引力波探测器在减小引力波事件天区定位不确定性方面发挥重要作用。我们研究澳大利亚天文台的一个具体科学案例，聚焦其对多信使天文学的潜在贡献。我们证明，澳大利亚天文台在第二代和第三代网络中均能以优于1-10平方度的定位精度，使每年最多探测到八次引力波加千新星的多信使事件。我们还表明，在全球第三代/XG天文台网络中，一座具有第二代灵敏度的澳大利亚天文台仍能显著推动多信使科学发展。澳大利亚天文台的完整论证需在更广泛背景下考虑；但本工作表明它可为多信使天体物理作出实质性贡献。

English: The next generation of an international network of gravitational-wave observatories is now in the advanced planning stages. With Australia's substantial history of contributions in this field, its history of scientific mega-projects like the Square Kilometer Array, and its unique geographic location and conditions, it is ideally placed to host an observatory in the global network. It has already been demonstrated that a gravitational wave detector in Australia plays an important role in minimizing the sky localization uncertainty of gravitational wave events. We study a particular science case for an Australian observatory, focusing on its potential contributions to multimessenger astronomy. We demonstrate that an observatory in Australia can enable the detection of up to eight multimessenger gravitational-wave plus kilonova events per year, with localisations better than $1-10\deg^2$ in both second- and third-generation networks. We show that, in a global network of XG observatories, an Australian observatory with 2G sensitivity would still enable significant advances in multimessenger science. The full case for an Australian observatory must be considered in a much broader context; however, this work indicates it could make substantial contributions to multimessenger astrophysics.

一句话导读：该文论证澳大利亚引力波天文台可提升多信使引力波-千新星事件的探测率与定位精度。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/引力波观测方向，与用户重点标签中的经典引力支线相关，但与全息理论无直接关系。

用于二阶自作用理论的Kerr度规微扰

Kerr metric perturbations for second-order self-force theory

Benjamin Leather, Samuel D. Upton, Patrick Bourg, Kevin O. Cunningham, Sam R. Dolan, Conor Dyson, Zachary Nasipak, Nami Nishimura, Andrew Spiers, Maarten van de Meent, Barry Wardell · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：我们计算了Kerr时空中沿圆轨道、赤道面运动的点质量所产生的一阶度规微扰。该微扰专门面向二阶自作用理论的要求：高精度；包含所有贡献，包括与黑洞质量和角动量微扰相关的贡献；在世界线附近为Lorenz规范，在大半径处为（近）Bondi-Sachs规范；并可在整个外部时空高效求值。Lorenz规范在粒子处给出易于理解的奇异结构，而（近）Bondi-Sachs规范确保渐近平直二阶源所需的渐近衰减。我们的形式从可分离的Teukolsky型方程重构度规，分解为自旋加权球谐模式，并在双曲切片上谱求解。所得(ℓ,m)模式和在大部分区域快速收敛，但在点质量附近收敛缓慢；我们通过新颖地使用puncture提取精确m模式数据来克服这一点，输出每m模式精度约1e-10，适用于二阶。结果覆盖Kerr自旋-0.9≤a/M≤0.9和轨道半径至50M。除直接用于二阶自作用计算外，结果还可作为其他涉及Kerr度规微扰计算的输入，包括环境效应和准正规模研究。

English: We present a calculation of the first-order metric perturbation arising from a point mass moving on a circular, equatorial orbit in Kerr spacetime. The metric perturbation is specifically aimed at meeting the requirements of second-order self-force theory: it is highly accurate; incorporates all contributions, including those associated with perturbations to the black hole's mass and angular momentum; is in Lorenz gauge near the worldline and in (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge at large radius; and can be efficiently evaluated across the entire exterior spacetime. The Lorenz gauge gives a well-understood singular structure at the particle, while the (near) Bondi--Sachs gauge ensures the asymptotic fall-off needed for an asymptotically flat second-order source. Our formalism reconstructs the metric from separable Teukolsky-type equations, decomposed into spin-weighted spheroidal harmonic modes and solved spectrally on hyperboloidal slices. The resulting $(\ell,m)$-mode sums converge rapidly across most of the domain, but slowly near the point mass; we overcome this with a novel use of a puncture to extract accurate $m$-mode data, yielding output accurate to $\sim 10^{-10}$ per $m$-mode and suitable for use at second order. Our metric-perturbation results cover a range of Kerr spins $-0.9 \le a/M \le 0.9$ and orbital radii out to $50M$. In addition to its immediate application in second-order self-force calculations, our results can also be used as input to other calculations involving Kerr metric perturbations, including studies of environmental effects and quasinormal modes.

一句话导读：高精度计算Kerr时空中圆轨道点质量的一阶度规微扰，服务于二阶自作用理论。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典广义相对论中的Kerr黑洞微扰与自作用计算，直接对应经典引力/广义相对论重点支线；不涉及全息。

Kerr时空的多域谱方法：用于自力计算的双曲型Teukolsky m模式求解器

Multi-domain spectral method for Kerr spacetimes: a hyperboloidal Teukolsky $m$-mode solver for self-force computations

Brad Cownden, Rodrigo Panosso Macedo, Christiana Pantelidou, Samuel D. Upton, Barry Wardell · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文求解频率域m模式Teukolsky方程，针对Kerr黑洞周围圆轨道、赤道面点质量源产生的自旋权重s=-2 Weyl标量ψ4。方程定义在到达事件视界和未来零无穷的双曲型切片上，因此仅靠正则性即可选出物理的推迟解，无需施加辐射条件。粒子采用m模式puncture/有效源方案处理，所得二维椭圆问题用四域伪谱方法求解，并通过场的偶/奇分解处理被打破的赤道对称性。作者在轨道半径r0=10M、自旋至|a|/M=0.9（包括顺行和逆行轨道）下测试方法。至无穷远的能量通量与独立Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit结果在主导模式上约一致到10^-7，在中等分辨率下对所有m≤20模式优于2×10^-5；静态m=0模式无需特殊处理。

English: We solve the frequency-domain, $m$-mode Teukolsky equation for the spin-weight $s=-2$ Weyl scalar $\psi_4$ sourced by a point mass on a circular, equatorial orbit around a Kerr black hole. The equation is posed on hyperboloidal slices that reach the event horizon and future null infinity, so that the physical (retarded) solution is selected by regularity alone, without imposing radiation conditions. The particle is treated with the $m$-mode puncture/effective-source scheme, and the resulting two-dimensional elliptic problem is solved with a four-domain pseudo-spectral method in which the equatorial symmetry is broken and handled by an even/odd decomposition of the field. We test the method for orbital radius $r_0=10M$ and spins up to $|a|/M=0.9$, covering prograde and retrograde orbits. The energy fluxes to infinity agree with independent Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit results to $\sim10^{-7}$ for the dominant modes and to better than $2\times10^{-5}$ for all modes up to $m=20$ at moderate resolutions. The static $m=0$ mode requires no special treatment.

一句话导读：提出并验证了双曲型切片上的多域谱Teukolsky m模式求解器，用于Kerr黑洞自力计算。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/广义相对论与黑洞微扰数值方法，并非全息研究；与用户的经典引力支线相关，但不属于全息重点方向。

有限距离大质量粒子偏折的参考重正化雅可比原函数

Reference-renormalized Jacobi primitives for finite-distance massive-particle deflection

Reggie C. Pantig · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-04

中文摘要：本文为静态球对称时空中大质量粒子的有限距离弱偏折建立了参考重正化的雅可比原函数高斯-博内形式。构造基于与类时测地线相关的固定能量雅可比度规，并将径向曲率原函数视为仅定义到相加常数的量。作者不通过在类时圆轨道处令原函数为零来固定常数，而是在相同守恒粒子能量下减去参考雅可比几何，并对曲率密度差施加端点归一化。这给出了不依赖圆轨道的原函数表示，同时保留由局部源和接收方向定义的有限距离偏折角操作定义。作者证明当两种处方均可适用时，参考归一化与圆轨道归一化一致，因为其原函数仅差一个在精确曲率条带积分中抵消的常数。该方法适用于不存在可容许类时圆轨道、轨道位于黎曼雅可比区域之外、或自然无透镜比较几何非欧几里得的情形。文中在 Schwarzschild-like bumblebee、Kottler 和 JNW 时空中展示了该形式。该形式表明圆轨道锚定是辅助的原函数规范选择，而非有限距离雅可比高斯-博内偏折的前提。

English: We develop a reference-renormalized Jacobi-primitive Gauss-Bonnet formalism for finite-distance weak deflection of massive particles in static, spherically symmetric spacetimes. The construction uses the fixed-energy Jacobi metric associated with timelike geodesics and treats the radial curvature primitive as a quantity defined only up to an additive constant. Instead of fixing this constant by requiring the primitive to vanish at a circular timelike orbit, we subtract a reference Jacobi geometry at the same conserved particle energy and impose endpoint normalization on the curvature-density discrepancy. This gives a circular-orbit-free primitive representation of the Gauss-Bonnet curvature contribution while preserving the operational finite-distance definition of the bending angle through local source and receiver directions. We prove that the reference-normalized and circular-orbit-normalized prescriptions agree whenever both are admissible, since their primitives differ only by a constant that cancels from the exact curvature strip integral. The method remains applicable when no admissible circular timelike orbit exists, when the orbit lies outside the Riemannian Jacobi domain, or when the natural no-lens comparison geometry is non-Euclidean. We demonstrate the formalism in three representative settings: Schwarzschild-like bumblebee, Kottler, and Janis-Newman-Winicour (JNW) spacetimes. The formalism therefore identifies circular-orbit anchoring as an auxiliary primitive gauge choice rather than a prerequisite for finite-distance Jacobi Gauss-Bonnet deflection.

一句话导读：提出不依赖圆轨道锚定的参考重正化雅可比原函数高斯-博内方法，用于静态球对称时空中大质量粒子的有限距离弱偏折。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的引力透镜与时空偏折研究，匹配“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签；不涉及全息或量子引力。

准正规模相遇：Kerr–Newman 例外点附近的伪谱

Quasinormal-mode encounters: Pseudospectra near Kerr--Newman exceptional points

Kyriakos Destounis · gr-qc, hep-ph, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：例外点（EP）是准正规模频率及其本征向量合并的点，可增强黑洞准正规模的谱不稳定性。本文通过伪谱和条件数研究 Kerr–Newman 时空中无质量中性标量场以及耦合引电磁（GE）扰动的这种行为。对标量场，作者沿 (ℓ,m)=(1,1) 椭球分支并由应力-能量张量导出能量范数；对 GE 扰动，使用具有正辐射能量的耦合四场系统。沿匹配参数路径趋近所选 EP 时，频率劈裂遵循平方根规律，条件数按到 EP 距离的负平方根增长；二者乘积近似双极点系数范数，不依赖偏移量，对 GE 也不依赖所用角向截断和投影。伪谱等值线分辨出从单模敏感性到模式对集体响应的转变。在所选背景和分辨率下，模式对两个成员比同一背景中两个更阻尼的模式更敏感，但这种排序仅在低于某个交叉偏移时成立，且该偏移随径向分辨率降低。对 GE 模式对，角向细化还会使合并位置移动超过所选偏移。本文强调，在所考虑的离散化问题和能量范数内，这些结果描述 EP 附近谱不稳定性的定性趋势而非连续谱幅度，并推动进一步研究其在黑洞铃宕中的可能作用。

English: Exceptional points (EPs), at which two quasinormal frequencies and their eigenvectors coalesce, can enhance the spectral instability of black hole quasinormal modes. We investigate this behavior in Kerr--Newman spacetimes through pseudospectra and condition numbers for neutral massless scalar fields and coupled gravitoelectromagnetic (GE) perturbations. For the scalar field, we follow the $(\ell,m)=(1,1)$ spheroidal branch and derive the energy norm from the stress-energy tensor. For GE perturbations, we use a coupled four-field system equipped with a positive radiative energy. Along matched parameter paths toward the selected EPs, the frequency splitting follows a square-root law and the condition numbers grow as the inverse square root of the distance to the EP. Their product, which approximates the norm of the double-pole coefficient, does not depend on the offset or, for GE, on the angular truncation and projection used. The pseudospectral contours resolve the transition from individual-mode sensitivity to the collective response of the pair. At the selected backgrounds and resolution, both pair members are more sensitive than two more-damped modes at the same background, but this ordering holds only below a crossover offset that decreases with radial resolution. For the GE pair, angular refinement also displaces the coalescence by more than the selected offset. Within the discretized problems and energy norms considered here, these results describe qualitative trends of spectral instability near EPs rather than continuum magnitudes, and motivate further study of their possible role in black-hole ringdown.

一句话导读：用伪谱与条件数研究 Kerr–Newman 黑洞准正规模例外点附近的谱不稳定性及其对铃宕的潜在意义。

相关度：medium — 论文直接研究黑洞准正规模、例外点与谱不稳定性，匹配“QNM”和“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签；但不涉及全息超流、探针膜、multi-trace 等用户主要全息研究支线。

引力Noether荷对内部视界数目的限制

Constraints on the number of inner horizons from gravitational Noether charges

Jing-Peng Ye, Run-Qiu Yang · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：黑洞内部研究的一个核心问题是物质和几何如何限制视界数目。在Einstein引力中，一种已知方法是利用场方程的ADM分解导出关于lapse的微分不等式，从而限制多个内部视界的可能存在。我们表明，同样的阻碍可以用Komar积分表述为引力Noether荷的体-边平衡。在高阶曲率引力中，直接的ADM分析变得更复杂，而引力Noether荷自然保留这种体-边结构。我们将引力Noether荷分析自然应用于Lovelock引力，引入一个类TCC的曲率条件，并证明它与热力学动机的正Wald熵假设一起，允许在非退化事件视界内至多存在一个非退化内部Killing视界。这表明该阻碍反映了体引力响应与视界热力学之间的相容性。

English: A central question in the study of black hole interiors is how matter and geometry constrain the number of horizons. In Einstein gravity, one known method is to use the ADM decomposition of the field equations to derive a differential inequality for the lapse, which then constrains the possible existence of multiple inner horizons. We show that the same obstruction can instead be formulated through the Komar integral as a bulk-boundary balance of gravitational Noether charge. In higher-curvature gravity, a direct ADM analysis becomes more complicated, whereas the gravitational Noether charge naturally retains this bulk-boundary structure. We naturally apply the gravitational Noether charge analysis to Lovelock gravity. We introduce a TCC-like curvature condition and prove that, together with the thermodynamically motivated assumption of positive Wald entropy, it allows at most one nondegenerate inner Killing horizon inside a nondegenerate event horizon. This suggests that the obstruction reflects a compatibility between bulk gravitational response and horizon thermodynamics.

一句话导读：用引力Noether荷的体-边平衡给出Lovelock引力中内部Killing视界数目的限制。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力与黑洞时空结构研究，并涉及视界热力学；不是全息对偶研究，但与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线直接相关。

来自约束瞬子的黑洞自由能景观

Black hole free energy landscapes from constrained instantons

Shan-Ping Wu, Jia-Zhou Liu, Yu-Xiao Liu, Shao-Wen Wei · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们应用引力路径积分分解，为固定外部温度和角速度下的广义黑洞自由能景观提供半经典解释。边界积分变换将形式配分函数表示为对约束扇区的积分，这些扇区由辅助超曲面上的完备局部 partial-Neumann 数据标记。独立变分确立成对的正则子区域解作为约束瞬子，其拉格朗日乘子支持时间流逝和切向位移的跳跃，以容纳热失配和转动失配。对于若干具有渐近边界的非极端定常族，约束鞍点作用量取 I_constr=β F_gen=β(E_B - Ω_B^I J_{B,I}) - S 的形式，其中 β=T^{-1}。这里 E_B 和 J_{B,I} 是重整化哈密顿荷，S 是黑洞熵，其对作用量的贡献来自正则视界帽。当保留的变分独立张成熵和角动量方向时，景观的内部驻点对应于光滑平衡鞍点。我们通过保留完整的辅助界面数据，将构造推广到无曲率导数的局部宇称偶 f(Riemann) 理论。相同热力学形式在 Wald 熵下成立，且不需要小亏缺展开。Schwarzschild 和 Kerr 黑洞说明 Einstein 构造，而 Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet 引力中的五维静态 AdS 黑洞和 new massive gravity 的转动 BTZ 分支为高阶曲率推广提供显式检验。

English: We apply gravitational path integral factorization to provide a semiclassical interpretation of generalized black hole free energy landscapes at fixed external temperature and angular velocities. Boundary integral transforms express the formal partition function as an integral over constrained sectors labeled by complete local partial-Neumann data on an auxiliary hypersurface. Independent variation establishes paired regular subregion solutions as constrained instantons, with Lagrange multipliers supporting lapse and tangential shift jumps that accommodate thermal and rotational mismatches. For selected nonextremal stationary families with an asymptotic boundary, the constrained saddle action takes the form $I_{\rm constr}=\beta\mathcal F_{\rm gen}=\beta(E_{\mathcal B}-\Omega_{\mathcal B}^{I}J_{\mathcal B,I})-S$, with $\beta=T^{-1}$. Here $E_{\mathcal B}$ and $J_{\mathcal B,I}$ are renormalized Hamiltonian charges and $S$ is the black hole entropy, whose contribution to the action comes from the regular horizon cap. Interior stationary points of the landscape correspond to smooth equilibrium saddles when the retained variations independently span the entropy and angular momentum directions. We extend the construction to local, parity-even $f(\mathrm{Riemann})$ theories without curvature derivatives by retaining the complete auxiliary interface data. The same thermodynamic form follows with Wald entropy, without a small-deficit expansion. Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes illustrate the Einstein construction, while five-dimensional static AdS black holes in Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity and the rotating BTZ branch of new massive gravity provide explicit checks of the higher-curvature extension.

一句话导读：用约束瞬子和引力路径积分分解半经典地解释固定温度/角速度下黑洞广义自由能景观，并推广到高阶曲率引力与 Wald 熵。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦黑洞自由能景观、约束瞬子与引力热力学，并检验高阶曲率、AdS 与 BTZ 黑洞；与经典引力/广义相对论和引力热力学相关，但不涉及全息超流、p波、multi-trace、QNM、探针膜等用户重点支线。

Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov 虫洞的轴度规/极规范扰动

Axial-metric/polar-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole

Abhishake Sadhukhan · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们研究 Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov（MMP）虫洞在将轴度规扰动与极规范扰动配对的宇称扇区中的线性扰动。费米子最低朗道能级（LLL）通过其电荷响应；其精确 Schwinger 电流使纵向电场获得 MMP 屏蔽质量 m_S^2=αq^2，而轴度规扰动以相对阶 l(l+1)/q 移动每个 LLL 态的世界面规范场。我们假设 Casimir 应力张量跟随倾斜的场线。力平衡与电荷守恒随后将极化电流（极霍尔电流的对应物）确定到自守恒电流为止。更高朗道能级通过一阶 η_H=g_c^2/12π^2 的单圈 Euler-Heisenberg 作用进入；向这些能级的虚跃迁可能以 l(l+1)/q 移动的阶进入，但未作估计。在费米子背反 α 的一阶，系统化为三个闭式耦合主方程，且口部与喉部匹配。一致性并不选择费米子本构关系；在无导数的局部闭合中，协变性将其固定到一个核。α=0 时，喉部有一个被屏蔽道、一个轻质量道和一个无质量中性道，且 Schwinger 响应已经破坏两种宇称的等谱性。更高能级在 AdS2 单位下将轻质量平方提高 η_H l(l+1)，并进一步破坏等谱性。在形式点 m_S^2=0，有质量道在每个闭喉能级上具有实的 O(α) 移动；轻道在物理 m_S^2 下、在屏蔽道以下的每个能级上也如此。中性道具有精确的零能解，即沿场线的持续电流，O(α) 口部项与更高能级均使其保持不变。有限元计算未发现其下方存在束缚态，其稳定性仍待研究。

English: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP) wormhole in the parity sector pairing axial metric and polar gauge perturbations. The lowest Landau level (LLL) of the fermions responds through its charge. Its exact Schwinger current gives the longitudinal electric field the MMP screening mass $m_S^2=\alpha q^2$, and the axial metric perturbation shifts the worldsheet gauge field of every LLL state at relative order $l(l+1)/q$. We assume that the Casimir stress tensor follows the tilting field lines. Force balance and charge conservation then fix a polarisation current, the counterpart of the polar Hall current, up to self-conserved currents. The higher Landau levels enter through the one-loop Euler-Heisenberg action, to first order in $\eta_{\rm H}=g_c^2/12\pi^2$. Virtual transitions to them could enter at the order of the $l(l+1)/q$ shift and are not estimated. At first order in the fermion backreaction $\alpha$ the system reduces to three coupled master equations in closed form, and mouth and throat match. Consistency does not select the fermion constitutive relation. Among local closures without derivatives, covariance fixes it up to one kernel. At $\alpha=0$ the throat has a screened, a light massive and a massless neutral channel, and the Schwinger response already breaks the isospectrality of the two parities. The higher levels raise the squared light mass by $\eta_{\rm H}l(l+1)$ in AdS$_2$ units and break it further. At the formal point $m_S^2=0$ the massive channels have real $O(\alpha)$ shifts on every closed-throat level, as does the light channel at the physical $m_S^2$ on every level below the screened channel. The neutral channel has an exact zero-energy solution, a persistent current along the field lines, which the $O(\alpha)$ mouth terms and the higher levels leave intact. Finite-element runs find no bound state below it, and its stability is open.

一句话导读：研究 MMP 全息虫洞在轴度规-极规范扇区的线性扰动，分析费米子最低朗道能级 Schwinger 响应、背反修正与稳定性。

相关度：medium — 论文研究 MMP 虫洞的线性引力扰动和费米子响应，属于 AdS/CFT 框架下的全息构造，同时涉及经典引力扰动，与用户的全息和经典引力支线相关，但不直接涉及全息超流、p波、multi-trace 等重点支线。

Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov虫洞的极向度规/轴向规范扰动

Polar-metric/axial-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole

Abhishake Sadhukhan · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们研究Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov可穿越虫洞的线性扰动，在极向度规扰动与轴向规范扰动耦合的扇区，并将其约化为两个Zerilli-Moncrief型耦合主方程。在喉部，带电费米子最低朗道能级的Casimir能量维持虫洞张开；仅当费米子响应时，线性化Bianchi恒等式才成立。一旦其状态固定，它们的二维响应由共形反常给出。我们假设施加每条场线上的度规随局域场强标度，将其提升到四维。通过迹反常，这种标度将场强与世界面曲率耦合，类似于Wen-Zee项。该响应伴随一个Hall电流，后者作为轴向规范模式的源。在费米子反作用α的零阶，喉部为AdS2×S2，模式是两个Pöschl-Teller问题，其质量平方为l(l-1)和(l+1)(l+2)。我们以闭式给出O(α)修正。在口部，费米子通过同阶的反常起作用，且口部与喉部匹配。更高朗道能级通过一圈Euler-Heisenberg作用量起作用。我们将其保留到η_H=g_c^2/12π^2的一阶，该量不被1/q压低。它增大AdS2半径，并分裂两个模式共享的能级。在所考察的闭合喉部能级上，它们的O(α)混合为二阶。那里由喉部中心的静态观察者测量的O(α)频率移动为实数，并依赖于所假定的标度；此处与在坐标平面上的提升进行比较。逐区域地，在α和η_H的一阶、在所考察的能级上，我们在l=2,3,4未发现增长模式，尽管O(α)口部依赖于一个估计。尚未证明虫洞整体的稳定性。

English: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov traversable wormhole in the sector where polar metric perturbations couple to axial gauge perturbations, and reduce it to two coupled master equations of Zerilli-Moncrief type. In the throat the Casimir energy of the lowest Landau level of charged fermions holds the wormhole open, and the linearised Bianchi identities hold only if the fermions respond. Once their state is fixed, their two-dimensional response follows from the conformal anomaly. We lift it to four dimensions on the field lines, assuming that the metric of each line scales with the local field strength. Through the trace anomaly this scaling couples the field strength to the worldsheet curvature, reminiscent of a Wen-Zee term. The response comes with a Hall current that sources the axial gauge mode. At zeroth order in the fermion backreaction $\alpha$ the throat is AdS$_2\times S^2$ and the modes are two Poschl-Teller problems with squared masses $l(l-1)$ and $(l+1)(l+2)$. We give the $O(\alpha)$ corrections in closed form. In the mouths the fermions act through the anomaly at the same order, and mouths and throat match. The higher Landau levels act through the one-loop Euler-Heisenberg action. We keep it to first order in $\eta_{\rm H}=g_c^2/12\pi^2$, which is not suppressed by $1/q$. It enlarges the AdS$_2$ radius and splits the levels shared by the two modes. On the closed-throat levels examined their $O(\alpha)$ mixing is of second order. The $O(\alpha)$ frequency shifts there, measured by the static observer at the throat centre, are real and depend on the assumed scaling, compared here with a lift on the coordinate planes. Region by region, at first order in $\alpha$ and $\eta_{\rm H}$ and on the levels examined, we find no growing mode at $l=2,3,4$, although the $O(\alpha)$ mouth rests on an estimate. Stability of the wormhole as a whole is not shown.

一句话导读：将MMP可穿越虫洞的极向度规-轴向规范线性扰动约化为Zerilli-Moncrief型主方程，并计算O(α)频率修正与所考察能级的稳定性。

相关度：medium — 论文属于全息可穿越虫洞的经典线性扰动与稳定性分析，直接对应“经典引力/广义相对论”重点；但未涉及多数其他重点标签（超流/超导、p波、畴壁、multi-trace、气泡成核、D3-D7等），且没有明确的QNM或非线性演化内容。

具有闵可夫斯基核心的正则黑洞：能量条件阻碍与光学屏蔽

Regular black holes with Minkowskian cores: energy-condition obstruction and optical screening

Francisco Tello-Ortiz, Y. G\'omez-Leyton, Alejandro Rueda, Kazuharu Bamba, Manuel Gonz\'alez-Espinoza · gr-qc, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文利用引力解耦构造了一个具有闵可夫斯基核心的渐近平直黑洞几何，并分析其静态与旋转推广。作者并不要求全局能量条件成立，而是指出：闵可夫斯基核心所要求的中心密度消失，会在当前静态闭合类中产生局域零能量条件阻碍。精确质量函数可用正则化不完全伽马函数表示，从而清楚显示视界结构和形变的指数局域化。这种局域化给出一个解析的光学屏蔽机制：在黑洞分支上，临界碰撞参数相对史瓦西的偏离由光子球处指数压低的质量亏缺控制。文章还澄清了旋转推广的地位：曲率不变量在物理区域 r≥0 上保持有限，但这一局域陈述本身不足以建立穿过 Kerr 盘的全局正则解析延拓。结果表明，局域核心正则化与全局完备性应加以区分，并解释了为何内部强修改可以与外部光学观测量的小改变共存。

English: We construct an asymptotically flat black-hole geometry with a Minkowskian core using gravitational decoupling and analyze its static and rotating extensions. Rather than imposing global energy-condition viability, we show that the vanishing central density required by the Minkowskian core produces a local null-energy-condition obstruction for the present static closure class. The exact mass profile can be written in terms of the regularized incomplete gamma function, which makes transparent both the horizon structure and the exponential localization of the deformation. This localization yields an analytic optical-screening mechanism: on the black-hole branch, deviations of the critical impact parameter from Schwarzschild are controlled by the exponentially suppressed mass deficit at the photon sphere. We also clarify the status of the rotating extension: curvature invariants remain finite on the physical domain $r\geq0$, but this local statement does not by itself establish a globally regular analytic extension through the Kerr disk. These results separate local core regularization from global completeness and explain why strong modifications of the interior can coexist with very small changes in exterior optical observables.

一句话导读：构造带闵可夫斯基核心的渐近平直正则黑洞，指出局域零能量条件阻碍，并给出由形变指数局域化导致的光学屏蔽机制。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典广义相对论与黑洞时空几何研究，涉及能量条件、光子球和光学观测量；不涉及全息对偶或量子引力，但直接契合经典引力支线。

相对论性与牛顿性 Proca 星：两种极限的故事

Relativistic and Newtonian Proca Stars: A Tale of Two Limits

Alberto Diez-Tejedor, Carlos Herdeiro, Claudio Lazarte, Eugen Radu, Armando A. Roque, Nicolas Sanchis-Gual, Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：研究爱因斯坦-普罗卡理论在牛顿极限 ω→μ 下的一类代表性静态孤子解，并与自旋-1薛定谔-泊松系统的有效描述进行比较。虽然爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈尔登情形中的对应较直接，但 Proca 场的矢量性质以及非相对论自旋-1情形的增强 U(3) 对称性带来若干需要仔细处理的新特征。我们通过将 ℓ=0 电 Proca 星与径向极化（hedgehog）构型、ℓ=1 电 Proca 星与线极化构型对应起来，建立两种描述之间的映射，并澄清基态的一些方面，解决相对论解与牛顿解在形态和稳定性性质上的若干表面差异。一个重要结论是，非相对论极限支持比相对论理论更丰富的稳定平衡构型谱，包括稳定的激发态。

English: We investigate a representative set of static solitonic solutions of the Einstein-Proca theory in the Newtonian regime, where the field frequency approaches the particle mass, $\omega \to \mu$, and compare them with the corresponding solutions of the spin-1 Schr\"odinger-Poisson system, which provides the effective description in this limit. While this correspondence is relatively straightforward in the Einstein-Klein-Gordon case, the vector nature of the Proca field, combined with the enhanced $U(3)$ symmetry of the nonrelativistic spin-1 regime, gives rise to several nontrivial features that require careful analysis. We establish a mapping between the two descriptions by identifying $\ell=0$ electric Proca stars with radially polarized (hedgehog) configurations and $\ell=1$ electric Proca stars with linearly polarized configurations. We further clarify some aspects of the ground state and resolve several apparent discrepancies between relativistic and Newtonian solutions, particularly concerning their morphology and stability properties. An important conclusion of this work is that the nonrelativistic regime supports a richer spectrum of stable equilibrium configurations than the relativistic theory, including stable excited states.

一句话导读：比较爱因斯坦-普罗卡星与其牛顿极限下的自旋-1薛定谔-泊松孤子，建立映射并澄清形态与稳定性差异。

相关度：medium — 直接属于经典引力/广义相对论中的自引力孤子研究，涉及 Proca 星与牛顿极限；不涉及 AdS/CFT 或全息字典，但与用户经典引力支线相关。

通过分层规范化构造体拓扑序

Constructing Bulk Topological Orders via Layered Gauging

Shang Liu · cond-mat.str-el, hep-th, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：理解存在对称性时的量子相与相变是量子多体物理的核心目标。拓扑全息为研究该问题提供了一个现代范式，它将 k 维中的对称性与 (k+1) 维中的“体”拓扑序联系起来。尽管概念深刻，现有大多数体构造方法依赖复杂数学形式，且难以应用于某些对称类型。本文提出一种物理直观且通用的方法，称为分层规范化构造，以从 k 维广义对称性系统生成 (k+1) 维（液体或 fracton）拓扑序。粗略地说，该方案是将许多层具有特定对称性的 k 维量子系统堆叠成 (k+1) 维堆叠，然后依次规范化作用在每对相邻层上的对角对称性。具体步骤依赖于对称类型。作者在不同空间维度、具有常规、高阶形式、子系统、反常、非阿贝尔或不可逆对称性的多个例子中实现了该方法，并因此猜想该方法具有普遍性。例如，从二维 plaquette Ising 模型的子系统对称性出发，导出了 X-cube 模型以及一个各向异性 fracton 拓扑序；从一维反常 Z2 对称性出发，构造了一个实现 double semion 拓扑序的新方格晶格模型。从量子纠错角度看，该分层规范化构造可视为超图乘积构造在标准 Calderbank-Shor-Steane 设定之外的推广。

English: Understanding quantum phases and phase transitions in the presence of symmetries is a central objective of quantum many-body physics. A powerful modern paradigm for investigating this problem is topological holography, which relates symmetries in $k$ dimensions to "bulk" topological orders in $(k+1)$ dimensions. While conceptually profound, most existing bulk construction methods rely on sophisticated mathematical formalisms and can be difficult to apply to certain symmetry types. In this work, we propose a physically intuitive and versatile method, termed the layered gauging construction, to systematically generate $(k+1)$-dimensional (liquid or fracton) topological orders from $k$-dimensional generalized symmetries. Roughly speaking, the prescription is to stack many layers of $k$-dimensional quantum systems with certain symmetries into a $(k+1)$-dimensional pile, and then sequentially gauge a diagonal symmetry acting on each nearest-neighbor pair of layers. The detailed procedure depends on the specific symmetry types. We have successfully implemented the method in a number of examples in different spatial dimensions, with symmetries that are conventional, higher-form, subsystem, anomalous, nonabelian, or noninvertible. We hence conjecture the method to be very general. For example, from the subsystem symmetry of the $2d$ plaquette Ising model, we derive the X-cube model and also an anisotropic fracton topological order. Additionally, starting from an anomalous $Z_2$ symmetry in $1d$, we construct a new square lattice model realizing the double semion topological order. From a quantum-error-correction perspective, our layered gauging construction may be viewed as a generalization of the hypergraph-product construction beyond the standard Calderbank-Shor-Steane setting.

一句话导读：提出分层规范化方法，从 k 维广义对称性系统构造 (k+1) 维体拓扑序，并给出 fracton、double semion 等例子。

相关度：low — 论文研究拓扑全息、广义对称性与体拓扑序构造，属于量子多体/量子信息方向；与用户重点的全息超流超导、p波与多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、multi-trace、QNM与非线性演化、D3-D7/Floquet驱动、经典引力等无直接交集。

关于耗散潮汐的新自旋视角：致密双星旋进中的一阶后牛顿效应

A New Spin on Dissipative Tides: First-Post-Newtonian Effects in Compact Binary Inspirals

Anand Balivada, Abhishek Hegade K. R., Nicol\'as Yunes · gr-qc, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文研究自旋致密双星中潮汐耗散对晚期旋进引力波信号的特征修正。作者建立了自旋致密双星中耗散型电四极潮汐的次领头阶后牛顿描述，推导了质心运动方程、推广的能量平衡定律，以及自旋与轨道角动量平行或反平行的准圆轨道对应的傅里叶相位修正。采用与旋转对称性相容的最一般低频线性潮汐响应，他们表明自旋诱导的潮汐耗散在 2.5 后牛顿阶进入引力波相位，并带有对数频率依赖，因此不会与并合相位简并。对于双黑洞，其耗散通量在极端质量比极限下重现视界吸收。

English: Tidal dissipation in spinning compact binaries imprints characteristic corrections on the late-inspiral gravitational-wave signal. We develop a next-to-leading order post-Newtonian description of dissipative, electric-quadrupolar tides in spinning compact binaries, deriving the center-of-mass equations of motion, a generalized energy-balance law, and the corresponding Fourier-phase correction for quasi-circular orbits with spins aligned or anti-aligned with the orbital angular momentum. Using the most general, low-frequency, linear tidal response compatible with rotational symmetry, we show that spin-induced tidal dissipation enters the gravitational-wave phase at 2.5 post-Newtonian order and carries a logarithmic frequency dependence, so it is not degenerate with the coalescence phase. For binary black holes, our dissipative flux reproduces horizon absorption in the extreme-mass-ratio limit. These results provide new waveform ingredients for precision modeling of spinning compact binaries in the high-signal-to-noise era.

一句话导读：在自旋致密双星中建立耗散电四极潮汐的次领头阶后牛顿描述，并给出自旋诱导潮汐耗散的 2.5PN 波形相位修正。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论与引力波波形建模，直接对应“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签；但不涉及全息、AdS/CFT 或用户列出的其他全息凝聚态/非平衡支线。

Einstein-Maxwell-scalar 理论中的稳定性分析与双临界现象

Stability analysis and double critical phenomenon in the Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory

Zi-Qiang Zhao, Mei-Ling Yan, Zhang-Yu Nie, Jing-Fei Zhang, Xin Zhang · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, hep-ph, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们研究了包含高阶自相互作用项 λ|ψ|^4、τ|ψ|^6 以及非最小耦合 h(ψ)=e^{α|ψ|^2} 的全息超流模型中的动力学稳定性和相变行为。热力学与动力学稳定性分析表明，系统的热力学稳定性和动力学稳定性一致。相图分析揭示了丰富的临界和超临界现象。对于固定的 λ<0 和 α，增大 τ 会将一级相变区域收缩到一个临界点，然后进入超临界区域。当改变 α 时，系统可以表现出无临界点，并且最值得注意的是出现双临界现象：随着 α 增大，系统先进入超临界区域，然后重新进入一级相变区域。这种由单一参数驱动的双临界现象首次在全息超流模型中被报道，揭示了非最小耦合与高阶相互作用项之间复杂的非单调耦合效应。

English: We investigate the dynamical stability and phase transition behavior in a holographic superfluid model incorporating higher-order self-interaction terms $\lambda |\psi|^4$, $\tau|\psi|^6$, and a non-minimal coupling $h(\psi)=e^{\alpha|\psi|^2}$. Thermodynamic and dynamical stability analyzes show that the thermodynamic stability and dynamical stability of the system are consistent. Phase diagram analysis reveals rich critical and supercritical phenomena. For fixed $\lambda<0$ and $\alpha$, increasing $\tau$ shrinks the first-order phase transition region to a critical point and then enters the supercritical region. When varying $\alpha$, the system can exhibit no critical point and, most notably, a double critical phenomenon in which, as $\alpha$ increases, the system first enters the supercritical region and then re-enters the first-order phase transition region. This double critical phenomenon driven by a single parameter is reported for the first time in holographic superfluid models, revealing a complex nonmonotonic coupling effect between the non-minimal coupling and higher-order interaction terms.

一句话导读：在全息超流模型中，高阶自相互作用与非最小耦合可导致热/动力学稳定一致及单参数驱动的双临界现象。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究全息超流模型的热力学/动力学稳定性与相变临界现象，属于全息超流超导和相变核心主题；未涉及经典引力或其他重点支线。

致敬 S. Deser：广义引力理论中的守恒量

A Tribute to S. Deser: Conserved Quantities in Generic Gravity Theories

Bayram Tekin · gr-qc, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我描述与 S. Deser [1931年3月19日—2023年4月21日] 的部分合作工作，始于2001年我在 Brandeis 大学做博士后时。我们的工作主要但不完全关于高阶曲率引力理论的守恒荷。我也描述一些近期进展，例如用线性化 Riemann 张量表示守恒荷；我详细阐述相关计算。我还回忆二十多年来超越科学合作的交往。本综述的物理部分面向对扩展引力理论感兴趣的研究生和研究人员，尤其是这些理论中的守恒量与微扰技术。

English: I describe parts of my joint work with S. Deser [March 19, 1931 - April 21, 2023] which started when I was working as a post-doc at Brandeis University in 2001. Our work was mostly, but not exclusively, on conserved charges of higher curvature theories of gravity. I also describe some recent developments, such as expressing the conserved charges in terms of the linearized Riemann tensor; I expound upon the computations. I also reminisce about our interaction for over two decades that went beyond our scientific collaboration. The physics part of this review is intended for graduate students and researchers interested in extended theories of gravity, especially about the conserved quantities and perturbative techniques in these theories.

一句话导读：综述高阶曲率等扩展引力理论中守恒荷的构造及用线性化 Riemann 张量表述的近期进展。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，讨论扩展引力理论中的守恒荷与微扰方法，与“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签直接相关；但不涉及全息超流、p波、畴壁、QNM 等用户其他重点支线。

Bogomolny点处的精确全息超流体：输运、大电荷与Rényi熵

Exact holographic superfluid at the Bogomolny point: transport, large charge, and R\'enyi entropies

Siqing Yu · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：AdS5中的Abelian Higgs模型在探针极限下是强耦合超流体的标准全息模型，通常需数值求解。当标量质量饱和Breitenlohner-Freedman界且四次耦合取Bogomolny值时，其凝聚相可在每个具有最大对称空间截面的静态不带电AdS5背景上以闭式给出。我们证明它是线性稳定的，正如标准量子化中具有非负四次耦合且无超流的任何无节点探针极限凝聚相一样。在每种切片上，它回答对偶CFT的不同问题。在平面黑膜上，它是所有低于Tc温度下的超流体：超流组分严格遵循Gorter-Casimir双流体定律，声速与声衰减以闭式给出。在整体AdS5中，它描述R×S3上电荷Q的态，可说是最低能量态，并在所有Q下以闭式给出其标度维度（体论经典）。其激发包括超流体有效理论不描述的塔，它们来自探针扇区的红外不动点。双曲切片以闭式给出计算球带电Rényi熵的黑洞的均匀毛发相。其凝聚在纠缠面上不为零。当由插入该处的算符源激发时，该相在零化学势下Rényi指数恰为一时出现，如同中性标量所见。

English: The Abelian Higgs model in AdS$_5$, in the probe limit, is a standard holographic model of a strongly coupled superfluid, usually solved numerically. When the scalar mass saturates the Breitenlohner-Freedman bound and the quartic coupling takes the Bogomolny value, its condensed phase can be found in closed form on every static, uncharged AdS$_5$ background with maximally symmetric spatial sections. We prove that it is linearly stable, as is any nodeless probe-limit condensate in the standard quantization with a nonnegative quartic coupling and no superflow. On each slicing it answers a different question about the dual conformal field theory. On the planar black brane it is the superfluid at all temperatures below $T_c$: the superfluid fraction follows the Gorter-Casimir two-fluid law exactly, and the speed and the attenuation of sound are found in closed form. In global AdS$_5$ it describes a state of charge $Q$ on $\mathbb{R}\times S^3$, arguably the lowest-energy one, and gives its scaling dimension in closed form at all $Q$, classically in the bulk. Its excitations include towers that the superfluid effective theory does not describe; they come from an infrared fixed point of the probe sector. The hyperbolic slicing gives in closed form a homogeneous hairy phase of the black holes that compute the charged R\'enyi entropies of a ball. Its condensate does not vanish at the entangling surface. When sourced by operators inserted there, the phase sets in at R\'enyi index exactly one at zero chemical potential, as found for a neutral scalar.

一句话导读：该文在Bogomolny点给出AdS5探针Abelian-Higgs全息超流体的精确闭式解，并解析研究输运、大电荷谱与带电Rényi熵。

相关度：high — 直接属于全息超流/超导方向：在Bogomolny点给出精确凝聚解，并覆盖输运、大电荷与Rényi熵，与重点标签“全息超流超导”高度吻合。

全息超流超导
