高维 Cardy 增长的纠缠上界

Entanglement Bounds on Cardy Growth in Higher Dimensions

Yuya Kusuki, Yin Tang · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文提出在幺正共形场论中把热熵与纠缠联系起来的界：四维中 f≤4π²a，三维中 f≤2.99830949…F。这里 f 是热有效作用中宇宙学常数项的无量纲系数，a 是 Euler 反常系数，F 是球面自由能。这些界自然地来自所提出的球面 Rényi 熵不等式的高温极限；这些不等式推广了四维的共形对撞机界和三维的类似猜想。为检验该提议，我们给出一个同时适用于两维度的更强猜想不等式，用应力张量三点函数表示，并被每个自由理论饱和。我们在弱耦合规范理论、大 N 矢量模型和全息 CFT 的可控区域中建立该不等式，有限 N 数值结果提供额外支持。该提议暗示，二维 CFT 中控制 Cardy 公式与纠缠熵的普适系数相等这一事实，在高维以普适不等式的形式推广。

English: We propose bounds relating thermal entropy to entanglement in unitary conformal field theories: $f\le4\pi^2a$ in four dimensions and $f\le2.99830949\ldots\,F$ in three dimensions. Here $f$ is the dimensionless coefficient of the cosmological constant term in the thermal effective action, $a$ is the Euler anomaly coefficient, and $F$ is the sphere free energy. These bounds arise naturally as the high-temperature limits of proposed inequalities for spherical R\'enyi entropy that extend the conformal collider bound in four dimensions and an analogous conjecture in three dimensions. To verify this proposal, we formulate a stronger conjectural inequality common to both dimensions, expressed in terms of the stress-tensor three-point function and saturated by every free theory. We establish this inequality in controlled regimes of weakly coupled gauge theories, large-$N$ vector models, and holographic CFTs. Finite-$N$ numerical results provide additional support. Our proposal suggests that the equality between the universal coefficients governing the Cardy formula and entanglement entropy in two-dimensional CFTs extends to higher dimensions in the form of a universal inequality.

一句话导读：提出并检验将热有效作用常数与纠缠熵联系起来的普遍上界，暗示二维 CFT 中 Cardy 公式与纠缠熵系数相等在高维推广为不等式。

相关度：low — 论文讨论 CFT 中热熵与纠缠熵的普遍上界，并用全息 CFT 作为验证区域之一；不涉及用户重点标签中的全息超流超导、p 波、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、相变气泡成核、QNM、冷原子或 D3-D7/Floquet 探针膜等方向。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

论 AdS5×S5 与 AdS4×CP3 中 AdS2 超弦配分函数的两圈计算

On 2-loop AdS$_2$ superstring partition function in AdS$_5 \times S^5$ and AdS$_4\times CP^3$

Arkady A. Tseytlin · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们重新审视 AdS5×S5 和 AdS4×CP3 中靠近 AdS2 面展开的 GS 超弦配分函数的两圈计算，其对偶为 N=4 SYM 和 ABJM 理论中的 1/2 BPS 圆形 Wilson 圈。arXiv:2601.08809 曾在 AdS5×S5 中用维数正规化完成该计算并发现一个 UV 极点。由于维数正规化对 GS 弦存在问题，这里改用热核截断。此时局域路径积分测度的贡献正比于 δ(0)，不能再被忽略。我们证明它会抵消作用量中四次相互作用产生的 δ(0) 项，只留下正规化玻色模式数与费米模式数之差 Str E 的平方。因此结果 UV 有限，arXiv:2601.08809 中的极点只是维数正规化的假象。若所有场都用相同的 smear kernel 正规化，则 Str E=0，两圈系数 f2 消失，这与 SYM 理论要求的 f2=3/(32π^2) 相矛盾。在 AdS4×CP3 情形中，同样的抵消留下一个有限项，它正比于无质量二维费米子 Green 函数重合值的平方，讽刺地给出 f2=3/(32π^2)，而不是 localization 要求的 f2=0。该项是 arXiv:2609.14497 中 AdS4×S7/Z_k 内 AdS2×S1 M2 膜结果的 level-zero（弦）部分；在那里它被更高的 S1 模式抵消，而这些模式也移除了与 AdS2 超对称相冲突的无质量费米子有限质量移动。这暗示 type II 弦路径积分测度应包含额外贡献，在 IIA 情形由母 M2 膜提供；在 AdS5×S5 情形中该贡献尚待识别，并可能解决与 localization 之间剩余的两圈不匹配。

English: We revisit the 2-loop computation of the partition function of the GS superstring expanded near the AdS$_2$ surface in AdS$_5\times S^5$ and AdS$_4\times {\rm CP}^3$, dual to $\frac{1}{2}$ BPS circular Wilson loop in ${\cal N}=4$ SYM and ABJM theories. In arXiv:2601.08809 it was done in AdS$_5\times S^5$ using dimensional regularization and a UV pole was found. Since dimensional regularization is problematic for the GS string, here we use a heat-kernel cutoff instead. Then the contribution of the local path integral measure, proportional to $\delta(0)$, can no longer be ignored. We show that it cancels the $\delta(0)$ terms coming from the quartic interactions in the action, leaving the square of the difference ${\rm Str}\,E$ between the regularized numbers of bosonic and fermionic modes. The result is thus UV finite, the pole of arXiv:2601.08809 being an artefact of dimensional regularization. If all fields are regularized with the same smearing kernel, ${\rm Str}\,E=0$ and the 2-loop coefficient $f_2$ vanishes, in tension with $f_2=\frac{3}{32\pi^2}$ in SYM theory. In the AdS$_4\times{\rm CP}^3$ case the same cancellation leaves a finite term proportional to the square of the coincident value of the massless 2d fermion Green's function, giving, ironically, $f_2=\frac{3}{32\pi^2}$ instead of $f_2=0$ required by localization. This term is the level-zero (string) part of the result of arXiv:2609.14497 for the AdS$_2\times S^1$ M2 brane in AdS$_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$, where it is cancelled by the higher $S^1$ modes, which also remove a finite mass shift of the massless fermion in conflict with the AdS$_2$ supersymmetry. This suggests that the type II string path integral measure should contain an extra contribution, supplied in the IIA case by the parent M2 brane; in the AdS$_5\times S^5$ case it is still to be identified and may resolve the remaining 2-loop mismatch with localization.

一句话导读：用热核截断重算 AdS2 超弦两圈配分函数，表明先前 UV 极点是维数正规化假象，并指出与 localization 的系数不匹配可能需修正弦路径积分测度。

相关度：low — 论文属于 AdS/CFT 与弦论配分函数的基础技术计算，讨论 Wilson 圈对偶、正规化与 localization 匹配；与所列全息超流超导、p波/多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、QNM、D3-D7/D3-D5 探针膜等具体支线无直接交集。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

初圈之爱：近极端黑洞的量子可变形性

Love at First Loop: Quantum Deformability of Near-Extremal Black Holes

Pablo A. Cano, Marina David, Roberto Emparan, Stefano Trezzi · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：在经典、四维、渐近平直广义相对论中，黑洞的静态 Love 数消失。我们研究近极端 Reissner–Nordström 黑洞对最小耦合无质量标量的响应，在近水平喉部的 Schwarzian 模式强烈涨落的能量尺度下如何改变。此时黑洞作为具有态谱的量子系统响应，其静态响应是由这些态之间虚跃迁给出的极化率。对于 ℓ=0，静态 Love 数因标量的平移对称性而消失；但对于 ℓ≥1 的多极，静态 Love 数非零：自旋 ℓ 中间态的有限转动能破坏了导致经典结果消失的抵消。由于喉部不再具有可读取潮汐响应的经典几何，我们通过推迟 Green 函数定义它，并在 r_+ω 领头阶计算其完整频率依赖。其保守部分需要重整化：近水平理论固定其对数项的系数，从而给出任何局域项都无法消除的能量依赖，而其有限局域项是四维理论的匹配数据。耗散部分有限且与方案无关。对于 ℓ≥1，当能量低于转动能隙时它在低频消失，而在能隙阈值处对频率非解析，并产生晚期 t^{-3/2} 尾。

English: The static Love numbers of black holes vanish in classical, four-dimensional, asymptotically flat General Relativity. We study how this changes for the response of near-extremal Reissner--Nordstr\"om black holes to a minimally coupled massless scalar, at energies where the Schwarzian mode of the near-horizon throat fluctuates strongly. The black hole then responds as a quantum system with a spectrum of states. Its static response is a polarizability, given by virtual transitions between them. For $\ell=0$ the static Love number vanishes, by the shift symmetry of the scalar, but for multipoles $\ell\geq1$ the static Love numbers are nonzero: the finite rotational energy of the spin-$\ell$ intermediate states spoils the cancellation responsible for the classical result. Since the throat no longer has a classical geometry from which to read off a tidal response, we define it through the retarded Green's function, and compute its full frequency dependence at leading order in $r_+\omega$. Its conservative part requires renormalization: the near-horizon theory fixes the coefficient of its logarithm, and with it a dependence on the energy that no local term can remove, while its finite local terms are matching data of the four-dimensional theory. The dissipative part is finite and scheme independent. For $\ell\geq1$, it vanishes at low frequencies when the energy is below the rotational gap, while at the gap threshold it becomes non-analytic in frequency and produces a late-time $t^{-3/2}$ tail.

一句话导读：研究近极端RN黑洞在Schwarzian强涨落下的量子潮汐响应，发现ℓ≥1静态Love数非零，并给出推迟Green函数的频率依赖与t^{-3/2}晚时尾。

相关度：medium — 论文研究黑洞量子响应与推迟Green函数的频率依赖及晚时尾，涉及Schwarzian模式，与用户重点关注的QNM/响应类标签部分相关，但不直接属于全息超流超导、p波序参量、探针膜或相变成核等方向。

超导穹顶的快速全息反演

Fast holographic inversion of superconducting domes

Sejin Kim · hep-th, cs.AI · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：一个标量质量依赖于规范场强度 M(F^2) 的全息超导体，在合适的 M 下能重现超导穹顶，但从穹顶反演 M 过去需要数天。我们得到由从视界和边界出发的两个积分确定临界温度的条件，并由同样两个解得到其对 M 的导数，因此训练无需有限差分，一次反演需十分钟到一小时。借助这一速度，我们研究最低掺杂处 F^2 的视界值与 F^2=0 之间的区间；没有视界达到 F^2=0，而在该区间端点 M(0) 固定对偶算符的维度。将 M(0) 固定于若干值、固定质量并重新训练其余部分，我们发现重构在 M 的极小值处共享，并在视界可达处一致（最深端除外），但在该区间上不同。一个保留具有最简单闭合形式重构的规则，可恢复由二次 M 生成的穹顶的标量质量和质量函数。在高斯与双高斯穹顶以及 YBa2Cu3Oy 和 2M-WS2 的测量相图上，其选择依赖并列或狭窄差距，并且对于从其保留的闭合形式生成的四个穹顶，只恢复其中两个的标量质量。因此穹顶只在视界可达处约束 M，而不约束标量质量；后者需要第二个可观测量来确定。

English: A holographic superconductor whose scalar mass depends on the gauge field strength, $M(\Fsq)$, reproduces a superconducting dome for a suitable $M$, but recovering $M$ from a dome has taken days. We obtain the condition that fixes the critical temperature from two integrations, started at the horizon and at the boundary, and its derivative with respect to $M$ from the same two solutions, so training needs no finite differences and an inversion takes ten minutes to an hour. With this speed we study the interval between the horizon value of $\Fsq$ at the lowest doping and $\Fsq=0$, which no horizon reaches and at whose end the scalar mass $M(0)$ fixes the dimension of the dual operator. Holding $M(0)$ at several values, the pinned masses, and retraining the rest, we find reconstructions that share the minima of $M$ and agree wherever the horizons reach, apart from the deepest end, but differ on that interval. A rule that keeps the reconstruction with the simplest closed form recovers the scalar mass and the mass function of a dome generated from a quadratic $M$. On Gaussian and double-Gaussian domes and the measured phase diagrams of YBa$_{2}$Cu$_{3}$O$_{y}$ and 2M-WS$_{2}$ its choice rests on ties or narrow margins, and it recovers the scalar mass of only two of the four domes generated from the closed forms it keeps for them. The dome thus constrains $M$ where its horizons reach but not the scalar mass, which a second observable would have to fix.

一句话导读：提出快速全息反演方法，从超导穹顶反推标量质量函数，并指出穹顶仅在其视界可达区域约束 M，标量质量需额外观测量确定。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究全息超导体的超导穹顶反演与标量质量重构，属于用户重点的全息超流超导方向。

Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov 方程与 AdS$_3$ 中弦的解析结构

Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov equations and the analytic structure of strings in AdS$_3$

Sergio Iguri, Nicolas Kovensky, Julian H. Toro · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文研究 AdS$_3$ 中弦关联函数的极点结构及其对 AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ 对偶的编码。作者提出一种替代方法：从世界面关联函数的局域化性质出发，在相应轨迹上求解 Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov 方程，并对任意 $n$ 和任意 spectral flow 荷显式完成世界面模空间的积分。由此直接从精确世界面理论并包含归一化，重现了此前用自由场方法得到的留数。作者预计这将为计算其余留数并最终确定 AdS$_3$ 弦振幅的完整解析结构提供系统框架。

English: The pole structure of string correlators in AdS$_3$ encodes essential information about the AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ duality. The corresponding residues for spectrally flowed three-point functions have been extracted from their exact expressions and shown to match the predictions of the holographic CFT. Away from the tensionless point, the latter is defined perturbatively in terms of a deformed symmetric orbifold. For $n$-point functions, such a matching has so far only been achieved for a specific subset of poles, and only by means of free-field techniques. Here we develop an alternative approach. We show that, starting from the localization properties of worldsheet correlators, one can solve the Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov (KZ) equations on the corresponding locus and perform the integration over worldsheet moduli explicitly for any $n$ and for arbitrary values of the spectral flow charges. In this way we reproduce, directly from the exact worldsheet theory and including the normalization, the residues previously obtained with free-field methods. We expect this to provide a systematic framework for computing the remaining residues and, ultimately, for determining the full analytic structure of string amplitudes in AdS$_3$.

一句话导读：用 KZ 方程和世界面局域化系统计算 AdS$_3$ 弦关联函数的留数，推进其完整解析结构。

相关度：medium — 论文属于 AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ 全息对偶与弦世界面关联函数的基础研究，与理论全息方向相关；但未直接涉及用户重点列出的全息超流超导、探针膜、QNM、相变成核等具体支线。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

光晶格上的矩阵全息

Matrix Holography on an Optical Lattice

Robin L\"owenberg, Julian Sonner · hep-th, cond-mat.quant-gas, physics.atom-ph, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们提出一种基于原子系综晶格与时间平均 Floquet 动力学的模拟量子模拟方案，用以构造通常出现在玻色 BFSS 类矩阵量子力学中的四体势。与需要深电路的基于门数字实现不同，我们的方法在每个 Floquet 周期通过固定的一组控制阶段生成目标模型，使连续幺正操作的数目不随矩阵尺寸 N 增长。我们构造中的主要标度成本来自控制不断增大的晶格所需的频率范围；我们证明该成本至多按 O(N^3) 增长，从而给出通往大 N 的多项式标度路径，有利于实验实现。此外，我们证明耦合参数可被调至 O(1) 以上，从而能够模拟全息相所需的强耦合物理。

English: We propose an analog quantum-simulation protocol based on a lattice of atomic ensembles and time-averaged Floquet dynamics to construct the four-body potentials that typically appear in bosonic BFSS-like matrix quantum mechanics. In contrast to gate-based digital implementations requiring deep circuits, our approach generates the target model through a fixed set of control stages per Floquet cycle, keeping the number of consecutive unitaries constant with respect to the matrix size $N$. The principal scaling cost in our construction is given by the frequency range required to control the growing lattice. We show that this cost grows at most as $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$, giving a polynomial scaling route to large$-N$, attractive to experimental implementation. Additionally, we demonstrate that the coupling parameter can be tuned beyond $\mathcal{O}(1)$, enabling the simulation of strong-coupling physics necessary for holographic phases.

一句话导读：提出用原子系综光晶格与时间平均 Floquet 动力学模拟 BFSS 类矩阵量子力学，控制成本随 N 多项式增长并可达到强耦合。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及 BFSS 类矩阵量子力学和全息相，与矩阵模型/量子引力支线相关；但其核心是冷原子模拟方案，而非对偶字典、具体全息模型求解或用户重点标签中的超流、探针膜、QNM 等方向。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

谁书写引力热力学第二定律？

Who Writes the Gravitational Second Law of Thermodynamics?

V. R. Shajiee, M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari, V. Taghiloo · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文将引力熵定义为与局部横向洛伦兹助推相关的、显式可积的面电荷，从而完全绕开对时空微分同胚的传统依赖。任何熵概念都必须满足第二定律。为回答标题中的问题，文章借助与牛顿经典力学的类比：牛顿第二运动定律由第一定律定义的惯性观察者书写。作者表明，引力热力学的第二定律由自由落体观察者书写，其路径参数化使测地线的非仿射度等于其速度矢量场的膨胀。随后，作者通过研究这类协变定义的因果自由落体观察者所看到的熵变，给出了局部第二定律的证明，并表明只要物质部分沿观察者路径满足积分强能量条件，熵变就严格非减。

English: We define gravitational entropy as the manifestly integrable surface charge associated with local transverse Lorentz boosts, entirely bypassing the conventional reliance on spacetime diffeomorphisms. Any notion of entropy must satisfy the second law. To answer the question posed in the title, an analogy with Newtonian classical mechanics is instructive: Newton's second law of motion is written by inertial observers who are defined by the first law of mechanics. We show that the second law of gravitational thermodynamics is written by free-fall observers with path parameterization in which the non-affinity of the geodesics is equal to the expansion of their velocity vector field. We then provide a proof of the local second law by studying variations in entropy as viewed by this class of covariantly-defined causal free-fall observers. We show that the entropy variation is strictly non-decreasing, provided the matter sector satisfies the integrated strong energy condition along the observer path.

一句话导读：该文把引力熵刻画为局部横向洛伦兹助推对应的可积面电荷，并证明在自由落体观察者视角下、物质满足积分强能量条件时局部第二定律成立。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典引力中的引力熵与局部热力学第二定律，属于用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”支线；未涉及全息对偶或量子引力。

物质耦合 F(4) 超引力中缠绕拓扑圆盘的 D4 膜

D4-branes wrapped on topological disks from matter-coupled F(4) gauged supergravity

Patharadanai Nuchino, Parinya Karndumri · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文研究物质耦合 F(4) 超引力中的若干超对称 AdS_4×Σ 解，其中 Σ 是边界上具有非平凡 U(1) holonomy 的拓扑圆盘或“半纺锤”。该超引力耦合三个矢量多重态，规范群为 SO(3)×SO(3)。所得解保持八个超荷以及 SO(2)×SO(2) 或 SO(2)_diag 对称性，并预期对偶于由五维 N=2 SCFT 在半纺锤上紧化产生的三维 N=2 SCFT。所有解都位于可嵌入 massive type IIA 理论的 U(1)×U(1) 子扇区。提升到十维后，这些解可解释为缠绕在 Σ 上的 D4-D8 膜系统。部分解渐近于局部 AdS_6 几何，可识别为五维 N=2 SCFT 中的余维二共形缺陷；在这些解中，Σ 内的圆在 AdS_6 极限下解紧化，使全息自由能发散。也存在对偶三维 SCFT 中自由能有限的解。作者还表明，这些结果既扩展了已知解，也提供了一类新解。

English: We study a number of supersymmetric $AdS_4\times \Sigma$ solutions with $\Sigma$ being a topological disk with non-trivial $U(1)$ holonomy on the boundary or a ``half-spindle'' from matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity. The gauged supergravity is coupled to three vector multiplets with $SO(3)\times SO(3)$ gauge group. The resulting solutions preserve eight supercharges and $SO(2)\times SO(2)$ or $SO(2)_{\text{diag}}$ symmetries and are expected to be dual to $N=2$ SCFTs in three dimensions arising from compactifications of five-dimensional $N=2$ SCFT on a half-spindle. All of the solutions lie within the $U(1)\times U(1)$ subsector of the matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity that can be embedded in massive type IIA theory. After uplifted to ten dimensions, the solutions can be interpreted as a system of D4-D8-branes wrapped on $\Sigma$. Some of the solutions are asymptotic to a locally $AdS_6$ geometry and can be identified as codimension-2 conformal defects within the $N=2$ SCFT in five dimensions. For these solutions, the circle inside $\Sigma$ decompactifies in the $AdS_6$ limit rendering the holographic free energy infinite. There also exist solutions with finite free energy in the dual three-dimensional SCFTs. In addition, we show that the results both extend the previously known solutions and provide a novel class of solutions.

一句话导读：在物质耦合 F(4) 超引力中构造 AdS4×拓扑圆盘/半纺锤超对称解，并讨论其全息对偶、D4-D8 膜解释以及缺陷与自由能性质。

相关度：low — 论文属于 AdS/CFT 超引力解与 D4-D8 膜构造，不直接涉及用户重点列出的全息超流超导、p波/多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、multi-trace、相变与气泡成核、QNM、冷原子或 D3-D7/D3-D5 探针膜与 Floquet 驱动等方向。

引力有效场论中基于Magnusian矩阵的引力法拉第旋转、引力自旋霍尔效应与自旋精化因果性分析

Gravitational Faraday rotation, gravitational spin Hall effect, and spin-refined causality analysis from Magnusian matrix in effective field theories of gravity

Kibok Jeong, Jung-Wook Kim, Soochang Lee · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文在几何光学近似下，通过黑洞背景上的波散射可观测量，探讨黑洞自旋在引力有效场论（EFT）中的作用。所考虑的可观测量包括偏振旋转角、波数踢（或偏折角）以及（Shapiro/Wigner-Smith）时间延迟，它们分别关联于引力法拉第旋转、引力自旋霍尔效应和红外因果性。这些可观测量由散射振幅经Magnusian形式计算，其中Magnusian被提升为矩阵以纳入螺旋度信息。结果显示：(1) 由于“非对易”的波数踢，引力EFT中的引力自旋霍尔效应与广义相对论情形有定性不同；(2) 黑洞自旋会略微增强对EFT系数的因果性约束。

English: The effects of black hole's spin in effective field theories (EFTs) of gravity are explored through observables of wave scattering on black hole backgrounds in the geometric optics approximation. The considered observables are polarisation rotation angle, wavenumber kick (or deflection angle), and (Shapiro/Wigner--Smith) time delay, each of which are related to gravitational Faraday rotation, gravitational spin Hall effect, and infrared causality. The observables are computed from scattering amplitudes through the Magnusian formalism, where the Magnusian is promoted to a matrix to account for helicity information. It is found that (1) the gravitational spin Hall effect in EFTs of gravity are qualitatively different from that of general relativity due to "noncommutative" wavenumber kicks, and (2) black hole's spin slightly enhances the causality constraints on EFT coefficients.

一句话导读：利用几何光学下的黑洞波散射与Magnusian矩阵形式，研究引力EFT中的自旋相关偏振、偏折和时间延迟及因果性约束。

相关度：medium — 主题属于经典引力/黑洞散射与有效场论引力，涉及几何光学和自旋效应，与经典引力支线相关；不涉及全息或用户列出的全息凝聚态、超流超导等标签。

IIB 型理论中的 R7-膜与 AdS₉ 解

R7-branes and AdS$_{\text{9}}$ solutions in type IIB

Giuseppe Dibitetto, Cameron Gibson, Nicol\`o Petri, Colin Sterckx · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们考虑最近在 IIB 型超引力中发现的 AdS₉ 背景。首先用更方便的坐标重新审视这些解，从而显式计算全息中心荷等若干相关观测量。随后研究由 IIB 轴-伸缩子源生的非超对称 7-膜位形，找到以这些 AdS₉ 背景为近地平几何的显式解析 7-膜解。最后，通过在横截平面施加反射单值性 τ→−τ̄，讨论这些 7-膜解与 R7-膜的关系。

English: We consider the recently found $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds in type IIB supergravity. We start by reviewing the solutions in a more convenient set of coordinates, which allows us to explicitly compute a few relevant observables, including the holographic central charge. Subsequently, we study non-supersymmetric 7-brane configurations sourced by the IIB axio-dilaton. We find explicit analytic 7-brane solutions that possess the above $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds as their near-horizon geometry. Finally, we discuss the relation of these 7-brane solutions to the R7 branes by imposing the reflection monodromy $\tau \rightarrow -\bar \tau$ in the transverse plane.

一句话导读：在 IIB 超引力中整理 AdS₉ 背景并构造以之为近地平几何的非超对称 7-膜解，讨论其与 R7-膜及反射单值性的关系。

相关度：low — 论文研究 IIB 超引力中的 AdS₉ 背景、7-膜解与全息中心荷，属于 AdS/CFT 与弦论背景构造；不涉及用户重点的全息超流/超导、p波、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、相变气泡成核、QNM、冷原子或 D3-D7/Floquet 等支线。

从弦Carroll几何中涌现洛伦兹几何

Emergence of Lorentzian geometry from string Carroll geometry

Anirudhda Shinde, Mangesh Mandlik · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：非极端黑洞近视界区域常由具有两个零方向的Carroll几何（弦Carroll几何）描述。本文研究四维Reissner-Nordström黑洞趋近极端时，弦Carroll几何如何演化为洛伦兹喉。作者识别出非极端描述中受抑制、但恢复AdS2曲率所需的二次时间项；同时趋近外视界与极端极限，缩放视界间距和径向距离并重标度Killing时间，可将该贡献带入领头纵向度规并与Rindler扇区并列。在ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein坐标下，仅弦Carroll度规本身也具有纵向扇区为平直的洛伦兹极限；二次时间项来自领头与次领头纵向余标架场的双线性，提供缺失曲率，而更高径向贡献消失。重构的极限几何在有限正缩放视界间距时已局部为AdS2×S2；令该参数为零可使所选视界极端而不改变局部曲率。极限度规与Maxwell场满足Einstein-Maxwell方程。在静态坐标中，时间分量同样来自二次项，而径向分量需对所有径向阶求和。该重构为将基于非极端弦Carroll描述的计算推广到喉区时需保留哪些展开数据提供几何起点，并涉及对弦、粒子和场动力学的意义。

English: The near horizon region of a generic non extremal black hole has been extensively studied in terms of a Carrollian geometry with two null directions, also called a string Carroll geometry. We investigate how this string Carroll geometry evolves to a Lorentzian throat as the four dimensional Reissner-Nordstr\"om (RN) black hole approaches extremality. We identify the quadratic temporal contribution that is suppressed in the non extremal description but is required to recover the $\mathrm{AdS}_2$ curvature. Approaching the outer horizon and extremality simultaneously, scaling the horizon separation together with the radial distance and correspondingly rescaling Killing time, brings this contribution into the leading longitudinal metric alongside the Rindler sector. In ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates, the string Carroll metric alone has a Lorentzian limit with a flat longitudinal sector. The quadratic temporal term arises from the bilinear of the leading and subleading longitudinal coframe fields and supplies the missing curvature, while higher radial contributions vanish. The reconstructed limiting geometry is already locally $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^2$ at finite positive scaled horizon separation; setting this parameter to zero makes the selected horizon extremal without changing the local curvature. The limiting metric and Maxwell field satisfy the Einstein-Maxwell equations. In static coordinates, the temporal component again follows from the quadratic term, while the radial component requires summing contributions from all radial orders. The reconstruction provides a geometric starting point for investigating which expansion data must be retained when extending calculations based on the non extremal string Carroll description into the throat regime, including their implications for string, particle and field dynamics.

一句话导读：四维RN黑洞趋近极端时，弦Carroll近视界几何通过二次时间项重构出AdS2×S2洛伦兹喉。

相关度：medium — 论文主要属于Carroll几何与黑洞近视界的经典引力研究，涉及AdS2喉重构；与用户的经典引力支线相关，但摘要未涉及全息超流、探针膜、QNM等具体重点标签，也未明确讨论全息对偶。

CGHS 模型中的渐近代数与信息全息

Asymptotic Algebras and Holography of Information in CGHS Model

Waheed A. Dar, Nirmalya Kajuri, Rinkesh Panigrahi · hep-th · v4 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：信息全息（HoI）与岛公式是处理黑洞信息悖论的两种近期方案，给出不同 Page 曲线；差异被认为源于各自关注的代数。本文研究 CGHS 模型的候选代数。作者构造引力完成，并在类似此前 HoI 文献的假设下，通过构造未来零无穷的辐射相空间和渐近代数，对耦合无质量标量的 CGHS 模型建立 HoI。证明右行模的量子态可从右未来零无穷过去端点的任意小邻域恢复。随后论证岛重建与左右辐射代数的精确对易不相容。讨论 CGHS 模型中 HoI 与岛方案的可能区别。

English: Holography of Information (HoI) and the island formula are two recent approaches to the black hole information loss paradox that yield different Page curves. The difference between the Page curves has been argued to be rooted in the algebra that each approach focuses on. In this paper, we study the candidate algebras for the CGHS model. For the gravitational completion constructed here and under assumptions analogous to those made in previous HoI literature, we establish HoI for the CGHS model coupled to a massless scalar by constructing the radiative phase space and asymptotic algebras at future null infinity. We prove that the quantum state of the right-moving modes can be recovered from an arbitrarily small neighbourhood of the past endpoint of the right future null infinity. We then argue that island reconstruction is incompatible with exact commutativity of the left and right radiative algebras. We discuss the possible distinction between the HoI and island approaches for the CGHS model.

一句话导读：在 CGHS 模型中构造未来零无穷渐近代数并建立信息全息，同时论证岛重建与左右辐射代数精确对易不相容。

相关度：low — 论文属于黑洞信息悖论与全息信息方向，未涉及用户重点标签中的全息超流超导、探针膜、QNM 等具体支线。

通过砖墙模型研究有限 N 黑洞

Finite N Black Holes through the Brick Wall

Seiji Terashima · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：在有限 N 的 AdS/CFT 中，黑洞由具有有限熵和离散谱的普通量子系统描述，而半经典体有效场论把近视界区域视为理想连续体。我们重新考虑砖墙模型，将其作为有限 N 对半经典近视界连续体偏离的简单有效描述。Dirichlet 墙是反射的；但一旦受调控的近墙模式被替换为相互作用的拉伸视界自由度，外部简单扇区可表现为吸收性并类似黑洞。若这些自由度处于热布居而外部入射模式处于真空，它们像普通热物质一样辐射，从而给出霍金辐射。我们还表明有限 N 阻止近视界扇区成为理想连续吸收体；砖墙模型是这一限制的简单有效表示，其中完美吸收的失败表现为残余反射或回声。这类返回效应出现在回声时标 κ^{-1} log S_BH 上，预计不会以 e^{-S_BH} 被压低，尽管其具体振幅依赖模型。我们还讨论了砖墙类模型，其中有效内边界由规则物质核动力学产生，而非由强加边界条件产生。

English: In AdS/CFT at finite $N$, black holes are described by ordinary quantum systems with finite entropy and discrete spectra, while semiclassical bulk effective field theory treats the near-horizon region as an ideal continuum. We reconsider the brick-wall model as a simple effective description of the finite $N$ departure from the semiclassical near-horizon continuum in AdS/CFT. A Dirichlet wall is reflecting; however, once the regulated near-wall modes are replaced by interacting stretched-horizon degrees of freedom, the exterior simple sector can look absorptive and black-hole-like. If these degrees of freedom are thermally populated while the exterior incoming modes are in their vacuum, they radiate as ordinary hot matter, and this gives Hawking radiation. Furthermore, we show that finite $N$ prevents the near-horizon sector from being an ideal continuum absorber; the brick-wall model is a simple effective representation of this limitation, where the failure of perfect absorption appears as residual reflection or echoes. Such return effects appear on the echo time scale $\kappa^{-1}\log S_{\rm BH}$ and are not expected to be suppressed as $e^{-S_{\rm BH}}$, although their detailed amplitude is model-dependent. We also discuss brick-wall-like models in which the effective inner boundary arises dynamically from a regular matter core rather than from an imposed boundary condition.

一句话导读：用砖墙模型刻画有限 N AdS/CFT 黑洞近视界连续体的失效，并讨论霍金辐射与回声。

相关度：medium — 论文属于 AdS/CFT 中有限 N 黑洞与近视界量子物理，涉及霍金辐射和回声；与用户列出的全息黑洞/量子信息主题相关，但未直接落在多数重点支线标签上。

模 Krylov 复杂度作为面积算符与纠缠岛的边界探针

Modular Krylov Complexity as a Boundary Probe of Area Operators and Entanglement Islands

Niloofar Vardian · hep-th, cond-mat.stat-mech, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们证明，量子极值面的面积算符可以直接从边界动力学重构，而不依赖体几何。我们的方法结合了 AdS/CFT 的算符代数量子纠错（OAQEC）结构与模 Krylov 复杂度。利用边界模动力学的 Lanczos 系数，我们提取限制在纠缠楔代数上的模哈密顿量谱，并分离其中心贡献，将其识别为面积算符。该构造纯基于边界，并且适用于半经典几何的叠加。作为一个前瞻性应用，我们讨论蒸发黑洞中岛的形成与 Page 转变，使用边界模演化绕过任何体极值化。更广泛地说，我们的结果确立模 Krylov 复杂度作为涌现时空几何的探针，为从边界量子动力学访问黑洞内部提供了一条新途径。

English: We show that the area operator of a quantum extremal surface can be reconstructed directly from boundary dynamics without reference to bulk geometry. Our approach combines the operator-algebra quantum error-correction (OAQEC) structure of AdS/CFT with modular Krylov complexity. Using Lanczos coefficients of boundary modular dynamics, we extract the spectrum of the modular Hamiltonian restricted to the algebra of the entanglement wedge and isolate its central contribution, which is identified with the area operator. The construction is purely boundary-based and applies to superpositions of semiclassical geometries as well. As a prospective application, we discuss island formation and the Page transition in evaporating black holes using boundary modular evolution, bypassing any bulk extremization. More broadly, our results establish modular Krylov complexity as a probe of emergent spacetime geometry, providing a new route to accessing black hole interiors from boundary quantum dynamics.

一句话导读：用边界模 Krylov 复杂度从边界动力学重构量子极值面面积算符，并讨论纠缠岛与 Page 转变。

相关度：medium — 论文属于 AdS/CFT 与量子信息/黑洞信息交叉方向，涉及纠缠楔、面积算符和纠缠岛；但与用户列出的全息超流超导、p波、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、QNM、D3-D7/Floquet、经典引力等重点支线没有直接对应。

多分体纠缠信号的时间演化

Time Evolution of Multi-Party Entanglement Signals

Vijay Balasubramanian, Hanzhi Jiang, Simon F. Ross · hep-th, cond-mat.stat-mech, cond-mat.str-el, quant-ph · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们研究混沌量子多体系统中多分体纠缠信号的实时动力学，这些系统包括但不限于全息共形场论。我们发现扰乱动力学产生具有丰富结构的多分体纠缠，包括：(a) 不同信号具有定性不同的动力学行为，可能反映不同纠缠模式的不同动力学；(b) 不连续性，表明纠缠结构中的动力学相变；(c) 瞬态且非单调的多分体纠缠；(d) 某些区域的广延纠缠完全为多分体纠缠的时段。我们的主要技术工具是纠缠动力学的膜理论。

English: We study the real-time dynamics of multi-party entanglement signals in chaotic quantum many-body systems including but not necessarily restricted to holographic conformal field theories. We find that scrambling dynamics generates multiparty entanglement with rich structure including: (a) qualitatively different dynamical behaviours for different signals, likely reflecting different dynamics for different kinds of entanglement patterns, (b) discontinuities indicating dynamical phase transitions in the entanglement structure, (c) transient and non-monotonic multiparty entanglement, and (d) periods during which the extensive entanglement of some regions is entirely multipartite. Our main technical tool is the membrane theory of entanglement dynamics.

一句话导读：利用膜理论研究混沌量子多体中多分体纠缠信号的实时演化，发现动力学相变和非单调瞬态结构。

相关度：medium — 论文属于全息/量子信息方向的纠缠动力学研究，涉及全息CFT与膜理论，但未直接对应用户列出的全息超流超导、探针膜、经典引力等重点支线。

Chord 图的接触图

Contact diagrams for chords

Yiyang Jia · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们展示接触 chord 图如何从某些 Fock 空间模型中出现，并使用 chord 路径积分技术计算相应的关联函数。特别地，我们的三点函数具有共形对称性所要求的形式，且部分四点函数与某些 AdS2 接触 Witten 图的结果一致。

English: We demonstrate how contact chord diagrams can arise from certain Fock-space models and compute the corresponding correlation functions using the chord path integral technique. In particular, our three-point functions are in the right form dictated by conformal symmetry, and some of our four-point functions match the results of some AdS$_2$ contact Witten diagrams.

一句话导读：展示 Fock 空间模型产生接触 chord 图，并用 chord 路径积分计算关联函数；三点函数符合共形对称性，部分四点函数匹配 AdS2 接触 Witten 图。

二维 CFT 中的纠缠膜：反射熵、RG 流与信息速度

The entanglement membrane in 2d CFT: reflected entropy, RG flow, and information velocity

Hanzhi Jiang, M\'ark Mezei, Julio Virrueta · hep-th, cond-mat.stat-mech, cond-mat.str-el · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：一般混沌多体系统中纠缠熵的时间演化可用最小膜有效描述，并由张力函数刻画。对于二维 CFT，退化的张力函数能复现若干熵动力学结果，这与高维中非退化张力的情形形成对比。本文利用全息方法表明，要在二维 CFT 中正确捕捉反射熵，需在膜描述中增加一个额外自由度。进一步地，作者证明在引入 CFT 的相关形变后，会出现传统的非退化膜张力函数；该形变对偶于带标量毛的平面 BTZ 黑洞，并具有内区 Kasner 宇宙。最后，本文还研究了高维中反射熵与信息速度的膜描述。

English: The time evolution of entanglement entropy in generic chaotic many-body systems has an effective description in terms of a minimal membrane, characterised by a tension function. For 2d CFTs, a degenerate tension function reproduces several results regarding the dynamics of the entropy; this stands in contrast to higher dimensions where the tension is non-degenerate. In this paper we use holography to show that, in order to correctly capture the reflected entropy in 2d CFT, one needs to add an additional degree of freedom to the membrane description. Furthermore, we show that the conventional non-degenerate membrane tension function emerges upon introducing a relevant deformation of the CFT, dual to a planar BTZ black hole with scalar hair and with an interior Kasner universe. Finally, we also study the membrane description for reflected entropy and information velocity arXiv:1908.06993 in higher dimensions.

一句话导读：用全息方法修正二维 CFT 的纠缠膜描述以纳入反射熵，并研究 RG 流与高维信息速度。

相关度：medium — 论文属于全息纠缠、反射熵与信息速度方向，和 AdS/CFT 及量子信息相关；但未直接涉及用户列出的全息超流超导、p波、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、相变与气泡成核、QNM、冷原子或探针膜等具体重点支线。

关于天球 CFT 与 AdS3 弦论的评论

Comments on Celestial CFT and $AdS_{3}$ String Theory

Igor Mol · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文评述并扩展 Ogawa 等人（2024）基于 H3+ WZNW 模型提出的天球共形场论模型。作者展示，在解析延拓到超双曲 Klein 空间 R2^2 后，该模型可由全息方式生成胶子与引力子的树级 MHV 散射振幅，从而为天球 Liouville 理论提供替代方案。文中构建了全息字典，将天球 CFT 中的顶点算符和共形原初场对应到欧氏 AdS3（玻色）弦论的世界面对象；在该字典下导出天球能动张量，计算两点与三点函数，并确定天球算符乘积展开。作者还利用 Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov 方程与世界面 Ward 恒等式，导出刻画该模型天球振幅的偏微分方程组。附录简要介绍 H3+ WZNW 模型，并强调其与欧氏 AdS3 弦论的联系。

English: In a recent work, Ogawa et al. (2024) proposed a model for celestial conformal field theory (CFT) based on the $H_{3}^{+}$-Wess-Zumino-Novikov-Witten (WZNW) model. In this paper, we extend the model advanced by Ogawa et al. (2024), demonstrating how it can holographically generate tree-level MHV scattering amplitudes for both gluons and gravitons when analytically continued to the ultra-hyperbolic Klein space $\mathbf{R}_{2}^{2}$, thereby offering an alternative to celestial Liouville theory. We construct a holographic dictionary in which vertex operators and conformal primaries in celestial CFT are derived from their worldsheet counterparts in Euclidean $AdS_{3}$ (bosonic) string theory. Within this dictionary, we derive the celestial stress-energy tensor, compute the two- and three-point functions, and determine the celestial operator product expansion (OPE). Additionally, we derive a system of partial differential equations that characterises the celestial amplitudes of our model, utilising the Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov (KZ) equations and worldsheet Ward identities. In the Appendix, we provide a concise introduction to the $H_{3}^{+}$-WZNW model, with emphasis on its connection to Euclidean $AdS_{3}$ string theory.

一句话导读：扩展 H3+-WZNW 天球 CFT，并通过欧氏 AdS3 弦论全息字典生成胶子与引力子的树级 MHV 天球振幅。

相关度：medium — 论文直接涉及天球 CFT、AdS3 弦论与全息字典，属于 dS/天球及 AdS/CFT 基础方向；但未涉及用户重点标签所列的全息超流超导、探针膜、QNM、相变成核等具体支线。

6维大荷与2维Virasoro块

6d Large Charge and 2d Virasoro Blocks

Jonathan J. Heckman, Adar Sharon, Masataka Watanabe · hep-th · v4 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们计算了相互作用的rank-one 6维 N=(2,0) SCFT 在大R荷下的可观测量。重点关注涉及 Φ^n 的关联函数，即包含应力张量超多重态底分量的对称乘积。利用模空间有效作用量和大荷展开方法，我们计算了OPE系数 ⟨Φ^nΦ^mΦ^{n+m}⟩ 的1/n展开。该展开的系数从6维角度只能部分确定，但我们利用6d/2d对应，通过数值方法逐阶固定它们。这是因为该6维可观测量可从真空Virasoro块的特定双标度极限在2维提取，而该块可被高效数值计算。我们还将计算推广到更高阶SCFT，并讨论结果在6维和2维中的应用。

English: We compute observables in the interacting rank-one 6d $\mathcal{N}=(2,0)$ SCFT at large R-charge. We focus on correlators involving $\Phi^n$, namely symmetric products of the bottom component of the supermultiplet containing the stress-tensor. By using the moduli space effective action and methods from the large-charge expansion, we compute the OPE coefficients $\langle\Phi^n\Phi^m\Phi^{n+m}\rangle$ in an expansion in 1/n. The coefficients of the expansion are only partially determined from the 6d perspective, but we manage to fix them order-by-order in 1/n numerically by utilizing the 6d/2d correspondence. This is made possible by the fact that this $6d$ observable can be extracted in 2d from a specific double-scaling limit of the vacuum Virasoro block, which can be efficiently computed numerically. We also extend the computation to higher-rank SCFTs, and discuss various applications of our results to 6d as well as 2d.

一句话导读：利用6d/2d对应和真空Virasoro块数值计算6维N=(2,0) SCFT大R荷下的OPE系数。

相关度：low — 论文主题为6维SCFT大荷展开与2维Virasoro块，不涉及用户重点的全息超流/超导、p波与多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、QNM、D3-D7探针膜或经典引力。

从跑动耦合研究全息RG流

Holographic RG Flows from Running Couplings

Chanyong Park, Jung Hun Lee · hep-th · v4 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们在全息框架下研究RG流，考察相关标量算符变形二维共形场论时在IR固定点出现的新物理。对偶引力侧从UV到IR CFT的RG流由标量场从不稳定平衡点滚向稳定平衡点描述。为更显式体现能标依赖，我们通过求解beta函数研究跑动耦合常数。采用简单标量势后，研究耦合常数变化及其沿RG流对基态的影响。结果表明中心荷沿RG流单调减小，其IR固定点值由变形算符的共形维数和IR固定点耦合常数决定。我们进一步在探针极限下研究基态改变如何影响局部算符的两点函数和共形维数。

English: We investigate the RG flow in the holographic setup and study new IR physics at the IR fixed point, when a relevant scalar operator deforms a two-dimensional conformal field theory (CFT). On the dual gravity side, the renormalization group (RG) flow from the UV to the IR CFT can be described by a scalar field rolling from an unstable to a stable equilibrium point. Specifically, to make the scale dependence of the RG flow more explicit, we study the running coupling constant obtained by solving the beta function. After considering a simple scalar potential, we study the change of the coupling constant and its effect on the ground state along the RG flow. We showed that the central charge monotonically decreases along the RG flow, and its value at the IR fixed point is determined by the conformal dimension of the deformation operator and the coupling constant at the IR fixed point. We further investigate how changing the ground state influences the two-point function and conformal dimension of a local operator in the probe limit.

一句话导读：研究二维CFT相关变形对偶引力中的全息RG流，并分析中心荷与探针两点函数随IR固定点的变化。

相关度：medium — 属于全息RG流与AdS/CFT字典的基础研究，但未直接涉及用户列出的全息超流超导、探针膜、QNM与非线性演化等具体重点支线。

非均匀淬火与 Floquet CFT 的新视界

New horizons for inhomogeneous quenches and Floquet CFT

Hanzhi Jiang, M\'ark Mezei · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：研究二维 CFT 在由共形对称生成元构成的哈密顿量驱动下的非平衡动力学，包括单次非均匀淬火和周期 Floquet 驱动。利用二维几何描述量子演化，并考察其在三维全息对偶时空中的体现，发现新视界；作者论证新视界后的体算符可借助模流重构。

English: A fruitful avenue in investigating out-of-equilibrium quantum many-body systems is to abruptly change their Hamiltonian and study the subsequent evolution of their quantum state. If this is done once, the setup is called a quench, while if it is done periodically, it is called Floquet driving. We consider the solvable setup of a two-dimensional CFT driven by Hamiltonians built out of conformal symmetry generators: in this case, the quantum dynamics can be understood using two-dimensional geometry. We investigate how the dynamics is reflected in the holographic dual three-dimensional spacetime and find new horizons. We argue that bulk operators behind the new horizons are reconstructable by virtue of modular flow.

一句话导读：用二维 CFT 几何与三维全息对偶研究非均匀淬火/Floquet 驱动，发现新视界并讨论模流重构。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究驱动二维 CFT 的非平衡动力学及其三维全息对偶，涉及新视界与模流重构，属于全息非平衡与 AdS/CFT 字典方向；未以经典广义相对论为主要对象。

D3-D7/D3-D5探针膜与Floquet驱动畴壁/孤子/涡旋/非均匀

一阶自力阶的精确静态两体引力势

Exact static two-body gravitational potential at first self-force order

Ricardo Monteiro · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：广义相对论中两个非自旋点质量的静态相互作用势此前已通过有效场论方法计算到七阶后牛顿阶。其探测极限，即零阶自力贡献，由 Schwarzschild 几何精确确定。我们推导了静态一阶自力修正的闭式表达式，该修正对轻质量是二次的，并建立了其到所有阶的弱场展开。在谐和规范下，我们构造了围绕 Schwarzschild 背景的耦合线性化引力方程所需的 Green 函数。我们通过匹配到维度延拓的点源理论，固定其齐次贡献与正则化重合极限。结果复现已知微扰系数，并确定了该质量扇区中所有更高阶静态后牛顿系数。

English: The static interaction potential of two non-spinning point masses in general relativity was previously computed through seventh post-Newtonian order using methods of effective field theory. Its probe limit, or zeroth-self-force contribution, is determined exactly by the Schwarzschild geometry. We derive a closed-form expression for the static first-self-force correction, quadratic in the light mass, and establish its weak-field expansion to all orders. Working in harmonic gauge, we construct the required Green function for the coupled linearised gravitational equations about the Schwarzschild background. We fix its homogeneous contributions and regularised coincident limit by matching to the dimensionally continued point-source theory. The result reproduces known perturbative coefficients and determines all higher static post-Newtonian coefficients in this mass sector.

一句话导读：在 Schwarzschild 背景下构造线性化引力 Green 函数，给出两体静态一阶自力势的闭式表达式及全阶弱场展开。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究广义相对论框架下的两体静态引力势与一阶自力修正，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及全息或量子引力。

Bumblebee 引力中的精确正则平面黑洞解

Exact Regular Planar Black Holes solutions in Bumblebee gravity

Daniil Stepanenko · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们讨论了在 bumblebee 引力中寻找精确正则平面黑洞解的可能性，其中 bumblebee 场包含时间分量和径向分量。我们分别考虑二次势和线性势，宇宙学常数分别取零和非零。场方程确定了非最小耦合以及模型参数之间的关系。所得解产生具有平面形变函数的正则平面黑洞。我们找到了这些函数的解析表达式。这些解在 r=0 处无奇点。

English: We discuss possibilities of finding exact regular planar black hole solutions in bumblebee gravity with temporal and radial components of the bumblebee field. We consider quadratic and linear potentials, with vanishing and nonzero cosmological constants, respectively. The field equations determine the nonminimal coupling and the relations between the model parameters. Found solution produce regular planar black hole with planar deformation function. Analytical expressions for this functions has been found. This solutions free of singularities at the $r=0$.

一句话导读：在 bumblebee 引力中构造含时间与径向分量的精确正则平面黑洞解，并给出形变函数的解析形式。

相关度：medium — 论文研究修改引力中的经典平面黑洞精确解，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；未涉及 AdS/CFT、全息字典或量子信息内容。

一个新的 toric ALF 引力瞬子

A new toric ALF gravitational instanton

Alfie Jones · gr-qc, hep-th, math.DG · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：作者构造了一个新的显式 toric ALF 引力瞬子。该瞬子欧拉示性数为 χ=4，Hirzebruch 符号差为 τ=1，无穷远处拓扑为透镜空间 L(3,1)。解依赖于一个参数，由 Belinski-Zakharov 四孤子解构造。作者证明该瞬子在轴上及轴外均光滑且正定。这是首个非 Hermitian ALF（非 AF）引力瞬子的例子。

English: We construct a new explicit toric ALF gravitational instanton. The instanton has Euler characteristic $\chi = 4$ and Hirzebruch signature $\tau = 1$. The topology at infinity is a lens space $L(3,1)$. The solution depends on one parameter and is constructed from the Belinski--Zakharov four-soliton solution. We prove that the instanton is smooth and positive definite on and away from the axis. This is the first example of a non-Hermitian ALF (not AF) gravitational instanton.

一句话导读：构造了首个非 Hermitian 的 toric ALF 引力瞬子，并证明其光滑正定。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的显式引力瞬子构造，直接匹配用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”；不涉及全息或量子信息。

带电标量准正规模的光谱不稳定性与超辐射：使不稳定稳定

Spectral instability of charged scalar quasinormal modes and superradiance: Stabilizing the unstable

Kyriakos Destounis · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-ph, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：黑洞的光谱不稳定性已成为黑洞光谱学计划的核心议题。已知对黑洞散射势的小改动会使准正规模在复平面上发生不成比例的大迁移，但这种敏感性是否延续到通过超辐射提取能量仍不清楚。作者在 Reissner–Nordström 与 Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter 背景上研究无质量带电标量场的这一关系。一个质量标度高度很小的局域正势垒被向外移离光球，同时保持原来的辐射边界条件。在渐近平直情形中，一个准正规模分支进入超辐射频率区间但仍保持阻尼；通过寻找竞争模式，作者表明，在准正规模迁移驱动的一系列模式交叉之后，最弱阻尼的正频模式也进入该区间。然而实频放大仅受弱影响，并未出现明显的窄共振。在 de Sitter 黑洞中，该势垒可将一个最初增长的单极模式移入复平面的阻尼半平面，从而稳定某些最初模式不稳定的构型。稳定之后，峰值超辐射放大仍可高于其未扰动值并高于 100%；只有当势垒位置更远时它才降到 100% 以下。因此，一个指定的正扰动可以抑制模式增长，而不改变超辐射频率边界，也不立即抑制散射峰。

English: The spectral instability of black holes has become a central topic of the black-hole spectroscopy program. Small changes to the black-hole scattering potential are known to produce disproportionately large migrations of its quasinormal modes in the complex plane, but it is unclear whether this sensitivity carries over to energy extraction through superradiance. We study this relation for massless charged scalar fields on Reissner--Nordstr\"om and Reissner--Nordstr\"om--de Sitter backgrounds. A localized positive potential bump, small in mass-scaled height, is displaced outward from the light ring while the original radiative boundary conditions are preserved. In the asymptotically flat case, a quasinormal branch enters the superradiant frequency regime while remaining damped. By searching for competing modes, we show that the least-damped positive-frequency mode also enters this regime, after a series of mode overtakings driven by quasinormal-mode migration. The real-frequency amplification, nevertheless, is only weakly affected and develops no pronounced narrow resonance. In de Sitter black holes, the bump can move an initially growing monopole mode into the damped half of the complex plane, stabilizing selected configurations that are initially modally unstable. After stabilization, the peak superradiant amplification can remain above both its unperturbed value and a gain of $100\%$; it falls below $100\%$ only for more distant bump locations. A prescribed positive perturbation can thus suppress modal growth without changing the superradiant frequency bounds or immediately suppressing the scattering peak.

一句话导读：微小正势垒可迁移 RN/RN-dS 黑洞带电标量 QNM 并稳定部分增长模，但超辐射放大基本不受显著影响。

相关度：high — 直接研究黑洞准正规模谱不稳定性与超辐射，属于 QNM 与经典引力支线；摘要未涉及全息对偶、全息超导/超流或 AdS/CFT 字典。

复合 Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs 场论中的云状黑洞构型：解析处理

Cloudy black-hole configurations of the composed Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs field theory: Analytic treatment

Shahar Hod · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs 场论中已知存在违反无毛猜想的非阿贝尔黑洞；毛发形成的起点表现为“云状”黑洞时空，即支持空间规则线性化场构型的磁荷 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞。本文用解析技术研究该理论中黑洞-线性化场束缚态的物理与数学性质，推导出描述云状黑洞-场构型的无量纲质量-半径参数 μ≡m_w r_+ 的离散共振谱 {μ_k} 的紧凑解析公式，适用于小质量弱磁化黑洞区域 1≪r_+≪m_w^{-1}（r_+ 为中心黑洞视界半径，m_w 为 Higgs 效应导致的 Yang-Mills 场质量）。结果表明激发黑洞-场共振满足渐近的 k 无关比值 μ_{k+1}/μ_k=e^{-2π/√3}。

English: Non-Abelian black holes that violate the non-hair conjecture are known to exist in the Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs field theory. The onset of hair formation in this composed physical system is marked by the presence of `cloudy' black-hole spacetimes, magnetically charged Reissner-Nordstr\"om black holes that support spatially regular linearized field configurations. In the present paper we explore, using {\it analytical} techniques, the physical and mathematical properties of the composed black-hole-linearized-field bound-state configurations of the Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs theory. In particular, we derive a remarkably compact analytical formula for the discrete resonance spectrum $\{\mu_k\}^{k=\infty}_{k=1}$ of the dimensionless mass-radius parameter $\mu\equiv m_{\text{w}} r_+$ that characterizes the cloudy black-hole-field configurations in the regime $1\ll r_+\ll m^{-1}_{\text{w}}$ of small-mass weakly-magnetized black holes (here $r_+$ is the horizon radius of the central supporting black hole and $m_{\text{w}}$ is the mass of the Yang-Mills field due to the Higgs effect). Interestingly, our results reveal that the excited black-hole-field resonances are characterized by the asymptotic $k$-independent dimensionless ratio ${{\mu_{k+1}}/{\mu_{k}}}=e^{-{{2\pi}/{\sqrt{3}}}}$.

一句话导读：用解析方法给出 Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs 理论云状黑洞线性化束缚态的离散共振谱及其渐近比值。

相关度：medium — 论文主要研究经典 Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs 黑洞与线性化场共振，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及 AdS/CFT 或全息对偶。

短脉冲引力辐射与物质形成陷俘面的简单通量判据

A simple flux criterion for trapped surface formation by short pulses of gravitational radiation and matter

Tetsuya Shiromizu, Keisuke Izumi, Daisuke Yoshida · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文在双零坐标表述下讨论由入射引力辐射和物质场短脉冲诱导的陷俘面形成。作者识别出与演化方程一致的、按脉冲宽度组织的几何量和物质量层级；在该层级于所考虑区域成立的假设下，入射通量近似做保守的内向输运并被放大。随后Raychaudhuri方程给出陷俘面形成的初始通量判据。对物质的约束通过Ricci分量及其导数表达，因此论证无需指定具体物质模型。

English: We discuss the formation of trapped surfaces induced by short pulses of incoming gravitational radiation and matter fields, in a double-null formulation. We identify a hierarchy of orders in the pulse width for the geometric and matter quantities consistent with the evolution equations. Under the assumption that this hierarchy holds throughout the region considered, the incoming fluxes undergo approximately conservative inward transport and are thereby amplified. The Raychaudhuri equation then gives an initial flux criterion for trapped surface formation. The conditions on the matter are expressed through the Ricci components and their derivatives, which allows the argument to be developed without specifying a particular matter model.

一句话导读：在双零坐标下利用脉冲宽度层级与Raychaudhuri方程，给出短脉冲引力辐射/物质形成陷俘面的初始通量判据。

相关度：high — 论文研究短脉冲引力辐射与物质导致陷俘面形成的经典广义相对论判据，直接属于重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”。

静态真空几何编码引力坍缩与宇宙学

Static Vacuum Geometry Encodes Gravitational Collapse and Cosmology

Yi Ling, Jinsong Yang, Zhangping Yu, Cong Zhang · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们证明，在球对称的协变哈密顿引力中，给定的一参数静态真空几何族唯一地确定与无旋尘埃耦合的引力坍缩的局域动力学，尽管底层引力哈密顿量不唯一。我们进一步导出所得动力学允许均匀各向同性宇宙学扇区的充要条件。这些条件在静态真空几何与修正 Friedmann 动力学之间建立双向对应，并对后者对空间曲率的依赖施加普遍限制。我们的结果为判断独立提出的黑洞模型和宇宙学模型是否能来自同一协变引力理论提供了判据。

English: We show that, within covariant Hamiltonian gravity in spherical symmetry, a prescribed one-parameter family of static vacuum geometries uniquely determines the local dynamics of gravitational collapse coupled to irrotational dust, despite the nonuniqueness of the underlying gravitational Hamiltonian. We further derive necessary and sufficient conditions under which the resulting dynamics admits a homogeneous and isotropic cosmological sector. These conditions establish a two-way correspondence between static vacuum geometries and modified Friedmann dynamics and impose a universal restriction on the spatial-curvature dependence of the latter. Our results provide a criterion for determining whether independently proposed black-hole and cosmological models can arise from a common covariant gravitational theory.

一句话导读：球对称协变哈密顿引力中，静态真空几何可唯一决定坍缩动力学，并给出其与修正 Friedmann 宇宙学对应的判据。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/广义相对论及宇宙学；未涉及全息对偶或量子引力，但与用户重点中的经典引力支线直接相关。

用物理信息神经网络计算黑洞潮汐 Love 数

Black Hole Love Numbers with Physics-Informed Neural Networks

Maria J. Rodriguez, Luis Fernando Temoche, Pavlos Protopapas · gr-qc, cs.LG, cs.NA, hep-th, math.NA · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文提出并验证一个物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，用于直接从黑洞微扰方程计算潮汐 Love 数。作者考虑 Kerr 黑洞的静态微扰，其精确超几何解为数值求解器和物理响应提取提供受控基准。该框架将视界边界条件的精确实施与包含微分方程残差导数的 H^2-Sobolev 损失相结合，并通过学习到的解的渐近系数提取 Love 数，而不使用解析连接公式。在标量、矢量和张量微扰测试中，方法恢复了有限的耗散响应，相对误差低于 10^{-5}。受控比较表明，高阶导数损失项将解精度提高数个量级，而对提取过程的独立测试可区分求解器误差与响应系数恢复误差。解析上为零的保守响应受数值误差下限约束。结果表明 PINN 能准确计算黑洞潮汐响应，并为未来推广到频率依赖和不可分离微扰问题建立经过验证的框架。

English: We develop and validate a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for computing the tidal Love numbers of black holes directly from their perturbation equations. We consider static perturbations of the Kerr black hole, whose exact hypergeometric solutions provide a controlled benchmark for both the numerical solver and the extraction of the physical response. The framework combines exact enforcement of the horizon boundary conditions with an $H^2$-Sobolev loss that incorporates derivatives of the differential-equation residual. The Love number is then obtained by extracting the asymptotic coefficients of the learned solution, without using the analytic connection formula. Across the tested scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations, the method recovers the finite dissipative response with relative errors below $10^{-5}$. Controlled comparisons show that the higher-derivative loss terms improve solution accuracy by orders of magnitude, while independent tests of the extraction procedure distinguish solver errors from errors in recovering the response coefficients. The conservative response, which vanishes analytically, is constrained by the numerical error floor. These results demonstrate that PINNs can accurately compute black-hole tidal response and establish a validated framework for future extensions to frequency-dependent and non-separable perturbation problems.

一句话导读：该工作验证了 PINN 可直接从 Kerr 黑洞静态微扰方程高精度提取潮汐 Love 数，并建立了可扩展的数值框架。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典广义相对论中的黑洞微扰与潮汐响应计算，直接对应“经典引力/广义相对论”重点支线；不涉及全息、量子引力或量子信息。

Cuscuton 第五力：非动力学标量对结构增长的影响

Cuscuton Fifth Force: Implications of Non-Dynamical Scalars for Structure Growth

Adam Smith, Ilaria Andrei, Maria Mylova, Niayesh Afshordi · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：Cuscuton 是一种服从约束而非演化方程的标量场，因此它修改引力但不增加传播的标量自由度。本文研究有效 cuscuton 理论的宇宙学：物质在 Jordan 框架中最小耦合，从而在 Einstein 框架中与 cuscuton 共形耦合。我们推导背景和线性扰动方程，并表明尽管标量本身不传播，其代数响应仍会媒介一种第五力。同一共形因子还改变 Einstein 框架中的粒子质量以及物质扰动的摩擦项，使该力的强度和符号与背景演化相关，而不是与独立耦合相关。我们研究一类幂律模型，其中包含 tracking 解和符号切换解；在后一类解中，有效 cuscuton 密度在物质主导时期为负，而今天变为正的、类似宇宙学常数的值。使用修改版 CLASS（包含复合时期由诱导粒子质量依赖带来的效应），我们在基准模型中展示对 CMB 温度谱和线性物质功率谱的相应改变，但不拟合数据。在最强基准中，第五力使亚视界尺度上的有效引力耦合改变约百分之一，小于伴随的物质功率谱和声学尺度的变化。对于符号切换解，这一改变在大部分宇宙历史中为负，因此一个非传播场可以削弱而非增强引力吸引。

English: The cuscuton is a scalar field that obeys a constraint rather than an evolution equation, so it modifies gravity without adding a propagating scalar degree of freedom. We study the cosmology of effective cuscuton theory when matter is minimally coupled in the Jordan frame, and therefore conformally coupled to the cuscuton in the Einstein-frame. We derive the background and linear perturbation equations and show that the algebraic cuscuton response mediates a fifth force even though the scalar itself does not propagate. The same conformal factor also changes Einstein-frame particle masses and the friction of matter perturbations, tying the strength and sign of the force to the background evolution rather than to an independent coupling. We study a power-law family of models which contain both tracking solutions and sign-switching solutions; in the latter the effective cuscuton density runs from negative during matter domination to a positive, cosmological-constant-like value today. Using a modified version of CLASS, including the induced particle-mass dependence of recombination, we illustrate the corresponding changes to CMB temperature and linear matter power spectra for benchmark models, without fitting to data. In the strongest benchmarks the fifth force changes the effective gravitational coupling on sub-horizon scales by about one percent, smaller than the accompanying changes to the matter power spectrum and the acoustic scale. For the sign-switching solutions this change is negative over most of cosmic history, so a non-propagating field can weaken rather than strengthen gravitational attraction.

一句话导读：Cuscuton 非传播标量可通过代数响应产生第五力，并在特定宇宙学模型中削弱结构增长。

相关度：low — 该文属于修改引力宇宙学，研究非动力学标量、第五力和结构增长；不是全息或 AdS/CFT 研究，与用户重点的全息超流、p波、multi-trace、QNM、D3-D7 等标签无直接关联，仅与经典引力/宇宙学有弱相关。

内视界过剩熵与Kerr形成的热力学

Excess inner-horizon entropy and the thermodynamics of Kerr formation

Francisco S. N. Lobo, Jorge Ovalle, Manuel E. Rodrigues · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们为构成Kerr形成精确模型的规则旋转黑洞族建立电荷匹配的热力学框架。由于Kerr端点已有Cauchy视界，隐藏熵被认定为固定质量M和角动量J=Ma下相对于Kerr的内视界面积超出量：Sexc=π[h_c^2-(r_-^Kerr)^2]，其中h_c是规则内视界半径，r_-^Kerr=M-sqrt(M^2-a^2)。在规则性和内部剖面假设下，证明r_-^Kerr<h_c<h并建立严格界0<Sexc<4πM sqrt(M^2-a^2)。Schwarzschild极限给出Sexc=πh_c^2，而在Kerr端点Sexc=0。Kerr参考熵满足S_+^Kerr+S_-^Kerr=4πM^2，因此旋转在固定质量下重新分配两个视界的贡献。对于含时模型，推导内根和Sexc的精确演化方程，解释为根记账恒等式，除非这些根被确立为动力学视界。在最小N=1轨迹中，n=2处的表观极点在质量剖面中可移除，给出根方程的对数延拓，尽管稳态曲率在该处奇异。稳态Ricci标量密度在-1<n≤2时局部绝对可积，Kerr端点n=-1单独处理。Minkowski破坏发生在n=0，与内视界消失无关。准极端规则构型接近但不能达到熵界而不失去规则Killing视界。

English: \noindent We develop a charge-matched thermodynamic framework for the regular rotating black-hole family underlying an exact model of Kerr formation. Since the Kerr endpoint already possesses a Cauchy horizon, hidden entropy is identified with the excess inner-horizon area relative to Kerr at fixed mass $M$ and angular momentum $J=Ma$: $\Sexc=\pi[h_c^2-(r_-^{\Kerr})^2]$, where $h_c$ is the regular inner-horizon radius and $r_-^{\Kerr}=M-\sqrt{M^2-a^2}$. Under regularity and interior-profile assumptions, we prove $r_-^{\Kerr}<h_c<h$ and establish the sharp bound $0<\Sexc<4\pi M\sqrt{M^2-a^2}$. The Schwarzschild limit yields $\Sexc=\pi h_c^2$, while $\Sexc=0$ at the Kerr endpoint. The Kerr reference entropy satisfies $S_+^{\Kerr}+S_-^{\Kerr}=4\pi M^2$, so rotation redistributes its two-horizon contributions at fixed mass. For the time-dependent model, we derive exact evolution equations for the inner root and $\Sexc$, interpreted as root-bookkeeping identities unless the roots are established as dynamical horizons. In the minimal $N=1$ trajectory, the apparent pole at $n=2$ is removable in the mass profile, giving a logarithmic continuation of the root equation, although the stationary curvature is singular there. The stationary Ricci-scalar density is locally absolutely integrable for $-1<n\leq2$, with the Kerr endpoint $n=-1$ treated separately. Minkowski breaking occurs at $n=0$, independently of inner-horizon disappearance. Quasi-extremal regular configurations approach, but cannot attain, the entropy bound without losing a regular Killing horizon.

一句话导读：为Kerr形成精确模型中的规则旋转黑洞建立热力学框架，以内视界面积超出Kerr参考定义隐藏熵，并推导其严格界、演化方程及端点行为。

相关度：medium — 论文研究经典旋转黑洞形成与内视界热力学，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及全息、AdS/CFT 或量子信息。

Gödel型时空中的和乐分类、陀螺仪进动与朗道能级及其超流类比

Holonomy classification, gyroscope precession, and Landau levels in G\"odel-type spacetimes and their superfluid analog

Claudio Furtado, G. Q. Garcia, Abdullah Guvendi, Alexandre M. de M. Carvalho · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文推导了Rebouças-Tiomno均匀Gödel型族中绕对称轴圆周的平行移动和乐，该族包括Gödel宇宙、m^2=4Ω^2的因果成员和Som-Raychaudhuri极限。和乐由单一不变量Δ(r)控制，其符号决定洛伦兹变换为椭圆、抛物或双曲型。作者确定族中抛物半径数目，并证明当方位圆周成为闭合类时曲线时，时序半径满足Δ(r_c)=-cosh(mr_c)<0；还用Killing矢量标量表示该不变量，导出矢量与旋量Wilson圈，给出有限圈数后和乐平凡的精确条件，并分析圆形CTC上的Fermi-Walker输运。最后讨论与旋转超流有效度规的类比（在所述条件下成立），并给出Klein-Gordon朗道模相对于Som-Raychaudhuri时序半径的径向L^2局域化的精确表达式。几何结果在所考虑度规族内是精确的；类比系统讨论依赖粗粒化有效描述的成立。

English: We derive the parallel-transport holonomies of circles centered on the symmetry axis in the homogeneous G\"odel-type family of Rebou\c{c}as and Tiomno, which includes the G\"odel universe, the causal member with $m^2=4\Omega^2$, and the Som--Raychaudhuri limit. The holonomy is governed by a single invariant $\Delta(r)$, whose sign determines whether the Lorentz transformation is elliptic, parabolic, or hyperbolic. We determine the number of parabolic radii across the family and prove that, whenever the azimuthal circles become closed timelike curves (CTCs), the chronology radius satisfies $\Delta(r_c)=-\cosh(mr_c)<0$. We also express this invariant in terms of Killing-vector scalars, derive the vector and spinor Wilson loops, give the exact condition for trivial holonomy after a finite number of turns, and analyze Fermi--Walker transport on circular CTCs. Finally, we discuss an analogy with the effective metric of a rotating superfluid, valid under stated conditions, and derive exact expressions for the radial $L^2$ localization of Klein--Gordon Landau modes relative to the Som--Raychaudhuri chronology radius. The geometric results are exact within the metric family considered; the discussion of the analog system relies on the validity of a coarse-grained effective description.

一句话导读：对Gödel型时空的平行移动和乐做精确分类，并联系陀螺仪进动、CTC、Wilson圈及旋转超流朗道模类比。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，涉及精确时空几何、CTC和超流类比；不涉及全息对偶或量子引力。

经典引力/广义相对论冷原子/双组分BEC/凝聚态实验启发

Schwarzschild黑洞中的涨落-耗散定理与动力学Love数

The Fluctuation-Dissipation Theorem and Dynamical Love Number in Schwarzschild Black Holes

Dimitrios Giataganas, Alex Kehagias, Antonio Riotto · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文研究Schwarzschild黑洞在Hartle-Hawking态下对无质量标量场外源的实时响应。作者用复化径向围道表示Schwinger-Keldysh闭合时间路径，将耗散、热涨落和守恒潮汐响应纳入统一框架；在潮汐匹配前，对任意实频率和每个标量多极子直接从径向构造导出平衡涨落-耗散关系。低频潮汐展开中，他们重现了ω^2阶守恒动力学Love系数的对数跑动与ω阶主导耗散响应之间的已知关系，并说明同一主导吸收系数如何进入两关系，以及跑动关系的单位系数来自Schwarzschild视界斜率。在Hartle-Hawking态中，该共同系数将对数跑动与零频热噪声联系起来；KMS解析性由耗散响应确定热噪声，而近区径向方程和潮汐匹配确定守恒对数跑动。

English: We study the real-time response of a Schwarzschild black hole in the Hartle-Hawking state to an external massless scalar field. Using a complexified radial contour to represent the Schwinger-Keldysh closed time path, we describe dissipation, thermal fluctuations and conservative tidal response within a common framework. Before performing tidal matching, we derive the equilibrium fluctuation-dissipation relation directly from the radial construction for every scalar multipole at arbitrary real frequency. In the low-frequency tidal expansion, we recover the known relation between the logarithmic running of the conservative dynamical Love coefficient at order $\omega^2$ and the leading dissipative response at order $\omega$. We explain how the same leading absorptive coefficient enters both relations at low frequency and trace the unit coefficient of the running relation to the Schwarzschild horizon slope. In the Hartle-Hawking state, this common coefficient relates the logarithmic running directly to the zero-frequency thermal noise. Kubo-Martin-Schwinger analyticity determines the thermal noise from the dissipative response, whereas the near-zone radial equation and tidal matching determine the conservative logarithmic running.

一句话导读：在Schwarzschild黑洞实时响应中统一描述耗散、热涨落与动力学Love数的潮汐响应，并导出低频涨落-耗散关系。

相关度：medium — 涉及黑洞扰动、潮汐Love数与实时涨落耗散，属于经典引力/黑洞响应方向；但不以AdS/CFT全息或用户列出的超流、畴壁、multi-trace等为主线。

Ellis虫洞上非最小耦合标量场的精确标量化阈值与起始定律

Exact scalarization thresholds and onset laws for a nonminimally coupled scalar field on the Ellis wormhole

Abdullah Guvendi, Omar Mustafa, Claudio Furtado · gr-qc, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文研究固定Ellis虫洞背景上非最小耦合到曲率的实标量场。标量扰动在线性阶解耦，通过替换 x=a tan z 将静态径向方程化为三角Pöschl-Teller问题。由此得到精确阈值谱 ζ_{nℓ}=(n+ℓ+1)^2/2、闭式Gegenbauer剖面，以及每个角扇区中负径向本征值的精确计数。s波阈值是零能共振，其增长率线性上升；而 ℓ≥1 的阈值态平方可积，增长率按平方根上升。在第一个阈值处还得到下一阶项 σ a=(π/4)g-(3π/16)g^2+O(g^3)，其中 g=2ζ-1。对于带四次自相互作用的探针场，作者证明当 ζ≤1/2 时不存在非平凡球对称静态解，并推导了s波阈值附近的局部分支展开。数值演化与无节点分支上的饱和行为一致。这些非线性结果仅针对探针场模型，并未确立完全背反作用标量化虫洞的存在性或稳定性。

English: We study a real scalar field nonminimally coupled to curvature on a fixed Ellis wormhole background. The scalar perturbation decouples at linear order, and the substitution $ x=a \tan z $ reduces its static radial equation to a trigonometric P\"oschl-Teller problem. This yields the exact threshold spectrum $ \zeta_{n\ell}=(n+\ell+1)^2/2 $, closed-form Gegenbauer profiles, and an exact count of negative radial eigenvalues in each angular sector. The s-wave thresholds are zero-energy resonances at which the growth rate rises linearly, whereas the $ \ell \geq1\ $ threshold states are square integrable and the growth rate rises as a square root. At the first threshold we also obtain the next-order term, $ \sigma a=(\pi/4)g-(3\pi/16)g^2+O(g^3) $ with $g=2\zeta-1 $. For a probe field with quartic self-interaction, we show that no nontrivial spherical static solution exists for $\zeta\leq1/2 $ and derive local branch expansions near the s-wave thresholds. Numerical evolutions are consistent with saturation on the nodeless branch. These nonlinear results refer to the probe-field model; they do not establish the existence or stability of fully backreacted scalarized wormholes.

一句话导读：在固定Ellis虫洞背景上给出非最小耦合标量场标量化阈值的精确谱、起始律及探针场非线性分支结果。

相关度：medium — 论文研究固定Ellis虫洞背景上的非最小耦合标量场标量化阈值，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及AdS/CFT或全息字典。

全息 Rényi 缺陷上共形性丧失引发的对称性破缺

Symmetry breaking from conformality loss on holographic R\'enyi defects

Siqing Yu · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文通过共形缺陷（Rényi 缺陷）计算球的 Rényi 熵，该缺陷在全息对偶中对应双曲黑洞。在指标 n_* 处，其带电算符与替代量子化伙伴合并，缺陷失去共形性。当 n>n_* 时，缺陷无源地流向破坏 U(1) 对称性的共形缺陷；当 n<n_* 时需要对称破缺源，因此在大 N 下共形性丧失使破缺成为自发。n_* 之外的标量毛预期会破坏双曲空间对称性，但按共形对称性要求，它在纠缠面薄层之外保持这些对称性；薄层外区域决定 Rényi 熵的普适项。我们在 AdS5 的探针 Abelian Higgs 模型 Bogomolny 点展示该机制，其中鞍点有闭式解，数值方法确认了流动。在球面上，我们证明正的无源流恰在 n>n_* 且球足够大时存在，并且唯一、终止于对称破缺缺陷。该机制要求标量质量位于有限窗口；对于小振幅吸引势，对称性可在共形性丧失前破缺。对于维度足够低的算符，第二 Rényi 熵已越过合并点。经典 backreaction 在任何阶都不移动合并点；我们论证远离 n_* 时流动在一阶持续。

English: The R\'enyi entropies of a ball are computed by a conformal defect, the R\'enyi defect, dual in holography to a hyperbolic black hole. At an index $n_*$ its charged operator merges with its alternate-quantization partner, and the defect loses conformality. Beyond $n_*$ it flows with no source to a conformal defect that breaks the U(1) symmetry; below $n_*$ that defect needs a symmetry-breaking source, so at large $N$, conformality loss makes the breaking spontaneous. The scalar hair beyond $n_*$, expected to break the symmetries of hyperbolic space, thus preserves them outside a thin layer at the entangling surface, as conformal symmetry requires. The region outside the layer sets the universal term of the R\'enyi entropy. We demonstrate the mechanism in the probe Abelian Higgs model in AdS$_5$ at the Bogomolny point, where the saddles are in closed form and numerical methods confirm the flows. On a sphere we prove that a positive source-free flow exists exactly when $n>n_*$ and the sphere is large enough; it is then unique and ends at the symmetry-breaking defect. The mechanism needs a scalar mass in a finite window, and for potentials attractive at small amplitude, the symmetry can break before conformality is lost. For operators of low enough dimension, already the second R\'enyi entropy lies beyond the merger. Classical backreaction does not move the merger at any order; we argue that away from $n_*$ the flows persist at first order.

一句话导读：全息 Rényi 缺陷在 n_* 处因带电算符与替代量子化伙伴合并而丧失共形性，并在大 N 下无源地触发 U(1) 对称性自发破缺。

相关度：medium — 主要讨论全息 Rényi 缺陷与纠缠熵，并借助探针 Abelian Higgs 模型实现对称破缺；与用户列出的全息超流超导/对称破缺支线有方法上的交集，但不涉及 p 波、D-brane、QNM 或经典引力等具体重点。

平滑 AdS/CFT 中反弹测地线的奇异性

Smoothing the Bouncing-Geodesic Singularity in AdS/CFT

Mingxuan Liu, Shan-Ming Ruan · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：黑洞奇点仍是量子引力的核心开放问题。在 AdS/CFT 中，体空间类空极值面可探测黑洞内部，近奇点几何的信息编码在解析延拓的边界关联函数中。当反弹测地线接近黑洞奇点时，它变得近乎类光，并在复时间产生特征发散。重要的是，这些复时间奇点只在严格半经典极限（G_N→0 或 c→∞）出现，并由渐近线性的准正规模（QNM）谱控制。在有限 c 下，QNM 轨迹的非线性变形产生抑制核，从而平滑复时间奇点。以 Liouville CFT 作为解析可解例子，我们明确展示 QNM 谱的有限 c 变形及其相关的极点-零点结构。我们还识别出热谱分解中有限能量涨落引起的互补高斯平滑机制。这些结果表明有限 G_N 量子修正如何修改经典黑洞奇点的全息印记。

English: Black hole singularities remain a central open problem in quantum gravity. In the AdS/CFT correspondence, bulk spacelike extremal surfaces can probe the black hole interior, and information about the near-singularity geometry is encoded in analytically continued boundary correlators. As the bouncing geodesic approaches the black hole singularity, it becomes nearly null and gives rise to characteristic divergences at complex times. Importantly, these complex-time singularities emerge only in the strict semiclassical limit ($G_N\to 0$ or $c\to\infty$) and are governed by the asymptotically linear quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum. At finite $c$, nonlinear deformations of the QNM trajectories produce suppression kernel that smooths out the complex-time singularities. Using Liouville CFT as an analytically solvable example, we explicitly demonstrate the finite-$c$ deformation of the QNM spectrum and its associated pole-zero structure. We also identify a complementary Gaussian smoothing mechanism arising from finite-energy fluctuations in the thermal spectral decomposition. These results show how finite $G_N$ quantum corrections modify the holographic imprints of the classical black hole singularity.

一句话导读：利用有限 c 的 QNM 谱变形与热谱高斯平滑，展示 AdS/CFT 中反弹测地线复时间奇点如何被量子修正抹平。

相关度：medium — 论文核心是 AdS/CFT 中黑洞内部、反弹测地线与 QNM 复时间奇点的有限 c 平滑，与用户重点标签中的“QNM与非线性演化”直接相关；但不涉及全息超流超导、p波/多分量序参量、畴壁/涡旋、multi-trace、探针膜或冷原子等具体支线。

QNM与非线性演化

全息半经典 de Sitter 时空中的广义熵

The generalized entropy in holographic semiclassical de Sitter spacetime

Akihiro Ishibashi, Kengo Maeda, Takashi Okamura · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们在全息半经典引力框架下考虑广义 de Sitter 熵，其中同时包含强耦合量子场的纠缠熵与引力的 Wald 熵。通过考虑经典解的静态形变，微扰地构造量子修正的 de Sitter 时空。将 Noether 荷公式应用于该半经典模型，我们证明即使体 AdS 引力场不满足 Dirichlet 边界条件，两个重整化熵也无需引入截断即保持有限。我们还证明，当共形边界度规被提升为动力学变量时，广义熵对 de Sitter 视界的一阶变分总是为零。

English: We consider generalized de Sitter entropy which includes both entanglement entropy of the strongly coupled quantum field and the gravitational Wald entropy in the framework of holographic semiclassical gravity. The quantum corrected de Sitter spacetime is constructed perturbatively by considering static deformations of the classical solution. By applying the Noether charge formula to our semiclassical model, we show that both the renormalized entropies remain finite without introducing a cutoff, even when the bulk AdS gravitational field does not satisfy the Dirichlet boundary condition. We show that the first order variation of the generalized entropy with respect to the de Sitter horizon always vanishes when the conformal boundary metric is promoted to a dynamical variable.

一句话导读：在全息半经典 de Sitter 中给出含纠缠熵与 Wald 熵的广义熵，并证明其重整化有限及视界一阶变分消失。

相关度：medium — 论文直接涉及全息半经典 de Sitter 中的广义熵、Wald 熵与视界变分，属于 dS/全息熵基础方向；但未直接涉及用户重点的全息超流超导、p波与多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、multi-trace、气泡成核、QNM 或 D3-D7/D3-D5 探针膜等具体支线，故相关性为中等。

d≥2 中全息零态方程的矩阵视角

A Matrix View of Holographic Null-State Equations in $d\geq 2$

Kuo-Wei Huang · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们表明，最近从带黑洞的 AdS 引力导出的零态型偏微分方程，可以用具有系统非对角结构的有限维矩阵高效组织。该矩阵表述在任意层级生成无穷多的 CFT 微分方程。d=2 矩阵重现了基于大中心荷下 Virasoro 零态矢量的已知结果，并给出由单位矩阵与 sl2 生成元线性组合构成的代数表示，其中重态变形编码中心荷贡献。d=4 的主导矩阵展现出类似但更丰富的结构，涉及 sl2 生成元的乘积。我们还讨论了与这些全息微分方程相关的递推关系和因子化乘积结构。

English: We show that the null-state-type partial differential equations recently derived from AdS gravity with a black hole can be efficiently organized using finite-dimensional matrices with systematic off-diagonal structures. This matrix formulation generates an infinite tower of CFT differential equations at arbitrary levels. The $d=2$ matrix reproduces a known result based on Virasoro null-state vectors at large central charge and admits an algebraic representation in terms of a linear combination of the identity matrix and $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ generators, with a heavy-state deformation encoding the central charge contribution. The $d=4$ governing matrix exhibits a similar yet richer structure involving products of $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ generators. We also discuss recursion relations and factorized product structures associated with these holographic differential equations.

一句话导读：用有限维矩阵系统化组织由 AdS 黑洞引力导出的全息零态型 CFT 微分方程，并给出 d=2 与 d=4 的代数结构。

相关度：medium — 属于 AdS/CFT 与 CFT 零态方程的形式结构研究，和全息基础相关；但不直接落在用户列出的具体重点标签（全息超流超导、p波、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、气泡成核、QNM、冷原子、D3-D7、经典引力）上。

AdS$_4$ 上自对偶 Yang-Mills 中不存在光滑实瞬子鞍点

No smooth real instanton saddles in self-dual Yang-Mills on AdS$_4$

Bayram Tekin · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：在 Heuveline、Ruzziconi、Sheta 和 Strominger 所研究的欧几里得 AdS$_4$ 上 Yang-Mills 理论的自对偶极限中，二阶作用量的边界条件变得空洞，但一阶作用量的边界方程非平凡。我们证明，在该方程下，当两个场都光滑到共形边界时，对于任意紧致规范群，真空是唯一具有实规范场的鞍点。特别地，Sarioglu 和 Tekin (2009) 的自对偶解不是鞍点；我们还修正了他们的荷，其遗漏了共形边界处的表面项。复鞍点和奇异极限仍未解决。

English: In the self-dual limit of Yang-Mills theory on Euclidean AdS$_4$ studied by Heuveline, Ruzziconi, Sheta and Strominger, the boundary condition of the second-order action becomes vacuous, but the boundary equation of the first-order action is non-trivial. We show that, with this equation, the vacuum is the only saddle with a real gauge field when both fields are smooth up to the conformal boundary, for any compact gauge group. In particular, the self-dual solutions of Sarioglu and Tekin (2009) are not saddles; we also correct their charges, which omitted a surface term at the conformal boundary. Complex saddles and singular limits remain open.

一句话导读：证明在欧氏 AdS$_4$ 自对偶 Yang-Mills 中，光滑实规范场下真空是唯一鞍点，并修正 Sarioglu-Tekin 解的荷。

相关度：low — 论文属于 AdS/CFT 基础/边界条件与鞍点问题，但不涉及用户列出的全息超导、p波、畴壁、multi-trace、相变、QNM、探针膜或经典引力等具体研究支线。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

Narain 系综平均中对 Seifert 流形的求和

Summing over Seifert Manifolds in Narain Ensemble Averages

Kei Kaneda, Masahito Yamazaki · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：Narain 共形场论在其模空间上的系综平均是一个实解析 Eisenstein 级数，其 Poincaré 展开可解释为以 Abel Chern-Simons 理论配分函数加权的透镜空间求和。本文把这一对应从透镜空间推广到 Seifert 流形，给出三维量子引力中受限拓扑求和的一个例子。Seifert 流形由固定核心（带 puncture 的曲面乘圆）在其边界环面上粘接实心环面得到，并在每个环面上放置一份 Narain 理论。系综平均提供对实心环面的求和，更准确地说，是对固定基亏格、纤维数和整数 Seifert 不变量的带标记表示的求和；所得求和绝对收敛，并且是向量值 Eisenstein 级数分量的多项式。当所有边界环面共享单一模时，平均获得连通修正，作者将其识别为把核心与自身粘接的虫洞；对于两个边界环面，Siegel-Eisenstein 级数中具有有理 Lagrangian 子格的项由含例外纤维的虫洞实现。沿纤维约化后，该构造与 Marolf-Maxfield 的二维拓扑引力密切平行，其中纤维化的基扮演其时空的角色。文中还讨论了带环面边界的三维流形的 Dehn 填充、不可定向基以及手术公式。结果提示模性可能是量子引力中几何求和的组织原则。

English: The ensemble average of Narain conformal field theories over their moduli space is a real-analytic Eisenstein series, whose Poincar\'e expansion can be interpreted as a sum over lens spaces weighted by the partition functions of Abelian Chern-Simons theory. In this paper we extend this correspondence from lens spaces to Seifert manifolds, which gives an example of a restricted sum over topologies of the kind that arises in three-dimensional quantum gravity. A Seifert manifold is obtained from a fixed core, a punctured surface times a circle, by gluing solid tori to its boundary tori, and we place a copy of the Narain theory on each of these tori. The ensemble average supplies the sum over the solid tori, or more precisely over marked presentations with fixed base genus, number of fibers and integer Seifert invariant. The resulting sum converges absolutely and is a polynomial in the components of the vector-valued Eisenstein series. When all the boundary tori share a single modulus, the average acquires connected corrections, which we identify with wormholes gluing the core to itself; for two boundary tori, the terms with rational Lagrangian sublattices of the Siegel-Eisenstein series are realized by wormholes containing an exceptional fiber. After reduction along the fiber, the construction closely parallels the two-dimensional topological gravity of Marolf and Maxfield, with the base of the fibration playing the role of their spacetime. We also discuss Dehn fillings of 3-manifolds with torus boundary, non-orientable bases, and the surgery formula. Our results suggest that modularity could be an organizing principle in the summation of geometries in quantum gravity.

一句话导读：将 Narain CFT 系综平均的 Poincaré 展开从透镜空间推广到 Seifert 流形，并把虫洞修正与三维量子引力中的拓扑求和联系起来。

相关度：low — 论文讨论 Narain CFT 系综平均与 Seifert 流形拓扑求和、虫洞及三维量子引力，不涉及重点标签中的全息超流超导、p波与多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、multi-trace、相变与气泡成核、QNM、冷原子或 D-brane 探针膜等方向。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

自旋黑洞散射的振幅方法

An Amplitudes Approach to Spinning Black Hole Scattering

Dogan Akpinar · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：引力波探测开启了引力的精确研究新时代，要求对致密双星动力学有更深入理解。散射振幅提供了利用高能物理方法研究这些系统的强大框架。本论文研究经典黑洞散射，系统地包含自旋——真实天体物理系统的重要特征——以描述 Kerr 黑洞动力学。我们将 Kerr 黑洞建模为有限自旋表示中的大质量点粒子，并微扰计算其散射动力学。我们使用自旋插值方法解决混合经典与量子贡献的歧义，在第三 post-Minkowskian 阶获得直至自旋四次的 Kerr 黑洞散射经典振幅。从该振幅中，我们提取规范不变的径向作用量，使得能够使用协变 Dirac 括号计算经典非对齐自旋可观测量——例如线性和角冲量。该振幅表现出自旋平移对称性，在黑洞自旋按动量转移平移下保持不变。受此性质启发，我们研究支配散射过程的守恒量，并表明探针黑洞的动力学在探针自旋四次以内于渐近无穷远仍可积。最后，我们表明，Kerr 背景中探针黑洞的自旋四次径向作用量，在任意微扰阶，只要满足渐近可积性和自旋平移对称性，就完全由对齐自旋动力学确定。

English: The detection of gravitational waves has opened a new era of precision studies of gravity, demanding advances in our understanding of compact binary dynamics. Scattering amplitudes provide a powerful framework for studying these systems using methods from high-energy physics. In this thesis, we study classical black hole scattering, systematically including spin---an important feature of real astrophysical systems---to describe Kerr black hole dynamics. We model Kerr black holes as massive point particles in finite-spin representations and compute their scattering dynamics perturbatively. We resolve an ambiguity mixing classical and quantum contributions using our spin interpolation method, obtaining a classical amplitude for Kerr black hole scattering through quartic order in spin at third post-Minkowskian order. From this amplitude, we extract the gauge-invariant radial action, enabling the computation of classical non-aligned-spin observables---such as the linear and angular impulses---using covariant Dirac brackets. The amplitude exhibits spin-shift symmetry, remaining invariant under a shift of the black hole spin by the momentum transfer. Motivated by this property, we study the conserved quantities governing the scattering process and show that the dynamics of a probe black hole remains integrable at asymptotic infinity through quartic order in probe spin. Finally, we show that the quartic-in-spin radial action for a probe black hole in a Kerr background, at any order in perturbation theory, is completely fixed by the aligned-spin dynamics, provided that it satisfies asymptotic integrability and spin-shift symmetry.

一句话导读：用散射振幅方法系统包含自旋，计算 Kerr 黑洞经典散射及可观测量。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典黑洞散射与广义相对论动力学，匹配“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签；不涉及全息。

跨黑洞极端性的准正规模临界性

Quasinormal Modes Criticality across Black Hole Extremality

Zi-Yu Hou, Yu-Qi Lei, Xian-Hui Ge, Bum-Hoon Lee, Li-Jun Tian · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们研究爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡反德西特黑洞中极端解支向非极端解支的转变，其中非线性电磁参数 λ 控制极端解支是否存在。在固定压强和电荷下，极端黑洞仅当 λ≤λ_c 时存在；当 λ>λ_c 时，最低霍金温度保持非零。在该阈值附近，最低温度遵循线性临界标度 T_min∝(λ−λ_c)^{α_T}。我们通过计算沿最低温度黑洞支的无质量标量微扰准正规模来研究相应动力学行为。接近临界阈值时，两支复频率准正规模在虚轴上碰撞并重组成不同的纯虚支。随后这些模式的虚部以及更高阶 overtone 在临界阈值处趋于零，满足 Γ_n∝(λ−λ_c)^{α_n}，且 α_n≃α_T。由此得到的零阻尼模式越来越局域于事件视界附近，其相对阻尼率密切遵循近视界 AdS_2 谱所预言的 overtone 间距。结果表明，即使极端黑洞解不再存在，热力学与动力学临界行为仍紧密关联，进一步支持近极端谱行为在这一不同临界区域中的相关性。

English: We investigate the extremal solution branch to nonextremal branch transition in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg anti-de Sitter black holes, where the nonlinear electromagnetic parameter $\lambda$ governs the existence of the extremal branch solution. At fixed pressure and charge, extremal black hole only exist for $\lambda\leq\lambda_c$. For $\lambda>\lambda_c$, the lowest Hawking temperature keeps nonzero. Near this threshold, the lowest temperature follows a linear critical scaling, $T_{\min}\propto(\lambda-\lambda_c)^{\alpha_T}$. We investigate the corresponding dynamical behavior by computing the quasinormal modes of a massless scalar perturbation along the lowest-temperature black hole branch. As the critical threshold is approached, two complex-frequency quasinormal modes branches collide on the imaginary axis and reorganize into distinct purely imaginary branches. Then the imaginary parts of these modes, together with higher overtones, reach zero at the critical threshold, described by $\Gamma_n\propto(\lambda-\lambda_c)^{\alpha_n}$, with $\alpha_n\simeq\alpha_T$. The resulting zero-damped modes become increasingly localized near the event horizon, and their relative damping rates closely follow the overtone spacing predicted by the near-horizon AdS$_2$ spectrum. Our results show that the thermodynamic and dynamical critical behavior remain closely connected even when extremal black hole solutions cease to exist, providing further evidence for the relevance of near-extremal spectral behavior in this distinct critical regime.

一句话导读：在 Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg AdS 黑洞中，极端解消失的临界阈值附近，准正规模发生虚轴碰撞并出现零阻尼，其标度指数与最低温度临界标度一致。

相关度：high — 该文直接研究 AdS 黑洞准正规模临界行为，并涉及经典引力与黑洞热力学，与 QNM 和经典引力支线高度相关；但摘要未显式讨论全息对偶或用户重点的全息超流、探针膜等方向。

味膜中的手征磁波与轴荷弛豫

Chiral magnetic wave and axial charge relaxation in flavor branes

Masataka Matsumoto, Kensei Tanaka · hep-th, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们在无质量夸克的全息 D3/D7 模型的手征对称性破缺相中研究手征磁波与轴荷弛豫；在该模型中，味扇区的轴荷可通过在探针极限下转移到伴随扇区而弛豫。我们发现，当弛豫率较小时，准正规模的色散关系可由手征磁波的唯象表达式很好地描述。随后我们确定该弛豫率如何依赖矢量荷密度和手征凝聚。在较高温度下，它随荷密度增加而增大，并随凝聚增加而减小；而在较低温度下，这些依赖关系变为非单调。这些结果表明，手征磁波的唯象描述在准流体力学区域的手征对称性破缺相中同样适用。

English: We investigate the chiral magnetic wave and axial charge relaxation in the chiral symmetry broken phase of the holographic D3/D7 model with massless quarks, where the axial charge in the flavor sector can relax through transfer to the adjoint sector in the probe limit. We find that the dispersion relations of the quasi-normal modes are well described by the phenomenological expression for the chiral magnetic wave when the relaxation rate is small. We then determine how this rate depends on vector charge density and the chiral condensate. At higher temperatures, it increases with charge density and decreases with increasing condensate, while these dependences become non-monotonic at lower temperatures. These results show that the phenomenological description of the chiral magnetic wave also applies in the chiral symmetry broken phase in the quasi-hydrodynamic regime.

一句话导读：在D3/D7全息模型中研究手征对称性破缺相的手征磁波与轴荷弛豫，并用准正规模色散关系检验唯象描述。

相关度：high — 直接涉及D3/D7探针味膜与准正规模色散关系，并讨论手征磁波和轴荷输运，与用户重点标签中的D3-D7探针膜及QNM方向高度相关。

D3-D7/D3-D5探针膜与Floquet驱动QNM与非线性演化

非线性电动力学中的量子黑洞

Quantum Black Holes in Non-Linear Electrodynamics

Jana Men\v{s}\'ikov\'a, David Kubiznak, Robert B. Mann · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：AdS/CFT 对应与膜世界全息技术已被证明是处理半经典引力问题的有用工具，其中量子物质会对经典时空几何产生反作用。本文将这一方法应用于与 RegMax 非线性电动力学耦合的 Einstein 引力，构造了一类新型带电量子黑洞，并讨论其热力学与视界结构。

English: Techniques of AdS/CFT correspondence and braneworld holography have proven to be a useful tool for solving the problem of semi-classical gravity where quantum matter provides the back-reaction to classical spacetime geometry. In this paper, we apply this approach to Einstein gravity coupled to RegMax nonlinear electrodynamics and construct novel type of charged quantum black holes. We then discuss their thermodynamics and horizon structure.

一句话导读：利用 AdS/CFT 与膜世界全息，在耦合 RegMax 非线性电动力学的 Einstein 引力中构造带电量子黑洞并分析其热力学与视界结构。

相关度：medium — 论文属于 AdS/CFT/膜世界全息与量子黑洞构造，并涉及 Einstein 引力、非线性电动力学和黑洞热力学；与用户列出的全息超流超导、p波、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、QNM、D3-D7 探针膜等具体支线无直接重合，但对全息与经典引力/黑洞热力学方向有中等相关性。
