多迹本征态热化假说

The Multi-trace Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis

Ahmed I. Abdalla, Gabriele Di Ubaldo, Elisa Tabor · hep-th, cond-mat.stat-mech · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们提出多迹本征态热化假说（multi-trace ETH），它是 ETH 的推广，用于描述具有任意指标结构、包括多个不相交指标循环的算符矩阵元连通关联。我们从局域旋转不变性推导多迹 ETH，将其独立数据与高阶自由累积量联系起来，并构造一个循环矩阵模型来编码由此得到的多循环数据。我们为混沌量子系统中的多迹 ETH 提供证据，并在低维引力中计算多迹关联。在少体系统中，多迹贡献来自相关的周期轨道，包括对单点谱形状因子的线性斜坡贡献。在二维中，我们计算 JT 引力中四次耦合体标量所诱导的相互作用多迹关联，将绕转物质图与物质通道权重及反常维数修正联系起来。在三维中，我们展示多迹非高斯性来自拓扑改变鞍点和轻物质。利用 Virasoro TQFT 和共形平均场论，我们计算了涉及纽结与链环补集以及带物质环的环面虫洞的例子。在这些情形中，多迹 ETH 为混沌量子系统中的连通关联子和引力中的多边界虫洞振幅提供了统一框架。

English: We introduce a multi-trace eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, an extension of ETH that governs connected correlations of operator matrix elements with arbitrary index structures, including several disjoint index cycles. We derive multi-trace ETH from local rotational invariance, relate its independent data to higher-order free cumulants, and construct a cyclic matrix model that encodes the resulting multi-cycle data. We provide evidence for multi-trace ETH in chaotic quantum systems and compute multi-trace correlations in low-dimensional gravity. In few-body systems, multi-trace contributions arise from correlated periodic orbits, including a linear ramp contribution to the one-point spectral form factor. In two dimensions, we compute interaction-induced multi-trace correlations from a quartically coupled bulk scalar in JT gravity, relating winding matter diagrams to corrections of matter-channel weights and anomalous dimensions. In three dimensions, we show that multi-trace non-Gaussianities arise from both topology-changing saddles and light matter. Using Virasoro TQFT and conformal mean-field theory, we compute examples involving knot and link complements and torus wormholes with matter loops. Across these settings, multi-trace ETH provides a common framework for both connected correlators in chaotic quantum systems and multi-boundary wormhole amplitudes in gravity.

一句话导读：提出多迹 ETH，将混沌量子系统的连通关联与低维引力中的多边界虫洞振幅统一起来。

相关度：high — 论文直接发展 multi-trace ETH，并系统联系混沌量子系统关联函数与低维引力多边界虫洞振幅，正好覆盖用户重点标签“multi-trace”。

强子结构的全息方法

Holographic Approaches to Hadron Structure

Luigi Cappiello, Anton Rebhan, Shigeki Sugimoto · hep-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们介绍并概述全息方法在QCD和强子结构中的应用。在介绍全息QCD的弦论基础之后，我们回顾top-down和bottom-up模型对介子、重子和胶球现象学的预言。我们重点关注由Witten低能Yang-Mills模型中的探针味膜得到的top-down模型；在更现象学的bottom-up模型中，主要关注基于五维反de Sitter空间的最小模型，这些模型也一致地实现了整体对称性及其反常，同时允许高能行为更好地匹配真实QCD。

English: We give an introduction to and an overview of holographic approaches to QCD and hadron structure. After an introduction to the string-theoretic foundations of holographic QCD, we review the predictions of top-down and bottom-up models for the phenomenology of mesons, baryons, and glueballs. We focus on the top-down model following from probe flavor branes in the Witten model of low-energy Yang-Mills theory and, in the more phenomenological approach of bottom-up models, mostly on minimal models based on five-dimensional anti-de Sitter space, which also implement global symmetries and their anomalies consistently, but allow the high-energy behavior to better match real QCD.

一句话导读：综述全息QCD中top-down探针味膜与bottom-up AdS模型对介子、重子和胶球强子结构的预测。

相关度：medium — 该文是面向全息QCD与强子结构的综述，涉及top-down探针味膜和bottom-up AdS模型，与用户重点中的探针膜方向有一般关联；但不涉及全息超流超导、p波与多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、multi-trace、QNM与Floquet驱动等重点内容。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

中子星的全局与壳层性质

Global and crustal properties of neutron stars

Luiz L. Lopes, Guilherme Grams · nucl-th, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们研究了中子星壳层的处理方式如何影响恒星的全局性质以及壳层本身的性质。比较了三种方法：固定壳层与核心状态方程（EOS）直接连接、二者之间平滑过渡，以及在可压缩液滴模型中用与核心相对论平均场模型相同的核经验参数计算壳层的统一 EOS。对每种方法，我们考虑对称能斜率 L 的四个取值，44 ≤ L ≤ 92 MeV。对于 1.4 倍太阳质量恒星，一些全局性质受到弱到中等影响：三种方法给出的半径和转动惯量在 1% 内一致。另一方面，对于 L = 92 MeV，直接连接得到的潮汐形变比统一 EOS 低最多 21%。壳层性质的对比大得多。相对于统一 EOS，直接连接给出的壳层薄 15–36%，质量小 6–12 倍，携带的转动惯量份额小 5–8 倍（0.3–1.1% 而非 2.8–6.2%）。因此，壳层处理方式之间的差异与 L 变化对壳层性质的影响相当或更大，而对全局性质仍处于次要地位。

English: We investigate how the treatment of the neutron star crust affects both the global properties of the star and the properties of the crust itself. Three approaches are compared: a direct connection between a fixed crust and the core equation of state (EOS), a smooth crossover between them, and a unified EOS in which the crust is computed within a compressible liquid-drop model with the same nuclear empirical parameters of the relativistic mean-field model used for the core. For each approach, we consider four values of the symmetry energy slope, $44 \le L \le 92$ MeV. For a $1.4\,M_\odot$ star, some global properties are weakly to moderately affected: the radius and the moment of inertia agree within 1\% among the three approaches. On the other hand, the tidal deformability obtained with the direct connection is up to 21\% lower than the unified one for $L = 92$ MeV. A much larger contrast comes from the crustal properties. Relative to the unified EOS, the direct connection yields a crust that is 15--36\% thinner, 6--12 times less massive, and that carries a fraction of the total moment of inertia 5--8 times smaller (0.3--1.1\% instead of 2.8--6.2\%). The differences between crust treatments are thus comparable to or larger than those arising from variations in $L$ for crustal properties, while they remain subleading for global ones.

一句话导读：比较三种中子星壳层处理对全局性质与壳层性质的影响。

相关度：medium — 论文研究中子星壳层与状态方程对半径、转动惯量和潮汐形变的影响，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及全息。

具有动力学纵向囚禁的标量介子 K_0^*(1430) 的全息研究

Holographic study of the scalar meson $K_0^*(1430)$ with dynamical longitudinal confinement

Osra Hemmati, Reza Khosravi · hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：在光前全息 QCD 框架中，横向囚禁由全息机制自然实现；本文采用不变质量拟设，并引入纵向囚禁势（具体为 't Hooft 方程和可解析求解的 LMZV 势），研究轻标量介子 K_0^*(1430) 的结构与质量谱。横向与纵向囚禁动力学的结合使关键可观测量的计算更可靠，包括衰变常数、部分子分布函数、ξ-矩以及 twist-2 和 twist-3 分布振幅。基于这些振幅计算了半轻子衰变 B → K_0^* ℓ^+ ℓ^- 的跃迁形状因子，并给出微分分支比和纵向轻子极化不对称性随 q^2 变化的预言。结果与其他理论模型进行了比较，为 QCD 的非微扰动力学提供了有价值的认识。所预言的观测量为 LHCb 和 Belle II 未来对 B → K_0^*(1430) ℓ^+ ℓ^- 衰变的实验研究提供了理论动机的基准。

English: Within the framework of light-front holographic QCD, where transverse confinement is inherently realized through holography, the structure and mass spectrum of the light scalar meson $K_0^{*}(1430)$ are investigated by employing the invariant mass ansatz and enhancing the model with a longitudinal confinement potential (specifically, the 't Hooft equation and the analytically solvable LMZV potential). The combined transverse and longitudinal confinement dynamics lead to more reliable calculations of key observables, including decay constants, parton distribution function, $\xi$-moments, and twist-$2$ and twist-$3$ distribution amplitudes. Transition form factors for the semileptonic decay $B \rightarrow K_0^{*} \ell^+ \ell^-$ are computed from these amplitudes, yielding predictions for differential branching ratios and longitudinal lepton polarization asymmetries as functions of $q^2$. The results are compared with other theoretical models, offering valuable insights into the non-perturbative dynamics of QCD. The predicted observables provide theoretically motivated benchmarks for future experimental investigations of $B\rightarrow K_0^*(1430)\ell^+\ell^-$ decays at LHCb and Belle II.

一句话导读：在光前全息 QCD 中引入纵向囚禁势，计算 K_0^*(1430) 的结构函数及 B → K_0^* ℓ^+ ℓ^- 衰变可观测量的理论预测。

相关度：low — 论文属于全息 QCD 介子结构与 B 衰变唯象，未涉及用户重点的全息超流/超导、相变与气泡成核、QNM、D3-D7 探针膜或经典引力等支线。

带纵向禁闭的光前全息中B介子分布振幅的重夸克极限

Heavy-quark limit of $B$-meson distribution amplitudes in light-front holography with longitudinal confinement

Reza Khosravi · hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文研究重-轻介子光前全息波函数在重夸克极限下的行为，重点分析领头扭度B介子光锥分布振幅 φ_B^+(ω)。文章比较了引入有限夸克质量的两种方案：不变质量拟设（IMA）和基于 Li--Maris--Zhao--Vary（LMZV）势的纵向禁闭框架。在 IMA 中，分布振幅峰值仍位于软旁观者动量处，但其特征宽度随重夸克极限按 Δω ∝ m_B^{-1/2} 减小，导致纵向分布在渐近上坍缩为 δ(ω-m_q)；相应地，逆矩趋于有限极限 λ_{B,IMA} → m_q = O(Λ_QCD)。相反，LMZV 框架趋于具有 O(Λ_QCD) 稳定宽度的有限领头幂剖面，并给出与重夸克质量无关的稳定逆矩 λ_{B,LMZV}^{LP}=m_q+σ/2=O(Λ_QCD)。文章还利用零横向间隔处的局域轴矢量流考察 f_B 的重夸克标度。虽然 LMZV 纵向波函数具有非奇异的重夸克极限，但裸光前流并不能重现重夸克有效理论的标准标度 f_B ∝ m_B^{-1/2}：在 LMZV 框架中衰变常数标度为 f_B ∝ m_B^{-1}，在 IMA 中为 f_B ∝ m_B^{-5/4}。因此，纵向波函数的非奇异重夸克极限是必要的，但若没有系统的流匹配，它不足以保证局域流矩阵元具有正确的重夸克标度。

English: The heavy-quark limit of light-front holographic wave functions for heavy--light mesons is investigated, with particular emphasis on the leading-twist $B$-meson light-cone distribution amplitude, $\phi_B^+(\omega)$. Two prescriptions for incorporating finite quark masses are compared: the invariant mass ansatz (IMA) and the longitudinal confining framework based on the Li--Maris--Zhao--Vary (LMZV) potential. In the IMA, although the peak of the distribution amplitude remains at a soft spectator momentum, its characteristic width decreases as $\Delta\omega \propto m_B^{-1/2}$ in the heavy-quark limit, causing the longitudinal distribution to collapse asymptotically to $\delta(\omega-m_q)$. Accordingly, the inverse moment approaches the finite limit $\lambda_{B,\mathrm{IMA}} \to m_q = \mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}})$. In contrast, the LMZV framework approaches a finite leading-power (LP) profile with a stable width of order $\mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\rm QCD})$ and a stable inverse moment, $\lambda_{B,\mathrm{LMZV}}^{\mathrm{LP}} = m_q + \sigma/2 = \mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}})$, which is independent of the heavy-quark mass. The heavy-quark scaling of $f_B$ is also examined using the local axial-vector current at zero transverse separation. While the LMZV longitudinal wave function has a non-singular heavy-quark limit, the bare light-front current does not reproduce the canonical heavy-quark effective theory scaling $f_B \propto m_B^{-1/2}$: the decay constant scales as $f_B \propto m_B^{-1}$ in the LMZV framework and as $f_B \propto m_B^{-5/4}$ in the IMA. Thus, a non-singular heavy-quark limit of the longitudinal wave function is necessary but insufficient to ensure the correct heavy-quark scaling of local current matrix elements without systematic current matching.

一句话导读：比较两种有限夸克质量方案下B介子光前全息分布振幅的重夸克极限，指出纵向波函数非奇异不足以保证局域流矩阵元的正确HQET标度。

相关度：medium — 论文属于光前全息QCD与重介子分布振幅研究，与全息QCD主题相关；但它不直接涉及用户重点列出的全息超流超导、p波多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、multi-trace、QNM、D3-D7/D3-D5探针膜或经典引力等具体支线。

中子星质量-半径曲线中的夸克物质特征

Signatures of quark matter from neutron star mass-radius curve

Tuhin Malik, Milena Albino, Pedro Sanson, Constan\c{c}a Provid\^encia · nucl-th, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：通过分析超过25万条描述核子星、超子星和杂化星的质量-半径曲线，基于五种强子相对论平均场模型和四种夸克物质描述，寻找中子星内部存在夸克物质的信号。结果表明：半径最大值出现在1.7倍太阳质量以上时强烈支持星体内部存在夸克物质；第二个判据为 X=[R(2.0)-R(1.6)]-[R(1.6)-R(1.4)] > -0.2 km。研究还表明，多数超子星的半径最大值约在1.5倍太阳质量附近，且最大质量构型的致密度低于0.27；而对于存在半径最大值的核子星，该峰值低于1.3倍太阳质量，最大质量构型的致密度高于0.26。

English: Signatures of the presence of quark matter inside neutron stars are obtained from the analysis of more than 250,000 mass-radius curves that describe nucleonic, hyperonic and hybrid stars, based on five different hadronic relativistic mean-field models and four different descriptions of quark matter. A radius maximum occurring above $1.7\,M_\odot$ strongly favors quark matter inside the star. A second criterion identifying the presence of quark matter is $X>-0.2$km, where X=[R(2.0)-R(1.6)]-[R(1.6)-R(1.4)] is obtained from the radius of stars with 2.0, 1.6 and 1.4 $M_\odot$. It is also shown that most of the hyperonic stars have a radius maximum that peaks around 1.5$M_\odot$ and compactness below 0.27 for the maximum-mass configuration, while for the nucleonic stars that have a radius maximum, this peaks below 1.3$M_\odot$ and the compactness of the maximum-mass configuration lies above 0.26.

一句话导读：大规模质量-半径曲线统计给出中子星内部夸克物质的可观测判据。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及中子星质量-半径与经典引力/致密天体物理，符合“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签；但不涉及全息对偶或量子引力，与用户多数全息研究支线关联有限。

高阶对称能曲率对中子星性质的影响

Higher-Order Symmetry Energy Effects on the Properties of Neutron Stars

Zijing Zhou, Xuhao Wu, Shishao Bao, Min Ju, Jinniu Hu, Hong Shen · nucl-th, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：在相对论平均场（RMF）框架下研究对称能曲率 K_sym 对状态方程和中子星性质的影响。基于 TM1 和 IUFSU 模型构造受控参数族，在饱和密度处固定对称能 J 及其斜率 L，仅改变 K_sym。结果表明：较大的 K_sym 会降低超饱和密度处的对称能并减少 β 平衡物质中的质子分数，却导致更硬的状态方程和更高的声速。最大中子星质量受影响较弱，而规范半径 R_{1.4} 对 K_sym 呈近似线性依赖。

English: We study the effects of the symmetry-energy curvature $K_{\rm sym}$ on the equation of state (EoS) and neutron-star properties within the relativistic mean-field (RMF) framework. We construct controlled families of RMF parametrizations based on the TM1 and IUFSU models by varying $K_{\rm sym}$ while keeping the symmetry energy $J$ and its slope $L$ fixed at saturation density. We find that, although a larger $K_{\rm sym}$ leads to a lower symmetry energy at suprasaturation densities and a reduced proton fraction in $\beta$-equilibrated matter, it results in a stiffer EoS and a higher sound speed. The maximum neutron-star mass is weakly affected, whereas the canonical radius $R_{1.4}$ exhibits a nearly linear dependence on $K_{\rm sym}$.

一句话导读：固定 J 和 L 变化 K_sym 时，较大的对称能曲率使超饱和密度对称能降低、质子分数减少，但 EoS 更硬、声速更高，最大质量弱受影响而 R_{1.4} 近似线性依赖 K_sym。

相关度：low — 论文属于核物理/中子星状态方程研究，仅在经典中子星性质意义上与经典引力标签弱相关；不涉及全息、超流、相变、QNM 等用户重点方向。

单个中子星自旋对双中子星并合抛射物、r过程核合成与千新星的印记

The imprint of individual neutron star spins on ejecta, $r$-process nucleosynthesis, and kilonovae in binary neutron star mergers

Beyhan Karakas, Rahime Matur, David Radice, Roland Haas, Maximilian Ruffert · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：在领头阶，双中子星的引力波信号仅通过有效自旋参数 χ_eff 依赖于各自自旋 χ1 和 χ2。我们首次系统研究各自自旋对抛射物、r过程核合成和千新星辐射的影响，并在固定总质量、质量比和 χ_eff 下进行比较。我们使用三个总质量区域的数值相对论抛射物，采用有限温度、依赖成分的 SFHo 状态方程以及中微子发射和吸收。对于 M_tot=2.55 M_sun、固定 χ_eff=0，各自自旋使动力学抛射物质量变化约 45 倍，而绝对 A≥140 产额跨越两个数量级以上，镧系与轻 r 过程质量比从约 2 增至约 70。瞬时坍缩的 4.10 M_sun 模型在 χ_eff=0 时重元素产额差异超过四个数量级。千新星保留各自自旋印记，峰值亮度差异可达约 0.9 等。在 40 Mpc 处，所有三组固定 χ_eff=0 的配对在所有视角的常见历元均高于所采用的探测深度，同历元颜色差异可达约 1.5 等。当模拟得到的长期抛射物被同一参数化盘外流替代时，颜色印记仍存在，表明盘质量差异不是主要驱动因素，动力学抛射物有重要贡献。中微子吸收系统性地增亮千新星并使峰值相关颜色蓝移。这些电磁特征可以打破引力波信号中各自自旋之间的简并。

English: To leading order, the gravitational-wave (GW) signal from binary neutron stars depends on the individual spins, $\chi_1$ and $\chi_2$, only through the effective spin parameter $\chi_{\rm eff}$. We present the first systematic investigation of individual-spin effects on ejecta, $r$-process nucleosynthesis, and kilonova emission, including comparisons at fixed total mass, mass ratio and $\chi_{\rm eff}$. We use numerical relativity ejecta from three total mass regimes with the finite-temperature, composition-dependent SFHo equation of state and neutrino emission and absorption. For $M_{\rm tot}=2.55\,M_\odot$ at fixed $\chi_{\rm eff}=0$, individual spins change the dynamical ejecta mass by a factor of ${\sim}45$, while the absolute $A\geq140$ yield spans more than two orders of magnitude and the lanthanide to light $r$-process mass ratio increases from ${\sim}2$ to ${\sim}70$. Prompt-collapse $4.10\,M_\odot$ models show heavy-element yield differences exceeding four orders of magnitude at $\chi_{\rm eff}=0$. The kilonova retains the individual-spin imprint, with peak brightness differences reaching ${\sim}0.9$ mag. At $40\,{\rm Mpc}$, all three fixed $\chi_{\rm eff}=0$ pairs remain above adopted depths at common epochs for all viewing angles, with same-epoch colour differences reaching ${\sim}1.5$ mag. The colour imprint persists when the simulation-derived secular ejecta are replaced by the same parametric disc outflow, indicating that disc mass differences are not the primary driver and that dynamical ejecta make an important contribution. Neutrino absorption systematically brightens the kilonova and shifts peak-associated colours blueward. These EM signatures can break the degeneracy between individual spins in the GW signal.

一句话导读：数值相对论显示，在固定有效自旋时双中子星各自自旋仍强烈改变抛射物、r过程产额和千新星颜色，为打破引力波自旋简并提供电磁探针。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/数值相对论与中子星并合天体物理，匹配用户“经典引力/广义相对论”支线；不涉及全息、AdS/CFT 或量子引力，与用户其他全息重点标签无直接关联。

从因果作用量原理几何推导 Einstein 方程

A Geometric Derivation of the Einstein Equations from the Causal Action Principle

Felix Finster, Christoph Krpoun · math-ph, gr-qc, math.DG, math.MP · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文分析因果费米子系统的因果作用量原理，假设最小化测度的支撑具有光滑流形 \tilde{M} 的结构，并引入“密切真空”（osculating vacua）概念。论文表明，拉格朗日量在 \tilde{M} 上诱导出洛伦兹度规；因果作用量的 Euler-Lagrange 方程进一步要求 Ricci 张量满足广义相对论的 Einstein 方程，其能量-动量张量由正则化长度的幂展开给出，而引力耦合常数为正则化长度的平方。该方法为系统推导 Einstein 方程的修正提供了程序。论文还包含对因果变分原理和因果作用量原理的自足介绍，并在任意维 \tilde{M} 的一般因果变分原理框架下引入和分析大多数几何结构（联络、黎曼度规和曲率）；洛伦兹情形仅适用于因果费米子系统，且只在四维时空中给出。

English: The causal action principle for causal fermion systems is analyzed for a minimizing measure whose support is assumed to have the structure of a smooth manifold $\tilde{M}$. The concept of osculating vacua is introduced. It is shown that the Lagrangian induces on $\tilde{M}$ a Lorentzian metric. Moreover, the Euler-Lagrange equations of the causal action imply that the Ricci tensor must satisfy the Einstein equations of General Relativity for an energy-momentum tensor given in terms of a power expansion in the regularization length. The gravitational coupling constant is found to be the square of the regularization length. Our methods provide a systematic procedure for deriving corrections to the Einstein equations. The paper includes a self-contained introduction to causal variational principles and the causal action principle. Most geometric structures (connection, Riemannian metric and curvature) are introduced and analyzed in the general setting of causal variational principles for an arbitrary dimension of $\tilde{M}$. The Lorentzian setting works only for causal fermion systems and is worked out only in four spacetime dimensions.

一句话导读：在因果费米子系统中，因果作用量的 Euler-Lagrange 方程导出 Ricci 张量满足 Einstein 方程，引力耦合常数为正则化长度的平方。

相关度：medium — 论文主题是从因果作用量原理几何推导 Einstein 方程，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线；不涉及 AdS/CFT 或全息对偶。

GW190814的坍缩星盘起源

A Collapsar-Disk Origin for GW190814

Vishal Baibhav, Brian D. Metzger, Lam Hui · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：GW190814是一次显著引力波事件：一个23倍太阳质量黑洞与一个2.6倍太阳质量致密天体并合，其极端质量比难以用标准孤立双星或动力学形成通道解释。近期工作表明，中微子冷却的坍缩星盘可发生引力不稳定并碎裂，在新形成的中心黑洞周围产生中子星或低质量黑洞。这些碎片随后可能相互作用、散射、彼此并合或旋入中心残余。我们探讨GW190814来自此类坍缩星盘碎片与中心黑洞并合的可能性。该情景的关键预言是引力波事件前存在剥离包层超新星的时间关联；我们识别出在并合前约60天、位于GW190814可信体积内的Ib型超新星候选体SN2019npv作为可能的电磁前兆。虽然该延迟对常规千新星对应体而言过长，我们证明盘碎片间的三体相互作用可将部分致密天体激发到宽轨道，并产生数周至数月的并合延迟。尽管GW190814本身预计不会产生可探测的潮汐瓦解驱动辐射，该通道中未来的延迟并合可通过再处理千新星加热或并合抛射物与先前超新星抛射物碰撞驱动的激波产生明亮暂现源。最后，若将SN2019npv视为电磁对应体，GW190814成为明亮标准汽笛，并给出H0=71.1^{+9.3}_{-7.5} km s^{-1} Mpc^{-1}。

English: GW190814 was a remarkable gravitational-wave (GW) event: a merger between a $23 M_\odot$ black hole (BH) and a $2.6\,M_\odot$ compact object, with an extreme mass ratio that is difficult to reproduce through standard isolated-binary or dynamical formation channels. Recent work has shown that neutrino-cooled collapsar disks can become gravitationally unstable and fragment, producing neutron stars (NSs) or low-mass BHs in orbit around the newly formed central BH. These fragments may subsequently interact, scatter, merge with one another, or inspiral into the central remnant. We explore the possibility that GW190814 originated from such a collapsar-disk fragment merging with the central BH. A key prediction of this scenario is a temporal association with a stripped-envelope supernova preceding the GW event, and we identify the Type Ib supernova candidate SN2019npv, which occurred inside the GW190814 credible volume approximately 60 days before coalescence, as a possible electromagnetic precursor. Although this delay is too long for a conventional kilonova counterpart, we show that three-body interactions among disk fragments can excite some compact objects to wide orbits and produce merger delays of weeks to months. While GW190814 itself was not expected to produce detectable tidal-disruption-powered emission, future delayed mergers in this channel could generate luminous transients through either reprocessed kilonova heating or shocks driven as merger ejecta collide with the preceding supernova ejecta. Finally, treating SN2019npv as the electromagnetic counterpart makes GW190814 a bright standard siren and yields $H_{0} = 71.1^{+9.3}_{-7.5} km s^{-1}Mpc^{-1}$.

一句话导读：该文提出GW190814可能源于坍缩星盘碎片与中心黑洞的延迟并合，并讨论超新星前兆及标准汽笛宇宙学限制。

相关度：low — 论文属于引力波天体物理与经典黑洞/致密天体形成通道研究，不涉及AdS/CFT、全息对偶或量子引力理论，因此与用户的全息研究重点相关性低。

LISA科学地面段约定

LISA science ground segment conventions

Quentin Baghi, Stanislas Babak, Leor Barack, Jean-Baptiste Bayle, Ollie Burke, Raffi Enficiaud, Hector Estelles, Cecilio Garc\'ia Quir\'os, Olaf Hartwig, Aurelien Hees, Sascha Husa, Henri Inchausp\'e, Eric Joffre, Antoine Klein, Philip Lynch, Sylvain Marsat, Jonathan Menu, Zach Nasipak, Ramon Pardo De Santayana, Harald Pfeiffer, Lorenzo Pompili, Adam Pound, Geraint Pratten, Antoni Ramos-Buades, Carlos Sopuerta, Niels Warburton · astro-ph.IM, astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文档规定激光干涉空间天线（LISA）分布式数据处理中心（DDPC）内用于数据模拟、波形和分析管线的约定，也可作为所有与LISA任务相关出版物的最佳实践指南。内容涵盖时频变换、引力波源参数化、仪器对引力波的响应、时间延迟干涉以及参考系定义。

English: This document sets out the conventions used for data simulations, waveforms, and analysis pipelines within the Distributed Data Processing Centre (DDPC) of the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). It can also be considered a best practice guide for all publications related to the LISA mission. Topics covered include time-to-frequency transformations, gravitational-wave source parametrization, the instrumental response to gravitational waves, time-delay interferometry, and reference frame definitions.

一句话导读：LISA数据模拟、波形与分析管线的约定文档。

针孔区域中动力学引力波并合渠道的偏心率分布

Eccentricity Distribution for Dynamical Gravitational-Wave Merger Channels in The Pinhole Regime

Mor Rozner, Teagan A. Clarke, Isobel M. Romero-Shaw, Johan Samsing · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

English: We argue that various dynamical astrophysical black hole merger channels are expected to result in a common eccentricity distribution at gravitational-wave (GW) frequencies relevant for LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA (LVK) in the high eccentricity limit. This follows from the large separation of scales between the GW regime required for creating eccentric mergers in LVK, and the underlying astrophysical formation environment. 基于熵的核坍缩超新星引力波分类用于核状态方程推断

Entropy-based classification of core-collapse supernova gravitational waves for nuclear equation-of-state inference

Aknur Sakan, Nurzhan Ussipov, Y. Sultan Abylkairov, Marat Zaidyn, Alisher Zhunuskanov, Almat Akhmetali, Jos\'e A. Font, Matthew C. Edwards, Ernazar Abdikamalov · astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：核坍缩超新星产生的引力波为探索恒星坍缩期间的物理过程以及致密核物质性质提供了独特探针。本文研究利用基于熵的机器学习框架，对与不同核状态方程相关的超新星引力波信号进行分类。该分析结合信号分解、特征提取和特征选择技术，以识别波形中有信息量的特征并区分不同的状态方程模型。我们使用一组紧凑的选定特征评估了几种机器学习分类器的性能，并考察了它们在探测器噪声存在下的鲁棒性。我们发现，基于熵的表示能够捕捉波形类别之间的潜在差异，并在噪声条件下保持比原始数据更好的性能，从而实现准确分类。这些结果展示了基于复杂度的特征提取作为核坍缩超新星引力波研究中一种高效且可解释方法的潜力。

English: Gravitational waves from core-collapse supernovae provide a unique probe of the physical processes occurring during stellar collapse and of the properties of dense nuclear matter. In this work we investigate the classification of supernova gravitational-wave signals associated with different nuclear equations of state using an entropy-based machine learning framework. The analysis combines signal decomposition, feature extraction, and feature selection techniques to identify informative characteristics of the waveforms and distinguish between different equation of state models. We evaluate the performance of several machine learning classifiers using a compact set of selected features and assess their robustness in the presence of detector noise. We find that entropy-based representations capture the underlying differences between waveform classes and enable accurate classification while maintaining better performance compared to raw data under noisy conditions. These results demonstrate the potential of complexity-based feature extraction as an efficient and interpretable approach for gravitational wave studies of core-collapse supernovae.

Type-D 黑洞的完整准正规模 II：极端 Kerr 黑洞

Complete quasinormal modes of Type-D black holes. II. Extremal Kerr black holes

Changkai Chen, Xiaohua Zhang, Zhoujian Cao, Jiliang Jing · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们发展了一种双合流 Heun 方法，将此前对 Type-D 黑洞完整准正规模（QNM）谱的研究推广到极端 Kerr 黑洞。径向边界条件和角向正则性决定了极端情况下的引力频率。与局限于传统黎曼面的早期谱相比，跨频率分支割线的解析延拓揭示了非传统黎曼面上的额外 QNM。自旋延拓使我们的 Schwarzschild 模族趋向极端性，调查在指定角向分支和黎曼面上延伸到第 41 阶泛音。我们首次证明，由非传统 Schwarzschild 模延拓而来的 m<0 的非传统 Class I 模在极端极限下形成趋近超辐射界 Mω=m/2 的零阻尼序列。在固定角向扇区内，有限阻尼和零阻尼序列在 Schwarzschild 泛音排序中交错，因此单一泛音截断无法将它们分开。在超辐射界附近的匹配区分了在临界频率累积的非极端序列与精确极端性处的孤立 QNM。散射振幅计算验证了代表性根及其黎曼面归属。扩展后的谱澄清了 Schwarzschild 模族在极端性处如何重组，完备性通过在指定角向分支和黎曼面上有界频率域内的围道计数建立。

English: We develop a double-confluent Heun method that extends our previous study of complete quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectra of Type-D black holes to extremal Kerr black holes. Radial boundary conditions and angular regularity determine the gravitational frequencies at extremality. Compared with earlier spectra restricted to the conventional Riemann sheet, analytic continuation across frequency branch cuts reveals additional QNMs on unconventional Riemann sheets. Spin continuation follows our Schwarzschild mode families toward extremality, with the survey extending through the 41st overtone on specified angular branches and Riemann sheets. We show for the first time that unconventional Class I modes with $m<0$, continued from unconventional Schwarzschild modes, form zero-damping sequences approaching the superradiant bound $M\omega=m/2$ in the extremal limit. Within a fixed angular sector, finite-damping and zero-damping sequences are interspersed in the Schwarzschild overtone ordering, so a single overtone cutoff cannot separate them. Matching near the superradiant bound distinguishes nonextremal sequences accumulating at the critical frequency from isolated QNMs at exact extremality. Scattering-amplitude calculations verify representative roots and their sheet assignments. The expanded spectrum clarifies how Schwarzschild mode families reorganize at extremality, with completeness established by contour counting within a bounded frequency domain on a specified angular branch and Riemann sheet.

一句话导读：用双合流 Heun 方法给出极端 Kerr 黑洞的完整 QNM 谱，并揭示跨黎曼面的额外模与超辐射界零阻尼序列。

相关度：high — 直接研究极端 Kerr 黑洞的准正规模谱，属于经典引力与 QNM 方向，与用户重点标签“QNM与非线性演化”和“经典引力/广义相对论”高度相关；不涉及全息。

半经典引力中带迹反常的旋转黑洞周围 Novikov-Thorne 吸积盘的特征

Signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes in semiclassical gravity with trace anomaly

Yuxiang Zuo, Shiyang Hu, Dan Li, Chen Deng, Guansheng He, Wenbin Lin · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：半经典引力通过由耦合参数刻画的量子反作用为解决黑洞奇点问题提供框架，因此有必要研究此类天体的吸积性质。本文在带 type-A 迹反常的半经典引力框架下，研究不同参数空间中旋转黑洞周围 Novikov-Thorne 吸积盘的热辐射特征，重点分析能流密度、温度分布和热光度。结果表明，引入正耦合参数会抑制盘发射，使能流密度和温度的峰值降低，并使热光度的峰值及对应峰值频率低于广义相对论中的 Kerr 情形；当黑洞具有高自旋时该差异尤为显著。机制上，增大耦合参数会增强中心致密天体的有效引力场强度，使赤道稳定圆轨道外移并提高轨道物质比能，从而降低辐射效率并削弱整体盘发射。该抑制机制为区分半经典引力与广义相对论、并利用热盘谱对耦合参数施加观测限制提供了潜在的天体物理窗口。

English: Semiclassical gravity provides a promising framework to address the black hole singularity problem through quantum backreaction characterized by a coupling parameter, making it essential to thoroughly explore the accretion properties around such objects. In this paper, within the framework of semiclassical gravity with type-A trace anomaly, we investigate the thermal radiative signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes across diverse parameter spaces, focusing on the energy flux density, temperature profile, and thermal luminosity. Our findings demonstrate that the introduction of a positive coupling parameter suppresses disk emission, leading to lower peak values of both energy flux density and temperature, as well as lower peak values and corresponding peak frequencies in thermal luminosity compared to their Kerr counterparts in general relativity. In particular, this discrepancy becomes remarkably pronounced when the black hole possesses a high spin. Mechanistically, increasing the coupling parameter reinforces the effective gravitational field strength of the central compact object, which pushes the equatorial stable circular orbits outward and increases the specific energy of the orbiting matter, thereby reducing the radiative efficiency and weakening the overall disk emission. This suppression mechanism offers a potential astrophysical window to distinguish semiclassical gravity from general relativity and to place observational constraints on the coupling parameter via thermal disk spectra.

一句话导读：在含 type-A 迹反常的半经典引力中，Novikov-Thorne 吸积盘的热辐射被正耦合参数抑制，高自旋时与 Kerr 的差异更显著。

相关度：medium — 属于经典/半经典黑洞与吸积盘研究，可对应“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签；但不涉及全息或其他全息研究支线。

修改黑洞反弹致密天体的修正热力学与辐射预测

Corrected thermodynamics and radiation predictions of modified black bounce compact objects

Shokhzod Jumaniyozov, Javlon Rayimbaev, Yassine Sekhmani, Satimbay Palvanov, Olmos Tursunboyev, Dilshod Karshiev · gr-qc · v4 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文研究对Schwarzschild修改引力黑洞施加Simpson-Visser（SV）正则化后得到的规则致密天体的热力学性质与辐射特征。所得SV-MOG时空的 lapse 函数同时包含MOG耦合参数α和黑洞反弹参数l，并随l在规则黑洞、单向虫洞和可穿越虫洞之间平滑过渡。作者对黑洞分支推导Hawking温度和热容，并通过C_V变号识别二阶相变；对于无视界虫洞分支，由于不存在因果视界因而没有真正Hawking辐射，转而由不稳定光子球的Lyapunov指数构造物理上定义良好的有效温度。熵的量子引力修正通过由系数β1、β2参数化的对数项纳入，并讨论圈量子引力和弦理论中理论上偏好的参数范围，表明在视界半径较小时对Bekenstein-Hawking面积律的偏离变得显著。线性标量扰动分析进一步显示有效径向势在黑洞外部及虫洞喉部保持非负，表明对单极扰动稳定，且这一稳定性独立于由热容推断的热力学稳定性。

English: We study the thermodynamic properties and radiation characteristics of a regular compact object obtained by applying the Simpson-Visser (SV) regularisation to the Schwarzschild modified gravity black hole. The resulting SV-MOG spacetime, whose lapse function involves both the MOG coupling parameter alpha and the black-bounce parameter l, smoothly interpolates between a regular black hole, a one-way wormhole, and a traversable wormhole depending on the parameter l. We derive the Hawking temperature and heat capacity for the black hole branch, identifying second-order phase transitions signaled by sign changes in CV, and, for the horizonless wormhole branch where no causal horizon and hence no genuine Hawking radiation exists we instead construct a physically well-defined effective temperature from the Lyapunov exponent of the unstable photon sphere. Quantum gravitational corrections to the entropy are incorporated via logarithmic terms parameterized by coefficients beta1, beta2, whose theoretically preferred ranges in loop quantum gravity and string theory we discuss, and we show that deviations from the Bekenstein-Hawking area law become significant at small horizon radii. A linear scalar-perturbation analysis further shows that the effective radial potential remains non-negative throughout the black-hole exterior and across the wormhole throat, indicating stability against monopole perturbations independently of the thermodynamic stability inferred from the heat capacity.

用于脉冲星计时数据闪电式引力波分析的新框架

A new framework for lightning-fast gravitational wave analysis of pulsar timing data

Aiden Gundersen, Rutger van Haasteren, Neil J. Cornish, Michele Vallisneri, Patrick M. Meyers · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：脉冲星计时阵列数据分析计算成本高昂，限制了可研究模型的复杂度。随着脉冲星计时数据集及相应模型规模与复杂度的增长，快速且可扩展的推断方法至关重要。本文通过在傅里叶系数空间采样而非解析边缘化来加速脉冲星计时分析。以往研究表明，傅里叶空间会诱导复杂的高维后验几何，通常难以采样。作者证明，在适当的坐标变换下，傅里叶系数近似服从标准正态分布，并可采用哈密顿蒙特卡洛方法高效采样。对于规模与复杂度与 NANOGrav 15 年发布相当的数据集，新方法在 NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090 GPU 上约 15 分钟内即可对包含脉冲星间关联、随机信号和确定性信号的一系列模型得到收敛后验分布。相比之下，传统脉冲星计时分析软件 ENTERPRISE 在 CPU 集群上分析同样联合随机与确定性模型的数据集则需要数月计算时间。

English: Pulsar timing array data analysis is computationally expensive, limiting the complexity of models which can be studied. As pulsar timing datasets and their respective models grow in size and sophistication, faster and scalable inference methods are essential. In this paper, we accelerate pulsar timing analyses by sampling in the space of Fourier coefficients instead of analytically marginalizing over them. Previous studies have shown the Fourier space induces a complex, high-dimensional posterior geometry, from which it is generally difficult to sample. We show that under an appropriate coordinate transformation the Fourier coefficients approximately follow a standard normal distribution, and may be efficiently sampled using a Hamiltonian Monte Carlo scheme. Under this coordinate transformation, for datasets of size and complexity comparable to the NANOGrav 15-year release, the new method produces converged posterior distributions for a range of models which include inter-pulsar correlations, stochastic, and deterministic signals in approximately 15 minutes on an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090 GPU. By comparison, the legacy pulsar timing analysis software \texttt{ENTERPRISE} would require months of computation on a CPU cluster to analyze comparable datasets under the same joint stochastic and deterministic models.

一句话导读：提出基于傅里叶系数坐标变换与哈密顿蒙特卡洛的脉冲星计时阵列引力波数据分析加速方法，将 NANOGrav 规模分析从数月缩短至约 15 分钟。

相关度：low — 论文属于经典引力/引力波观测数据分析的计算方法，不涉及 AdS/CFT、全息对偶、量子引力等理论全息研究，与用户主要研究支线相关性较低。

SPLIT：面向长旋进引力波源的宽松相干推断，以增强对建模与数据异常值的鲁棒性

SPLIT: Loosely-coherent inference of long-inspiral gravitational-wave sources for robustness against modeling and data outliers

Shubham Kejriwal · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：激光干涉空间天线（LISA）将探测数十个极端质量比与中等质量比旋进（EMRI/IMRI）的引力波。这些源将在频带内停留数月至数年，具有非凡的科学潜力。然而，当前流程中完全相位相干分析的标准对波形精度提出严格要求；若无法在如此长的时间内建模信号，可能导致显著的系统偏差。为此，我们构建了一个稳健的贝叶斯推断框架，将数据分段为块，对每块进行相干推断，并利用宽松相干相位信息重组结果。通过将完整相位跟踪限制在更短的块时长内，该方法防止了相位误差的显著累积。我们在 SPLIT（长旋进模板的分段后验）中实现了该方法，这是一个 GPU 加速的 Python 包。将 SPLIT 应用于富含环境的注入，我们证明：完全相干的真空 GR 分析相对真值产生最大一维系统偏差约 4.8σ，而宽松相干方法较短的积分窗口将此类偏差限制在 ≲0.5σ。总体而言，尽管最优信噪比有分数损失，该方法在参数精度上的显著提升为 LISA 和其他未来引力波探测器提供了非常有利的权衡。

English: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will detect gravitational waves (GWs) from dozens of extreme- and intermediate-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs/IMRIs). These sources will stay in-band for months to years, offering extraordinary scientific potential. However, their fully phase-coherent analysis standard in current pipelines imposes stringent waveform accuracy requirements; failing to model the signal over such long durations can result in significant systematic biases. To address this, we formulate a robust Bayesian inference framework that segments the data into blocks, infers each block coherently, and recombines the results using loosely-coherent phase information. By restricting full phase-tracking to much shorter block durations, this approach prevents significant accumulation of phase errors. We implement this methodology in \textsc{SPLIT} (Segmented Posteriors for Long-Inspiral Templates), a GPU-accelerated Python package. Applying \textsc{SPLIT} to an environment-rich injection, we demonstrate that while a fully-coherent vacuum-GR analysis incurs a maximum 1D systematic bias of $\approx 4.8\sigma$ from the truth, the shorter integration window of our loosely-coherent approach restricts such biases to $\lesssim 0.5\sigma$. Overall, despite a fractional loss of optimal signal-to-noise ratio, the substantial improvement in parameter accuracy offered by our method presents a highly advantageous trade-off for LISA and other future GW detectors.

一句话导读：提出 SPLIT 分段宽松相干贝叶斯推断方法，用于 LISA 长旋进引力波源，显著抑制波形建模误差导致的系统偏差。

相关度：low — 该文属于经典引力波探测与数据分析方法，研究 LISA 长旋进源推断鲁棒性，不涉及 AdS/CFT、全息对偶或量子引力理论。

论Finsler引力中球对称Berwald真空解

On spherically symmetric Berwald vacuum solutions in Finsler gravity

Nicoleta Voicu, Diana - Maria Birla, Christian Pfeifer · gr-qc, math-ph, math.DG, math.MP · v3 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：Berwald-Finsler时空是最接近伪黎曼几何的Finsler时空，其典范非线性联络在时空上定义了仿射联络。本文找到了Finsler引力真空方程的首批精确、非Ricci平坦、SO(3)对称的Berwald解。为此，作者先从所有球对称Berwald时空中选出唯一允许平坦Finsler时空结构的类，使其特别适合未来研究渐近平坦性；然后对该类完全求解Finsler引力真空方程，并得到三族解。特别地，文章表明在Finsler几何中存在SO(3)对称、非Ricci平坦的真空解。这些解有望用于在黎曼描述之外建模致密天体周围的引力场。

English: Berwald-Finsler spacetimes are Finsler spacetimes that are closest to pseudo-Riemannian geometry, as their canonical nonlinear connection defines an affine connection on spacetime. In this paper, we find the first exact, non-Ricci flat, $SO(3)$-symmetric Berwald solutions to the Finsler gravity vacuum equation. To this aim, we first select from all spherically symmetric Berwald spacetimes, the only class that admits flat Finsler spacetime structures, making it particularly suitable for future investigations of asymptotic flatness. Then, for this class, we completely solve the Finsler gravity vacuum equation, and find three families of solutions. In particular, we show that in Finsler geometry there exist $SO(3)$-symmetric, vacuum solutions that are not Ricci-flat. These solutions are promising candidates to model the gravitational field around compact objects, beyond their Riemannian description.

一句话导读：在Finsler引力中构造并完全求解了一类球对称Berwald真空方程，得到首批非Ricci平坦的SO(3)对称精确解。

厚膜世界中的谱蝴蝶效应与稳健铃宕

Spectral Butterfly Effect and Resilient Ringdown in Thick Braneworlds

Hai-Long Jia, Wen-Di Guo, Yu-Peng Zhang, Yu-Xiao Liu · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：准正规模谱是将引力波观测与额外维几何联系起来的独特指纹。本文表明厚膜世界存在谱蝴蝶效应：有效势的无穷小形变会触发准正规模的剧烈迁移，从而挑战该指纹的稳定性。频域不稳定性敏感依赖于扰动的位置和强度。时域上，近膜扰动主要改变早期铃宕，远膜扰动产生干净的晚期回波。关键的是，引力子零模仍保持局域化，维持四维引力。尽管谱很脆弱，当前探测器灵敏度下可观测的早期信号仍由原基模主导。因此厚膜世界呈现脆弱谱与稳健铃宕的非平凡共存，支持当前引力波天文学继续使用标准指纹，同时揭示其隐藏敏感性。

English: The quasinormal mode spectrum is a unique fingerprint linking gravitational-wave observations to extra-dimensional geometry. In this article, we show that thick braneworlds exhibit a spectral butterfly effect: infinitesimal deformations of the effective potential trigger dramatic migrations of quasinormal modes, challenging the presumed stability of this fingerprint. Frequency-domain instabilities depend sensitively on the perturbation's location and strength. In the time domain, near-brane perturbations primarily modify the early ringdown, while far-brane perturbations generate clean late-time echoes. Crucially, the graviton zero mode remains localized, preserving four-dimensional gravity. Despite this pronounced spectral fragility, the observable early-stage signal under current detector sensitivities is still dominated by the original fundamental mode. Hence, thick braneworlds display a nontrivial coexistence of a fragile spectrum and a resilient ringdown, supporting the continued use of the standard fingerprint in present-day gravitational-wave astronomy while revealing its hidden sensitivity.

一句话导读：厚膜世界中有效势的微小形变会显著迁移准正规模谱，但当前可观测的早期铃宕仍由原基模主导。

相关度：medium — 论文直接涉及准正规模谱、铃宕与引力波信号，并属于经典引力/膜世界方向；但未聚焦用户重点的全息超流、multi-trace、D3-D7探针膜或非线性演化。

QNM与非线性演化经典引力/广义相对论

论通过解析延拓的束缚态与黑洞准正规模映射：谱不稳定性视角

On the mapping between bound states and black hole quasinormal modes via analytic continuation: a spectral instability perspective

Guan-Ru Li, Wei-Liang Qian, Xiao-Mei Kuang, Ramin G. Daghigh, Jodin C. Morey, Michael D. Green, Peng Xu, Rui-Hong Yue · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文在谱不稳定性背景下研究黑洞微扰理论中束缚态与准正规模之间的关系。分析表明，这种谱映射的可靠性超出用于连接微扰束缚态问题与相应开放系统动力学的解析延拓的有效域。然而，为使 Völkel 提出的数值方案奏效，度规参数的变换必须在底层 Taylor 展开收敛的区域内进行。作为可解析处理的显式例子，我们考察受扰动的 delta 函数势垒和 Pöschl-Teller 势垒。对于后者，我们构造两种不同的微扰设置，使微扰论中级数展开的收敛性可被严格控制。当形变位于势的极值附近时，所得的束缚态能量修正可解析延拓为受扰动的准正规模频率，并与已知半解析结果一致。相反，当微扰局域在远离致密天体的渐近区域时，束缚态仅被轻微修改，并可由一阶微扰展开准确描述；但相应的解析延拓给出强烈变形的谱，与准正规模没有明显联系。这些发现有助于审视束缚态在何种条件下忠实编码准正规模谱，并有助于理解黑洞谱不稳定性的物理。

English: In this work, we investigate the relation between bound states and quasinormal modes within black hole perturbation theory in the context of spectral instability. Our analysis indicates that the reliability of such spectral mapping stretches beyond the domain of validity of the analytic continuation employed to connect the perturbative bound-state problem to the corresponding open-system dynamics. However, for the numerical scheme proposed by V\"olkel to work, the transformations of the metric parameters must be carried out in a region where the underlying Taylor expansion is convergent. As analytically accessible explicit examples, we explore the perturbed delta-function and P\"oschl-Teller potential barriers. For the latter, we construct two distinct perturbative setups for which the convergence of the series expansion involved in the perturbation theory can be rigorously controlled. When the deformation is placed near the potential's extremum, the resulting corrections to the bound-state energies can be analytically continued to yield perturbed quasinormal frequencies, in agreement with known semi-analytic results. In contrast, when the perturbation is localized asymptotically far from the compact object, the bound states are only mildly modified and are accurately described by a perturbative expansion to the first order. However, the associated analytic continuation yields a strongly deformed spectrum that shows no clear connection to the quasinormal modes. These findings contribute to the effort to scrutinize the conditions under which bound states faithfully encode quasinormal spectra and to shed light on the underlying physics of black hole spectral instability.

一句话导读：研究束缚态—准正规模解析延拓映射在谱不稳定性下的适用条件，指出远离势极值的微扰可破坏该映射。

相关度：high — 直接讨论黑洞准正规模及谱不稳定性，匹配 QNM 与经典引力重点标签；但不涉及全息。

QNM与非线性演化经典引力/广义相对论

评估基于摊销模拟的推断对引力波铃宕瞬态噪声的鲁棒性

Assessing the robustness of amortized simulation-based inference to transient noise in gravitational-wave ringdowns

Song-Tao Liu, Tian-Yang Sun, Yu-Xin Wang, Yong-Xin Zhang, Shang-Jie Jin, Jing-Fei Zhang, Xin Zhang · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.IM, hep-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：双星系统发出的引力波使我们能够在强场区对广义相对论进行精确检验。铃宕信号可用于高精度探测黑洞质量和自旋。随着当前和下一代引力波探测器的改进，发展无似然参数推断方法至关重要，尤其在面对非标准噪声、部分数据或不完整信号模型而无法使用解析似然函数时。本文提出一种摊销的基于模拟的推断策略，直接估计铃宕参数。具体而言，方法基于摊销神经后验估计，针对先验范围内的所有数据段训练后验的神经密度估计器。结果表明，训练后的摊销网络具有有效的置信覆盖，并在中等信噪比下给出与成熟马尔可夫链方法一致的后验。在较高信噪比下，恢复的后验在位置上与马尔可夫链结果一致，尽管振幅后验更宽。该网络提供的推断速度快若干数量级。此外，我们评估了方法对瞬态噪声污染的鲁棒性。结果表明，毛刺注入的时序显著影响估计偏差，尤其当毛刺污染信号早期高振幅部分时。本研究不仅在测试条件下为铃宕分析提供了一个高效且经统计验证的推断框架，也为未来真实噪声环境下发展鲁棒数据处理流程奠定基础。

English: Gravitational waves (GW) emitted by binary systems allow us to perform precision tests of general relativity in the strong field regime. Ringdown signals allow for probing black hole mass and spin with high precision in GW astronomy. With improvements in current and next-generation GW detectors, developing likelihood-free parameter inference methods is crucial. This is especially important when facing challenges such as non-standard noise, partial data, or incomplete signal models that prevent the use of analytical likelihood functions. In this work, we propose an amortized simulation-based inference strategy to estimate ringdown parameters directly. Specifically, our method is based on amortized neural posterior estimation, which trains a neural density estimator of the posterior for all data segments within the prior range. The results show that our trained amortized network achieves valid confidence coverage and yields posteriors in agreement with established Markov-chain methods at moderate signal-to-noise ratios. At higher signal-to-noise ratios, the recovered posteriors agree with the Markov-chain results in location, although the amplitude posterior is broader. The network provides inference speeds that are orders of magnitude faster. Furthermore, we evaluate the robustness of the method against transient noise contamination. Our results indicate that the timing of glitch injection significantly affects estimation bias, in particular when the glitch contaminates the early, high-amplitude portion of the signal. This study not only provides an efficient and statistically validated inference framework under the tested conditions for ringdown analysis but also lays a foundation for developing robust data-processing pipelines for future GW astronomy in realistic noise environments.

一句话导读：提出摊销神经后验估计用于引力波铃宕参数直接推断，验证其统计覆盖、速度及对瞬态毛刺噪声的鲁棒性。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/引力波数据分析，直接涉及铃宕与广义相对论检验；但不涉及全息、量子引力或经典场方程理论构造，故与经典引力支线相关但方法学为主。

球对称 Einstein-Maxwell-Klein-Gordon 方程下亚极端 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞内部的稳定性与不稳定性

Stability and instability of the sub-extremal Reissner-Nordstr\"om black hole interior for the Einstein-Maxwell-Klein-Gordon equations in spherical symmetry

Maxime Van de Moortel · gr-qc, math-ph, math.AP, math.MP · v4 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们在球对称情形下证明带电黑洞内部的非线性稳定性与不稳定性结果；该黑洞在无穷远处足够快地趋近亚极端 Reissner-Nordström 背景，并存在大质量带电标量场，动机来自该设定下的强宇宙监督猜想。1. 稳定性：证明趋近 Reissner-Nordström 背景、且在事件视界上具有足够多项式衰减率的球对称特征初值，对 Einstein-Maxwell-Klein-Gordon 方程产生一个在类时无穷附近邻域内具有 Cauchy 视界的时空；若衰减更强，还证明时空度规可连续延拓到 Cauchy 视界。这推广了 Dafermos 关于球对称 Einstein-Maxwell-实标量场方程的著名稳定性结果。2. 不稳定性：对稳定性部分考虑的时空类，若标量场在事件视界上还满足多项式平均 L² 下界，则标量场在横穿 Cauchy 视界方向满足一个积分下界；由此证明非退化能量在穿过 Cauchy 视界的任意零面上无穷大，且测地矢量场的曲率在类时无穷附近于 Cauchy 视界处发散。这推广了 Luk 与 Oh 关于球对称 Einstein-Maxwell-实标量场方程的不稳定性结果。该黑洞内部的不稳定性也可视为朝解决单端渐近初值的 C² 强宇宙监督猜想迈出的一步。

English: We show non-linear stability and instability results in spherical symmetry for the interior of a charged black hole -approaching a sub-extremal Reissner-Nordstr\"om background fast enough at infinity- in presence of a massive and charged scalar field, motivated by the strong cosmic censorship conjecture in that setting : 1. Stability : We prove that spherically symmetric characteristic initial data to the Einstein-Maxwell- Klein-Gordon equations approaching a Reissner-Nordstr\"om background with a sufficiently decaying polynomial decay rate on the event horizon gives rise to a space-time possessing a Cauchy horizon in a neighbourhood of time-like infinity. Moreover if the decay is even stronger, we prove that the spacetime metric admits a continuous extension to the Cauchy horizon. This generalizes the celebrated stability result of Dafermos for Einstein-Maxwell-real-scalar-field in spherical symmetry. 2. Instability : We prove that for the class of space-times considered in the stability part, whose scalar field in addition obeys a polynomial averaged-L^2 (consistent) lower bound on the event horizon, the scalar field obeys an integrated lower bound transversally to the Cauchy horizon. As a consequence we prove that the non-degenerate energy is infinite on any null surface crossing the Cauchy horizon and the curvature of a geodesic vector field blows up at the Cauchy horizon near time-like infinity. This generalizes an instability result due to Luk and Oh for Einstein-Maxwell-real-scalar-field in spherical symmetry. This instability of the black hole interior can also be viewed as a step towards the resolution of the C^2 strong cosmic censorship conjecture for one-ended asymptotically initial data.

一句话导读：证明球对称带电黑洞内部在带电大质量标量场扰动下 Cauchy 视界附近的非线性稳定与不稳定，并推进强宇宙监督研究。

相关度：high — 论文主要属于经典引力与数学相对论，研究黑洞内部、Cauchy 视界和强宇宙监督，契合“经典引力/广义相对论”重点支线；不涉及全息或 AdS/CFT。

Mercury（Hg）：Horndeski 引力的相对论 N 体模拟

Mercury (Hg): a relativistic $N$-body simulation for Horndeski gravity

Ahmad Nouri-Zonoz, Farbod Hassani, Julian Adamek, Emilio Bellini, Martin Kunz · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, physics.comp-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们提出 Mercury，一个用于 Horndeski 引力 α-基表述下宇宙学模拟的相对论 N 体代码。Mercury 扩展了粒子-网格方案，纳入额外标量自由度的动力学以及相应的修改 Einstein 方程，这些方程由含时函数 α_K、α_B、α_M、α_T 和有效 Planck 质量 M_*^2 表示。这将我们此前对 Horndeski 子类模型（k-essence 和 kinetic gravity braiding）的实现推广到一般线性 Horndeski α-基。代码演化额外标量自由度及其有效应力-能量张量，并与非线性物质场和度规场一起演化，从而使非线性结构形成对标量场演化产生源，并反过来反馈到引力势。通过其与 hi_class 的接口，Mercury 支持现象学 α-函数参数化和协变 Horndeski 理论；我们用 nKGB、Cubic Galileon 和 Brans-Dicke 模型展示了后者。我们在线性区域对照 hi_class 验证实现，并恢复预期的非线性 k-essence 和 KGB 极限。对于本文考虑的光速 Horndeski 模型，我们展示跑动的有效 Planck 质量如何通过标量各向异性应力产生引力滑移，并修改支配矢量与张量度规扰动的摩擦项。M_*^2 的演化还修改有效暗能量密度contrast的归一化和尺度依赖，并在其功率谱上留下相应印记。当前框架为非线性 Horndeski 模拟提供了中间步骤，其中非线性标量自相互作用和屏蔽效应可在同一相对论框架内一致地纳入。

English: We present Mercury, a relativistic $N$-body code for cosmological simulations in the $\alpha$-basis formulation of Horndeski gravity. Mercury extends a particle-mesh scheme by incorporating the dynamics of the additional scalar degree of freedom and the corresponding modified Einstein equations, expressed in terms of the time-dependent functions $\alpha_{\rm K}, \alpha_{\rm B}, \alpha_{\rm M}, \alpha_{\rm T}$ and the effective Planck mass $M_*^2$. This generalises our previous implementation of Horndeski subclass models, namely $k$-essence and kinetic gravity braiding (KGB), to the general linear Horndeski $\alpha$-basis. The code evolves the additional scalar degree of freedom and its effective stress--energy tensor alongside the nonlinear matter and metric fields, so that nonlinear structure formation sources the scalar-field evolution and feeds back on the gravitational potentials. Through its interface with hi_class, Mercury supports both phenomenological $\alpha$-function parametrisations and covariant Horndeski theories; we demonstrate the latter using nKGB, the Cubic Galileon, and Brans--Dicke models. We validate the implementation against hi_class in the linear regime and recover the expected nonlinear $k$-essence and KGB limits. For the luminal Horndeski models considered here, we show how a running effective Planck mass generates gravitational slip through the scalar anisotropic stress and modifies the friction terms governing the vector and tensor metric perturbations. The evolution of $M_*^2$ also modifies the normalisation and scale dependence of the effective dark-energy density contrast, leaving a corresponding imprint on its power spectrum. The present framework provides an intermediate step towards nonlinear Horndeski simulations, in which nonlinear scalar self-interactions and screening effects can be incorporated consistently within the same relativistic framework.

一句话导读：介绍并验证用于 Horndeski 引力 α-基宇宙学非线性结构形成的相对论 N 体代码 Mercury。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/修改引力宇宙学，涉及 Horndeski 理论和非线性 N 体模拟；不与全息对偶直接相关。

利用X射线三重态评估中子星XTEJ 1807-294的质量和角动量

Mass and angular momentum assessement of the neutron star XTEJ 1807-294 using X-ray triplets

Radostina P. Tasheva, Ivan Z. Stefanov · astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.SR, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：吸积毫秒脉冲星XTEJ 1807-294的X射线功率密度谱包含若干同时观测到的准周期振荡（QPO）三重态：一个下kHz QPO、一个上kHz QPO和一个低频QPO。这一事实使我们能够评估中心中子星的质量和比角动量。我们还应用误差传播公式获得恒星参数的区间估计。计算用几种不同的kHz QPO模型重复进行。不同三重态所得界限的一致程度可能为所用模型的可行性提供信息。

English: The X-ray power-density spectrum of the accreting millisecond pulsar XTEJ 1807-294 contains several triplets of simultaneously observed quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs): a lower and a upper kHz QPOs and a low-frequency QPO. This fact allows us to assess the mass and the specific angular momentum of the central neutron star. We also apply an error propagation formula to obtain interval estimates of the parameters of the star. The calculations are repeated with several different models for the kHz QPOs. The degree of consistency of the bounds obtained with the different triplets may provide information for the viability of the used models.

一句话导读：利用XTEJ 1807-294的QPO三重态估计中子星质量与比角动量，并比较不同kHz QPO模型的一致性。

相关度：low — 主题为中子星QPO参数估计，与全息/量子引力及用户列出的全息支线无直接关系；仅在天体物理中子星意义上接近经典引力标签。

短伽马射线暴相对论磁流体喷流的引力波信号

Gravitational-Wave Signal from a Short GRB Relativistic MHD Jet

Or Zuriel, Dmitry D. Ofengeim, Omer Bromberg, Eyal Efrima, Tsvi Piran · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们分析由短伽马射线暴喷流相对论磁流体力学模拟产生的引力波信号，其参数对应 GRB 170817A。通过直接积分该 MHD 模拟的线性化 Einstein 方程推迟解，计算在 GRB 隐藏中心引擎中产生的信号。将引力波信号分为重子贡献和电磁贡献，并识别其主要特征的物理来源。波形为记忆型，低频满足 h(f) ∝ 1/f，并由对应 GRB 持续时间的能量注入时间设定约 0.5 Hz 的拐点。一个显著的早期峰反映磁力线打开以及喷流起始时对抛射物的赤道踢，表明引力波信号可揭示 GRB 引擎深处否则无法观测的过程。信号对远离喷流轴的观测者最强。总辐射引力波能量仅约 10^42 erg，约为喷流能量的 10^-9；它按 E^2/τ 缩放，其中 τ 为最短变化时标，因此该结果是下限。将预测谱与当前和未来探测器灵敏度比较，发现类似 GW170817 的事件可被 DECIGO 从最多 10 Mpc、Einstein Telescope 从 1 Mpc 边缘探测。该模拟中注入的 Poynting 通量是光滑的；若引擎在毫秒时标上变化（一些 GRB 模型预言如此），将显著增加引力波能量和可探测性。

English: We analyze the gravitational-wave (GW) signal produced by a relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) simulation of a short GRB jet with parameters corresponding to those of GRB 170817A. The signal that is generated in the hidden central engine of the GRBs is computed by direct integration of the retarded solution of the linearized Einstein equations of an MHD simulation. Separating the GW signal into its baryonic and electromagnetic contributions, we identify the physical origin of its dominant features. The waveform is of the memory type, with $h(f) \propto 1/f$ at low frequencies and a knee at $\sim 0.5-0.5$Hz set by the energy injection time, which corresponds to the GRB duration. A pronounced early peak reflects the opening of the magnetic field lines and the equatorial kick given to the ejecta at the onset of the jet, demonstrating that the GW signal can reveal processes deep in the GRB engine that cannot be observed otherwise. The signal is strongest for observers far from the jet axis. The total radiated GW energy is only $\sim 10^{42}$ erg, about $10^{-9}$ of the jet energy; it scales as ${\cal E}^2/\tau$ with the shortest variability time $\tau$, so this is a lower limit. Comparing the predicted spectrum with the sensitivity of current and future detectors, we find that events similar to GW170817 are marginally detectable by DECIGO from up to 10 Mpc and by the Einstein Telescope from 1 Mpc. The injected Poynting flux in this simulation was smooth. An engine variable on a millisecond timescale, as predicted in some GRB models, would substantially increase the GW energy and detectability.

一句话导读：相对论 MHD 模拟显示短 GRB 喷流可产生记忆型引力波信号，但能量低、探测需未来探测器。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/引力波与相对论天体物理，不属于全息研究；但与用户列出的“经典引力/广义相对论”重点支线相关。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

通过 p-调和时间函数研究宇宙学刚性

Cosmological rigidity via $p$-harmonic temporal functions

Argam Ohanyan · math.DG, gr-qc, math.AP · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们证明了一个分裂定理：若宇宙学时空允许某个 p<1 的全局 p-调和 Cauchy 时间函数，则无需使用类时线即可得到分裂结论。在 Bartnik 的宇宙学且类时测地完备时空背景下，我们建立了一个 Liouville 型无界性结果，表明 p<1 的全局 p-调和时间函数必为 Cauchy 时间函数。此外，利用椭圆偏微分方程理论，我们证明局部 Lipschitz 的 p<1 p-调和时间函数实际上是光滑的。总之，我们的工作将 Bartnik 宇宙学分裂猜想的解决归结为：对某个 p<1，p-d'Alembert 方程在局部 Lipschitz 时间函数类中具有全局弱可解性。

English: We prove a splitting theorem for cosmological spacetimes which admit a globally $p$-harmonic Cauchy temporal function for some $p < 1$, without making any use of timelike lines. In Bartnik's context of cosmological and timelike geodesically complete spacetimes, we establish a Liouville-type unboundedness result which shows that globally $p$-harmonic temporal functions, $p < 1$, are necessarily Cauchy temporal. Moreover, using the theory of elliptic PDEs, we show that locally Lipschitz $p$-harmonic temporal functions with $p < 1$ are in fact smooth. Altogether, our work reduces the resolution of Bartnik's cosmological splitting conjecture to the global weak solvability of the $p$-d'Alembert equation for some $p < 1$ in the class of locally Lipschitz temporal functions.

一句话导读：用 p-调和时间函数与椭圆 PDE 研究宇宙学时空分裂及 Bartnik 猜想。

基于轨道角动量的引力波探测器的可探测性

Detectability of an orbital-angular-momentum-based gravitational wave detector

Haorong Wu, Xilong Fan · physics.ins-det, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM, gr-qc, physics.optics · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：光子的轨道角动量（OAM）可通过OAM态之间的跃迁来探测引力波。本文提出一种读出方案，并评估基于OAM的引力波探测器在1 mHz至100 Hz频段的噪声预算，包括激光强度噪声、激光相位噪声、模式杂质噪声和散粒噪声。对于对称叠加OAM态，引力波诱导的跃迁振幅满足α_+=-α_-^*，而噪声诱导的模式串扰系数满足n_+=n_-^*，因此引力波信号构成差分模贡献，激光强度噪声和模式杂质噪声作为共模贡献可通过平衡探测抑制。在固定束腰约束下，较短臂长允许更大的傍轴参数，从而降低等效角噪声应变灵敏度。采用L=4 m、N=160次往返、傍轴参数γ=0.1、分束器入射激光功率P_BS=10 kW、波长λ_opt=1064 nm，并使用代表性噪声谱，发现对于红移z=1、源架分量质量M_src=10^4 M_⊙的等质量双黑洞引力波，平衡探测在失配参数ε=10^-3时信噪比为24.7，在ε=10^-5时为239，后者灵敏度接近散粒噪声极限。结果表明紧凑光学配置和强共模抑制对实现基于OAM的引力波探测器很重要。

English: The orbital angular momentum (OAM) of light provides a new approach to probing gravitational waves (GWs) through transitions between OAM states. Here, we assess the detectability of an OAM-based GW detector by developing a readout scheme and evaluating its noise budget over the frequency range from $1~\mathrm{mHz}$ to $100~\mathrm{Hz}$, including laser-intensity noise, laser-phase noise, mode-impurity noise, and shot noise. For a symmetric superposition OAM state, the GW-induced transition amplitudes satisfy $\alpha_+=-\alpha_-^*$, whereas the noise-induced mode-crosstalk coefficients obey $n_+=n_-^*$. Hence, the GW-induced signal forms a differential-mode contribution, while laser-intensity and mode-impurity noises enter as common-mode contributions, enabling their suppression by the balanced detection. Under a fixed beam-waist constraint, a shorter arm permits a larger paraxial parameter, reducing the equivalent angular-noise strain sensitivity. We adopt an arm length of $L=4\,\mathrm{m}$, $N=160$ round trips, a paraxial parameter of $\gamma=0.1$, a laser power incident on the beam splitter of $P_{\mathrm{BS}}=10\,\mathrm{kW}$, and a wavelength of $\lambda_{\mathrm{opt}}=1064\,\mathrm{nm}$. Using representative noise spectra, we find that, for GWs from an equal-mass binary black hole with source-frame component masses of $M_{\mathrm{src}}=10^{4}\,M_{\odot}$ at redshift $z=1$, balanced detection yields an SNR of $24.7$ for an imbalance parameter of $\epsilon=10^{-3}$ and $239$ for $\epsilon=10^{-5}$, with the sensitivity approaching the shot-noise limit in the latter case. These results highlight the importance of a compact optical configuration and strong common-mode rejection for realizing an OAM-based GW detector.

一句话导读：本文设计读出方案并评估基于光轨道角动量跃迁的引力波探测器在1 mHz–100 Hz的噪声预算与探测能力。

基于WaSABI-C旋进与数值相对论波形混合的准圆、自旋对齐、非对称质量双黑洞引力波波形代理模型

Surrogate model of gravitational waveforms for quasi-circular, spin-aligned, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries based on the hybridization of WaSABI-C inspirals with numerical relativity waveforms

H. L. Iglesias, D. M. Shoemaker · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：随着自力波形模型（如WaSABI-C旋进模型）引入自旋对齐动力学，以及SXS合作组第三版公开数值相对论模拟目录的发布，自旋、非对称质量双黑洞的引力波建模取得了显著进展。据此，我们提出WaSABINRHybSur2dq15，这是首个融合自力理论、后牛顿理论和数值相对论信息的代理模型。该模型在65个混合波形上训练，这些波形对应准圆、自旋对齐、非对称质量双黑洞，质量比覆盖1:1.25至1:20，主黑洞自旋对齐分量范围为-0.5至0.5。混合波形由一组WaSABI-C旋进波形与第三版SXS公开目录中的数值相对论波形生成。模型覆盖参数域1.25≤q≤15和|χ_{1z}|≤0.5，并包含(2,2)、(2,1)、(3,3)、(4,4)和(5,5)自旋加权球谐模式。对于总质量M≳111.5 M⊙，模型覆盖整个LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA频段（f_low=20 Hz）。我们发现，对于总质量在80 M⊙≤M≤300 M⊙范围内，WaSABINRHybSur2dq15模型能以小于10^{-3}的失配率忠实再现混合波形。

English: With the incorporation of aligned-spin dynamics into self-force waveform models, such as the WaSABI-C inspiral model, and with the release of the SXS collaboration's third public catalog of NR simulations, the gravitational waveform modeling community has made significant advances in the modeling of spinning, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries. In light of these developments, we present WaSABINRHybSur2dq15, the first surrogate model to incorporate information from self-force theory, post-Newtonian theory, and numerical relativity. The model is trained on 65 hybrid waveforms for quasi-circular, spin-aligned, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries with mass ratios spanning 1:1.25 to 1:20 and aligned-spins for the primary black hole ranging -0.5 to 0.5. The hybrid waveforms are generated from a set of WaSABI-C inspirals and NR waveforms from the third SXS public catalog. The model covers the parameter domain $1.25 \leq q \leq 15$ and $|\chi_{1z}| \leq 0.5$, and includes the $(2,2),\, (2,1),\, (3,3),\, (4,4),$ and $(5,5)$ spin-weighted spherical harmonic modes. The model also spans the entire LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) frequency band (with $f_{\text{low}}=20$ Hz) for total masses $M \gtrsim 111.5\,M_{\odot}$. We find that the WaSABINRHybSur2dq15 model faithfully reproduces the hybrid waveforms with mismatches less than $10^{-3}$ for total masses in the range $80\,M_{\odot} \leq M \leq 300\,M_{\odot}$.

一句话导读：提出首个融合自力、后牛顿与数值相对论波形的双黑洞引力波代理模型WaSABINRHybSur2dq15，并在质量比与自旋对齐参数范围内实现低失配。

O3 LIGO数据中武仙座X-1连续引力波的试点搜索：隐藏马尔可夫模型的增强

Pilot search for continuous gravitational waves from Hercules X-1 in O3 LIGO data: enhancements to a hidden Markov model

Nicholas Kah Yean Low, Andrew Melatos · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：利用LIGO第三次观测运行数据，对武仙座X-1恒星振荡产生的连续引力波进行半相干搜索，采用隐藏马尔可夫模型追踪信号频率的随机游走。主要目标是实现并测试若干面向未来搜索的算法增强，包括二阶投影半长轴修正、轨道偏心率修正、搜索子带宽度调整，以及用非零轨道周期导数传播电磁测定的轨道参数。通过向高斯噪声注入合成信号验证增强，对类武仙座X-1目标将灵敏度深度提高约14%。在60–200 Hz频段，试点搜索得到四个通过五级否决程序的候选体，终止频率分别为76.41、109.47、144.40和189.24 Hz。在95%置信度下，最大可探测波应变的上限在174 Hz处任意设定为9.3×10^-26，假设偏振均匀先验。

English: A semi-coherent search is conducted for continuous gravitational waves from stellar oscillations in Hercules X-1 with data from Observing Run 3 of the Laser Interferometer Gravitational-wave Observatory, using an established hidden Markov model algorithm to track stochastic wandering of the signal frequency. The primary goal is to implement and test several algorithmic enhancements relevant to future searches, including a second-order projected semi-major axis correction, an orbital eccentricity correction, an adjustment to the width of a search subband, and propagation of the electromagnetically measured orbital parameters with a nonzero orbital period derivative. The enhancements are validated by injecting synthetic signals into Gaussian noise and improve the sensitivity depth by $\approx$ 14% for a Hercules X-1-like target. In the 60--200 Hz band, the pilot search yields four candidates that survive a five-level veto procedure, whose terminating frequencies are 76.41 Hz, 109.47 Hz, 144.40 Hz, and 189.24 Hz. The frequentist upper limit on the maximum detectable wave strain at 95% confidence is arbitrarily set at 174 Hz and found to be $9.3 \times 10^{-26}$, assuming a uniform prior on polarization.

一句话导读：用增强HMM在LIGO O3数据中试点搜索武仙座X-1连续引力波并给出候选与上限。

相关度：low — 该文是连续引力波观测搜索与数据分析算法增强，不属于全息或量子引力研究；仅与用户标签中的经典引力/广义相对论支线有外围重叠。

准拓扑引力的奇点定理

A Singularity Theorem for Quasi-Topological Gravities

Francesco Di Filippo · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：准拓扑引力理论近来受到大量关注，因为该类中的若干理论以非奇异黑洞作为真空球对称解。这激发了一种期望：这些理论能够解决奇点问题并描述黑洞核心。然而，已知解具有柯西视界，它标志着可预测性的终点，因为初始数据不能唯一决定该视界之后的延拓。本文表明，Penrose 奇点定理可以推广到所有已知的、具有非奇异黑洞解的准拓扑理论类。我们证明，满足零能量条件的球对称坍缩一旦形成囚禁球面，就必然导致未来零测地线不完备。这意味着这些理论缺乏可预测性并非真空解的假象，并且在黑洞内部动力学研究方面与广义相对论一样有问题。

English: Quasi-topological theories of gravity have recently attracted substantial attention as several theories in this class have a non-singular black hole as a vacuum and spherically symmetric solution. This motivates the expectation that these theories can provide a resolution to the singularity problem and a description of black holes cores. The known solutions, however, possess a Cauchy horizon which denotes the end of predictability of the theory, as the initial data do not determine a unique continuation past this type of horizon. In this paper, we show that Penrose singularity theorem can be extended to all the known classes of quasi-topological theories that have non-singular black holes as solutions. We prove that spherical collapse satisfying the null energy condition necessarily leads to future null geodesic incompleteness once a trapped sphere forms. This implies that the lack of predictability of the theories is not an artefact of vacuum solutions and these theories are as problematic as general relativity for the dynamical studies of black hole interiors.

一句话导读：将 Penrose 奇点定理推广到具有非奇异黑洞解的准拓扑引力，表明球对称坍缩仍导致零测地线不完备。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的黑洞奇点定理研究，与用户的经典引力支线直接相关；不涉及全息、超流超导、QNM 或其他重点标签。

跨时空维度与引力理论的光环稳定性

Light Ring Stability Across Spacetime Dimensions and Theories of Gravity

Kajol Paithankar, Sanved Kolekar · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：光环定理指出，在四维稳态、轴对称、无视界超紧凑天体（UCO）中，光环（LR）成对出现；若广义相对论中零能量条件（NEC）成立，则其中一个光环是稳定的。稳定光环的存在可能触发非线性时空不稳定性，从而对UCO作为长寿命黑洞模仿者的可行性提出疑问。我们将LR存在性与稳定性定理从四维广义相对论推广到修改引力理论，特别是f(R)引力、标量-张量理论和受限Horndeski引力，并在d≥4维时空中讨论。结果表明，在四维修改引力中，LR稳定性除受NEC约束外，还受理论特定耦合参数控制；因此，在某些参数区域不保证出现稳定LR。在更高维（d>4）中，我们用拓扑论证确认LR仍成对出现；然而，拓扑荷现在与四类不同的LR相关，其中包括两类鞍点。稳定性分析进一步限制允许的LR配对，意味着与四维广义相对论不同，出现的配对不一定包含稳定LR。这些发现直接影响使用UCO检验广义相对论，表明LR稳定性对底层引力理论和时空维度高度敏感。

English: The light ring theorem states that four-dimensional stationary, axisymmetric, horizonless ultracompact objects (UCOs) possess light rings (LRs) in pairs, one of which is stable if the Null Energy Condition (NEC) holds in General Relativity (GR). The existence of a stable LR may trigger nonlinear spacetime instabilities, thereby raising questions about the viability of UCOs as long-lived black hole mimickers. We extend the LR existence and stability theorem beyond four-dimensional GR to modified gravity theories, in particular, $f(R)$ gravity, scalar-tensor theories, and restricted Horndeski gravity, in ($d \geq 4$) dimensional spacetimes. Our findings show that in four-dimensional modified gravities, the stability of LRs is governed by theory-specific coupling parameters in addition to the NEC. Consequently, a stable LR is not guaranteed to occur in certain parameter regimes. In higher dimensions ($d>4$), we confirm using topological arguments that LRs still emerge in pairs; however, the topological charges are now associated with four distinct types of LRs, including two types of saddle points. The stability analysis further constrains the allowed LR pairings, implying that, unlike in four-dimensional GR, the emergent pair does not necessarily include a stable LR. These findings directly impact tests of GR using UCOs, demonstrating that LR stability is highly sensitive to both the underlying gravitational theory and spacetime dimensionality.

一句话导读：将光环存在性与稳定性定理推广到修改引力和高维时空，发现稳定光环不再普遍保证。

Ricci-非度规全息暗能量

Ricci-Non-metric Holographic Dark Energy

Sukanta Bhunia, Prabir Rudra, Abhijit Lahiri · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文提出一种受Ricci标量R与非度规标量Q启发的全息暗能量模型。该全息暗能量的红外截断由Ricci标量与非度规标量的组合描述。这种结构很有帮助，因为演化完全依赖于宇宙当前特征，而不是其过去或未来特征。此外，由于使用了不变量，该理论变得更基本。我们构建了该模型并考察了其宇宙学特征，分析了密度参数、状态方程参数和减速参数等多个宇宙学参数的行为。结果表明，该全息暗能量模型可以描述每一个宇宙学时期，从早期宇宙的辐射期，到物质期，再到晚期暗能量主导期。根据所选参数空间，状态方程参数表明该模型可能具有精质性质、跨越幽灵分界，甚至在未来具有幽灵性质。

English: In this work we propose a holographic dark energy model inspired by the Ricci scalar $R$ and non-metricity scalar $Q$. The infrared cutoff of the holographic dark energy is described by a combination of the Ricci scalar and the non-metricity scalar. Such a structure is very helpful because the evolution is entirely dependent on the universe's current characteristics rather than its previous or future characteristics. Additionally, the theory becomes more fundamental due to the application of invariants. We have built the model and examined its cosmological characteristics. The behavior of several cosmological parameters, including the density parameter, equation of state parameter, and deceleration parameter, is examined. It is observed that the holographic dark energy model may depict every cosmological epoch, beginning with radiation in the early universe, moving on to matter, and concluding with the dark energy-dominated period in later times. Depending on the parameter spaces selected, the equation of state parameter demonstrates that the model may have quintessence nature, phantom-divide crossing, or even phantom nature in future times.

一句话导读：构建了以Ricci标量与非度规标量组合为红外截断的全息暗能量模型，并分析其宇宙学演化与状态方程行为。

相关度：low — 该文属于全息暗能量宇宙学模型，使用Ricci标量与非度规标量构造红外截断，并非AdS/CFT意义上的理论全息对偶；与用户关注的全息超流超导、p波/多分量序参量、QNM、D3-D7探针膜等支线无直接关联。

辐射对激光干涉月基天线（LILA）的影响

Effects of radiation on the Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA)

James M. Trippe, Brett N. Shapiro, Chloe A. Champagne, Brian D. Sierawski, Michael D. Hu, Volker Quetschke, Teviet Creighton, Philippe Lognonn\'e, Mark P. Panning, John W. Conklin, Robert A. Reed, Karan Jani · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：月球为中频段引力波观测提供了有前景的环境。激光干涉月基天线（LILA）结合月球形变通道（LDC）和测地线形变通道（GDC）覆盖中频段：LDC利用月球对引力波的共振响应覆盖中频段较低部分，GDC通过测量悬浮测试质量的位移覆盖较高部分。月面辐射可通过测试质量充电、动量转移和热弹性变形产生虚假信号。我们针对一个设想的100 kg熔融石英光学元件，使用Geant4输运、有限元静电力图和空间热弹性响应核估计这些贡献。太阳活动极小期银河宇宙线和月球反照中子定义背景情形；最坏日太阳高能粒子谱（1989年太阳耀斑条件）定义极端但罕见情形。比较了50至1000密耳（1.27至25.4 mm）铝等效屏蔽和两种静电驱动器电压配置。在均匀电荷近似下，采用电极配置（B）的平衡驱动显著降低线性电荷-力耦合。对平均充电电流的理想连续补偿可抑制相干电荷积累，同时引入可忽略的放电散粒噪声。有补偿时，电荷相关噪声在低频响应中占主导，而热弹性变形在较高频率占主导。累积剂量和光电探测器瞬态估计指出了额外的部件鉴定考虑。估计表明，即使在大阳耀斑条件下，只要遵循电荷感知设计，辐射噪声也不是LILA总噪声预算的重要贡献者。

English: The Moon provides a promising environment for gravitational-wave (GW) observations in the midband. The Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA) combines a lunar deformation channel (LDC) and a geodesic deformation channel (GDC) to cover the midband. The LDC uses the Moon's resonant response to GW for the lower portion of the midband while the GDC measures displacement of suspended test masses to measure the latter. Radiation on the lunar surface can produce spurious signals through test-mass charging, momentum transfer, and thermoelastic deformation. We estimate these contributions for a notional 100 kg fused-silica optic using Geant4 transport, a finite-element electrostatic force map, and a spatial thermoelastic response kernel. Solar-minimum galactic cosmic rays and lunar albedo neutrons define a background case; a worst-day solar energetic particle spectrum (1989 solar flare conditions) defines an extreme, but rare, case. Aluminum-equivalent shielding from 50 to 1000 mils (1.27 to 25.4 mm) and two electrostatic actuator voltage configurations are compared. In the uniform-charge approximation, balanced drive with electrode configuration (B) substantially reduces linear charge-to-force coupling. Ideal continuous compensation of the mean charging current suppresses coherent charge accumulation while introducing negligible discharge shot noise. With compensation, charge-related noise dominates the low-frequency response, whereas thermoelastic deformation dominates at higher frequencies. Accumulated-dose and photodetector transient estimates identify additional component-qualification considerations. Estimates indicate that even under solar flare conditions, radiation noise is not a significant contributor to LILA's overall noise budget as long as charge-aware designs are followed.

一句话导读：评估月面辐射通过测试质量充电、动量转移和热弹性变形对LILA引力波探测噪声的贡献，并指出电荷感知设计下辐射噪声不显著。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及引力波探测与经典引力实验噪声预算，属于经典引力/引力波观测支线；但不涉及全息、量子引力或用户多数全息重点标签。

Bumblebee AdS黑洞的引力动力学与逆向光线追踪阴影

Gravitational dynamics and backward ray-traced shadows of bumblebee AdS black holes

Muhammad Yasir, Senyue Lou, Zelin Zhang, Abdul Jawad, Faren Wang · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：我们研究了bumblebee AdS黑洞事件视界附近圆轨道上大质量测地线粒子所发射光子的总观测频移。通过支配宇宙演化和特征性质的动力学方程，考察bumblebee场的宇宙学效应。此外，bumblebee场作为各向异性源并选择优先空间方向，产生宇宙学各向异性。我们用有效势方法建立最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）的特征方程。进一步分析致力于建立黑洞质量与吸积盘发射光子观测红移/蓝移之间的函数关系。最后，我们使用Arcmancer代码实现的逆向光线追踪过程研究bumblebee AdS黑洞的阴影，其中光子测地线从观测者位置初始化并向时间反演积分以生成黑洞图像，通常由附近吸积流的电磁辐射照亮。

English: We investigate the total observed frequency shift of photons emitted by massive geodesic particles in circular orbits near the event horizon of bumblebee AdS black holes. The cosmological effects of the bumblebee field are investigated through dynamical equations governing the evolution and characteristic features of the Universe. In addition, the bumblebee field generates cosmological anisotropies by acting as a source of anisotropy and selecting a preferred spatial direction. We formulate the characteristic equations for the innermost stable circular orbit using the effective potential approach. Further analysis is devoted to establishing a functional relationship between the BH mass and the observed redshift and blueshift of photons emitted from the accretion disk. Finally, we study the shadow cast by the bumblebee AdS BH using the backward ray tracing process implemented in the Arcmancer code, where photon geodesics are initialized at the observers location and integrated backward in time to generate BH images, typically illuminated by electromagnetic radiation from nearby accretion flows.

一句话导读：研究bumblebee AdS黑洞附近的测地线频移、ISCO及逆向光线追踪阴影。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，研究AdS黑洞测地线、频移与阴影；未涉及全息对偶或所列全息专题。

黑洞并合中动力学视界的强引力物理及其在引力辐射中的印记

Strong gravity physics of dynamical horizons in black hole mergers and its imprint in their gravitational radiation

Vaishak Prasad · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：在双黑洞并合中，引力波的旋进部分对应两个不同视界相互绕转，并合与铃宕信号对应最终视界形成并趋于平衡。然而，这三个阶段中视界几何与观测波形之间的关系仍缺乏细致理解。本论文表明，熟知的旋进啁啾波形在黑洞视界上有明确对应物，即视界处出射零测地线的剪切；该剪切行为很像致密双星并合波形，频率和振幅增加，且由视界估计的系统参数与由波形估计的一致。随后，作者在若干双黑洞并合模拟中追踪动力学视界的源多极矩，并计算轴对称模式的相应Love数；与非自旋黑洞的场潮汐Love数不同，这些量不消失，并给出证据表明至少在中等质量比下其行为具有普适性。论文还将研究推广到非轴对称矩，表明动力学视界编码了双星动力学的详细信息，其源多极矩与零无穷处引力场强相关。最后，论文研究并合期间形成的外共同动力学视界及其与出射引力波的关系，展示变形的共同视界如何通过吸收带有准正规模铃宕特征的引力辐射趋近最终Kerr态；其引力场与未来零无穷处具有相似的多极结构，并指出一些需要解决的局限、不一致和异常。

English: In a binary black hole merger, the inspiral portion of the gravitational waveform corresponds to two distinct horizons orbiting each other, and the merger and ringdown signals correspond to the final horizon being formed and settling down to equilibrium. However, we still lack a detailed understanding of the relation between the horizon geometry in these three regimes and the observed waveform. In this thesis, we show that the well-known inspiral chirp waveform has a clear counterpart on black hole horizons, namely the shear of the outgoing null rays at the horizon. The shear behaves very much like a compact binary coalescence waveform with increasing frequency and amplitude, and the parameters of the system estimated from the horizon agree with those estimated from the waveform. We then track the source multipole moments of the dynamical horizons in simulations of several binary black hole mergers and calculate the corresponding Love numbers for axisymmetric modes. Unlike the (field) tidal Love numbers of non-spinning black holes, these do not vanish. We present evidence that, at least for modest mass ratios, their behaviour is universal. We extend this to the non-axisymmetric moments and show that the dynamical horizons encode detailed information about the dynamics of the binary, and that their source multipole moments are strongly correlated with the gravitational field at null infinity. Finally, we study the outer common dynamical horizon formed during the merger and its relationship with the outgoing gravitational waves, and show how the deformed common horizon approaches the final Kerr state by absorbing gravitational radiation with signatures of quasi-normal ringing. Its gravitational field shares a similar multipolar structure as that at future null infinity, and we point out some limitations, inconsistencies, and anomalies that need to be addressed.

一句话导读：该论文研究双黑洞并合中动力学视界的剪切、多极矩与Love数，并分析其与引力波、共同视界演化和准正规模铃宕的联系。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典广义相对论与黑洞并合数值研究，直接涉及QNM/铃宕和非线性演化，与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”和“QNM与非线性演化”高度相关；不属于全息或AdS/CFT研究。

经典引力/广义相对论QNM与非线性演化

用多信使引力波探测隐藏维度

Probing Hidden Dimensions with Multimessenger Gravitational Waves

Hanlin Song, Minghui Du, Peng Xu · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：时空维数是自然的基本属性，但我们的四维时空是否嵌入更高维几何仍未在实验上解决。引力波通过双星并合的强场动力学为紧致额外维度提供了直接探针。本文在紧致化Kaluza-Klein框架下研究额外维度对引力波产生的修正，推导牛顿阶守恒双星动力学以及引力波能流的领头阶额外维度修正，并在参数化后爱因斯坦框架（ppE）中给出相应的波形修正。结合此前由内部体积扰动得到的1PN振幅修正，我们构建了适用于地面和空间探测器的修正引力波波形模板。为缓解引力波参数估计中的参数简并，我们将GW170817的应变数据与独立电磁观测结合进行多信使贝叶斯分析，得到现象学维度参数n的95%可信上限n<0.11。我们进一步研究太极对注入的多信使大质量黑洞双星观测，发现该上限可提升近两个数量级至n<0.00178。这些结果凸显了多信使引力波观测在强场和动力学区域探测额外维度效应并检验基础引力的潜力。

English: The dimensionality of spacetime is a fundamental property of nature, yet whether our observed four-dimensional spacetime is embedded in a higher-dimensional geometry remains experimentally unresolved. Gravitational waves (GWs) offer a direct probe of compact extra dimensions through the strong-field dynamics of binary coalescences. In this work, we investigate extra-dimensional modifications to GW generation within a compactified Kaluza--Klein framework. We derive the Newtonian conservative binary dynamics and the leading-order extra-dimensional correction to the GW energy flux and formulate the corresponding waveform modifications within the parameterized post-Einsteinian (ppE) framework. Combining these results with the previously derived 1PN amplitude correction arising from perturbations of the internal volume, we construct modified GW waveform templates for ground- and space-based detectors. To alleviate parameter degeneracies in GW parameter estimation, we combine the observed strain data of GW170817 with independent electromagnetic observations in a multimessenger Bayesian analysis, yielding a 95\% credible upper bound of $n<0.11$ on the phenomenological dimensional parameter. We further investigate injected multimessenger massive black hole binary observations with Taiji, finding that the upper bound could be improved by nearly two orders of magnitude to $n<0.00178$. These results highlight the potential of multimessenger GW observations to probe extra-dimensional effects and test fundamental gravity in the strong-field and dynamical regime.

一句话导读：在紧致Kaluza-Klein框架下推导额外维度对引力波产生与波形的修正，并用多信使贝叶斯分析给出维度参数上限。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/引力波与额外维度现象学，不涉及全息或量子引力；与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”相关，但与全息各研究支线无直接关系。

史瓦西黑洞的结构化伪谱

The structured pseudospectrum of Schwarzschild black hole

Liang-Bi Wu, Yu-Sen Zhou, Ming-Fei Ji, Xia-Yuan Liu, Libo Xie, Li-Ming Cao · gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：黑洞准正规模（QNM）的非厄米性质可导致显著的谱不稳定性。这种不稳定性自然由算符的伪谱来刻画。然而，传统伪谱分析允许任意算符扰动，这些扰动可能并不对应物理上可接受的修改。本文把结构化伪谱引入黑洞微扰理论。我们基于有效场论和Rezzolla--Zhidenko参数化构造两个具有物理动机的扰动空间，并使用两种不同范数得到它们的结构化伪谱。数值结果表明，施加物理约束会显著降低可达到的谱不稳定性，尤其对高阶泛音。更重要的是，在足够小的扰动尺度ε下，围绕孤立QNM的结构化伪谱呈现近似椭圆轮廓，其偏好方向同时依赖于模式和扰动空间，揭示出超越传统伪谱熟悉的圆形行为的各向异性结构。

English: The non-Hermitian nature of black hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) can give rise to remarkable spectrum instability. Such instability is naturally characterized by the pseudospectrum for the operator. However, conventional pseudospectrum analyses permit arbitrary operator perturbations that may not correspond to physically admissible modifications. In this work, we introduce the structured pseudospectrum into black hole perturbation theory. We construct two physically motivated perturbation spaces based on effective field theory and the Rezzolla--Zhidenko parametrization, and get their structured pseudospectra using two distinct norms. Our numerical results show that imposing physical constraints substantially reduces the accessible spectrum instability, especially for higher overtones. More importantly, at sufficiently small perturbation scales $\varepsilon$, the structured pseudospectrum around isolated QNMs exhibits approximately elliptical contours with preferred directions that depend on both the mode and the perturbation space, revealing an anisotropic structure beyond the familiar circular behavior of the conventional pseudospectrum.

一句话导读：将结构化伪谱引入黑洞微扰理论，在物理约束扰动空间下分析史瓦西黑洞QNM谱不稳定性。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究史瓦西黑洞准正规模的谱不稳定性与伪谱方法，属于经典黑洞微扰和QNM分析，与用户重点标签中的QNM与经典引力方向高度相关。

q-变形振子与双标度 SYK 的 Lee-Yang 定理

Lee-Yang theorems for $q$-deformed oscillators and doubly-scaled SYK

Arpon Basu, Siddhant Midha · quant-ph, math-ph, math.MP, math.PR · v1 · 更新 2026-11-03

中文摘要：本文证明 q-变形振子的真空配分函数 L_q(z)=⟨0|e^{zT_q}|0⟩（其中 T_q=a_q+a_q^*）具有 Lee-Yang 性质：对所有 q∈[-1,1)，L_q 的全部零点位于虚轴上，从而证明了 Okuyama'23 的猜想。利用 q-变形振子与双标度区域中 Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) 哈密顿量之间的联系，作者证明退火 SYK 配分函数在热力学极限下满足 Lee-Yang 性质，即其全部 Fisher 零点位于虚轴上。由此进一步证明淬火 SYK 配分函数以高概率具有恒定宽度的无零点条带，严格补充了 Zlokapa 与 Kiani'26 的大 k 复本计算。为证明 L_q 的虚根性，文章将 φ_q(z):=L_q(i z) 表示为“q-变形”匹配多项式的局部一致极限，并证明这些 q-变形多项式具有实根，由此得到 φ_q 实根且属于 Laguerre-Pólya 类。q-变形多项式的实根性还给出 q-变形匹配矩 M_ℓ(q):=∑_{π∈P_2(2ℓ)} q^{cr(π)} 的新对数凹性关系。证明通过 Touchard-Riordan 公式导出联系其 q-导数与 z-导数的偏微分方程，并分析相应根的运动。

English: We show that the vacuum partition function of the $q$-deformed oscillator \[ L_q(z):= \langle0|e^{zT_q}|0\rangle, \qquad T_q := \mathsf{a}_q + \mathsf{a}_q^* \] satisfies the Lee-Yang property, i.e., all zeros of $L_q$ lie on the imaginary line for all $q\in[-1, 1)$, proving the conjectures of Okuyama'23. Using the connection between $q$-deformed oscillators and the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian in the doubly-scaled regime, we show that the annealed SYK partition function satisfies the Lee-Yang property in the thermodynamic limit, that is, all of its Fisher zeros are on the imaginary axis. As a result, we show that the quenched SYK partition function admits a zero-free strip of constant width with high probability, rigorously complementing the large$-k$ replica calculations of Zlokapa and Kiani'26. We show the imaginary-rootedness of $L_q$ by expressing $\varphi_q(z):= L_q(\mathbf{i} z)$ as a locally uniform limit of ``$q$-deformed'' matching polynomials and then showing that those $q$-deformed polynomials are real-rooted. This establishes that $\varphi_q$ is real-rooted, and in fact belongs to the \emph{Laguerre-P\'olya} class. The real-rootedness of the $q$-deformed polynomials yields novel log-concavity relations on the \emph{$q$-deformed matching moments} \[M_\ell(q):= \sum_{\pi\in\mathcal{P}_2(2\ell)}q^{\operatorname{cr}(\pi)}.\] We show the real-rootedness of the $q$-deformed polynomials by using the Touchard--Riordan formula to derive a partial differential equation relating their $q$-derivatives to their $z$-derivatives, and then analyzing the resulting motion of their roots.

一句话导读：证明 q-变形振子与双标度 SYK 配分函数的 Lee-Yang 零点性质，并导出 q-匹配矩的对数凹性。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及双标度 SYK 与 q-变形振子，属于 SYK/量子引力相关模型；但摘要核心是 Lee-Yang 零点、实根性与对数凹性等数学结果，未直接讨论 AdS/CFT 字典或用户重点关注的全息超流、经典引力等。
