极端视界对度规扰动的放大

Amplification of metric perturbations by extremal horizons

Samuel E. Gralla, Sepehr Salamat · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：通常情况下，落入黑洞的观察者在穿过视界时不会感到任何特殊之处。本文发现一个有趣的例外：在极端 Kerr-Newman（KN）时空的大部分参数范围内，随视界共转的扰动在视界附近被增强，使得落入的观察者在进入黑洞时经历异常大的潮汐形变。当黑洞外部存在持续源，且该源本身共转或在相关频率 ω=mΩ_H（m 为方位角数，Ω_H 为视界频率）处具有离散 Fourier 支撑时，就会产生这类扰动。在精确共转时，增强在形式上为无穷大；我们使用近共转模式来保持微扰论可控。其物理本质是极端极限下离散自相似性的出现，复标度权重形式为 -1/2±iα（α 为实数）。这类似于黑洞 Meissner 效应，区别在于近视界场被增强而非屏蔽。我们数值计算了极端 KN 黑洞耦合 gravitoelectromagnetic（GEM）扰动的标度指数，并表明复指数出现在 Q<Q_*、Q_*≈.93M 的参数范围内。这些指数还预言了 KN 时空的一般 GEM 扰动在视界上和视界外的衰减与增长率（Aretakis 效应）。共转扰动的增强可视为一种驱动 Aretakis 不稳定性。

English: Under normal circumstances, an observer falling into a black hole feels nothing special upon crossing the horizon. In this paper we find an intriguing exception: For most of the parameter range of the extremal Kerr-Newman (KN) spacetime, horizon co-rotating perturbations are enhanced near the horizon, such that an infalling observer experiences an anomalously large tidal deformation as they enter the black hole. Such perturbations arise when there is a persistent source outside the black hole that is either co-rotating itself or has discrete Fourier support at the associated frequencies $\omega=m\Omega_H$ (where $m$ is the azimuthal number and $\Omega_H$ is the horizon frequency). The enhancement is formally infinite at precise co-rotation, and we work with a nearly co-rotating mode to keep perturbation theory under control. The underlying physics is the emergence of discrete self-similarity in the extremal limit, with complex scaling weights of the form $-1/2\pm i\alpha$ for real $\alpha$. It is analogous to the black hole Meissner effect, except that the near-horizon field is enhanced rather than screened. We numerically calculate the scaling exponents for coupled gravitoelectromagnetic (GEM) perturbations of extremal KN black holes and show that the complex exponents arise in the parameter range $Q < Q_*$ with $Q_*\approx.93M$. These exponents also predict the decay and growth rates (Aretakis effect) of generic GEM perturbations of the KN spacetime, both on and off the horizon. The enhancement of co-rotating perturbations can be viewed as a driven Aretakis instability.

一句话导读：极端 Kerr-Newman 黑洞近视界的共转度规扰动被放大，使落入观察者经历异常潮汐形变，并可由复标度指数与驱动 Aretakis 不稳定性描述。

相关度：medium — 论文研究极端 KN 黑洞的经典微扰增强、复标度指数与 Aretakis 不稳定性，属于经典引力及 QNM/扰动演化相关支线；不涉及全息对偶或用户重点的全息超流、相变成核等方向。

六阶后牛顿阶的远区引力相互作用

Far-Zone Gravitational Interactions at the Sixth Post-Newtonian Order

Giacomo Brunello · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：我们在广义相对论有效场论中计算致密双星在六阶后牛顿（6PN）阶的非线性远区动力学。我们推导了因果 prescription 与保守 prescription 下一般多极源完整的 O(G^2) 远区作用量。除已知的 tails 与 failed tails 外，它还包含由辐射波的非线性记忆产生的五个新扇区。在散射轨道上，它们含有一个由角动量平衡固定的、时间非局域的边界项。同一构造给出辐射波形，其能量与角动量通量平衡作用量的损失。对于双星，6PN 动力学还需要低阶扇区的 PN 修正、迭代辐射反作用，以及复合源曲率中的二次耦合；我们通过将引力 Compton 振幅与组成成分的相应振幅匹配来确定该耦合系数。我们计算了两辐射贡献对总散射角在第四、第五和第六 post-Minkowskian（PM）阶的贡献，以及对保守角在 4PM 和 5PM 阶的贡献，并保留到 6PN 的整数-PN 项。对于保守角，我们给出 local 与 gamma-3 两种 prescription。结合 tail 与已知势贡献，我们的结果重现了保守和总 4PM 角以及保守 5PM 角的第二自 force 部分。计入耗散 tail 后，我们重现了通过 5PN 的总 5PM 角，并确定了其新的 6PN 系数。在 6PM 阶，两辐射贡献对总角的贡献将已知的 5PN 结果推广到 6PN。

English: We compute the nonlinear far-zone dynamics of compact binaries at the sixth post-Newtonian (6PN) order in the effective field theory of general relativity. We derive the complete $\mathcal{O}(G^2)$ far-zone action of a generic multipolar source in both the causal and the conservative prescriptions. Besides the known tails and failed tails, it contains five new sectors generated by the nonlinear memory of the emitted waves. On scattering orbits, they contain a boundary term, nonlocal in time, fixed by the balance of angular momentum. The same construction yields the emitted waveforms, whose energy and angular-momentum fluxes balance the losses of the action. For the binary, the 6PN dynamics also requires PN corrections to the lower-order sectors, the iterated radiation reaction, and a coupling quadratic in the curvature of the composite source, whose coefficient we fix by matching the gravitational Compton amplitude to that of the constituents. We compute the two-radiation contributions to the total scattering angle at fourth, fifth and sixth post-Minkowskian (PM) order and to the conservative angle at 4PM and 5PM, retaining the integer-PN terms through 6PN. For the conservative angle, we give both the local and $\gamma$-3 prescriptions. Combined with the tail and the known potential contributions, our results reproduce the conservative and total 4PM angles and the second-self-force part of the conservative 5PM angle. Including the dissipative tail, we recover the total 5PM angle through 5PN and determine its new 6PN coefficient. At 6PM, the two-radiation contribution to the total angle extends the known 5PN result to 6PN.

一句话导读：在广义相对论有效场论中计算致密双星远区 6PN 非线性动力学及散射角。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论与后牛顿引力波理论，匹配用户经典引力支线，但不涉及 AdS/CFT 或全息。

耦合Gauss-Bonnet项的多场宇宙学中的新效应

New Effects in Multifield Cosmology Coupled to the Gauss-Bonnet Term

Lilia Anguelova, Sonia Paban · hep-th, astro-ph.CO · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：我们研究了耦合Gauss-Bonnet项的多场宇宙学的快速转向机制。我们在双场情形下推导了暴胀Hubble慢滚参数与势慢滚参数之间相应的修改关系。这些关系表明，引入Gauss-Bonnet耦合拓宽了实现快速转向暴胀以及满足swampland猜想的可能性。我们还推导了一个将标量场空间度规与标量势及Gauss-Bonnet耦合联系起来的相容性条件，双场慢滚和快速转向暴胀解必须满足该条件。最后，我们找到了在耦合Gauss-Bonnet项的多场暗能量模型中可能出现类幻影行为的一般条件。值得注意的是，尽管我们没有考虑暗物质与暗能量之间的直接相互作用，暗物质对于幻影区域的出现仍是至关重要的。

English: We study the rapid-turn regime of multifield cosmology coupled to the Gauss-Bonnet term. We derive the corresponding modified relations between the inflationary Hubble and potential slow-roll parameters, in the two-field case. These relations show that including the Gauss-Bonnet coupling broadens the possibilities for realizing rapid turn inflation and for satisfying the swampland conjectures. We also derive a consistency condition relating the scalar field-space metric to the scalar potential and the Gauss-Bonnet coupling, which two-field slow-roll and rapid-turn inflationary solutions have to satisfy. Finally, we find general conditions under which phantom-like behavior is possible in multifield dark energy models coupled to the Gauss-Bonnet term. Notably, although we do not consider a direct interaction between dark matter and dark energy, dark matter is crucial for the occurrence of a phantom regime.

平直空间极限下 AdS 态的引力记忆

Gravitational Memory from AdS States in the Flat-Space Limit

David A. Lowe, Juanyi Yang · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：研究全局 AdS4 中带动力学标量物质的线性化引力重构，基于固定边界度规和非平凡可归一化数据的真空 Einstein 扰动。外部标量源制备激发态并修正全息 Ward 恒等式，随后演化可在无边界驱动下进行。将体物质源响应与真空扰动分离，推导出含 Einstein 约束所要求物质修正的电四极主方程。还验证柱形边界积分逐模重现 Regge-Wheeler 主模场，但不将其等同于完整全局 Fefferman-Graham 重构。对弱相互作用标量（在平直空间极限下无质量），从推迟引力演化中提取 Bondi shear 和 news。所得超平移记忆与出射能量重分布及边界功的四极矩数值一致。在有序平直空间极限下，后续边界控制不改变首个事件波形，尽管在有限 AdS 半径下会抵消残余引力模式。

English: We study linearized gravitational reconstruction in global AdS$_4$ with dynamical scalar matter, building on vacuum Einstein perturbations with fixed boundary metric and nontrivial normalizable data. External scalar sources prepare an excited state and modify the holographic Ward identities, while subsequent evolution can proceed without boundary driving. Separating the bulk matter-sourced response from vacuum perturbations, we derive an electric quadrupole master equation with the matter-dependent corrections required by the Einstein constraints. We also verify that a cylindrical boundary integral reproduces the Regge--Wheeler master field mode by mode, without identifying it with the full global Fefferman--Graham reconstruction. For weakly interacting scalars that become massless in the flat-space limit, we extract the Bondi shear and news from retarded gravitational evolution. The resulting supertranslation memory agrees numerically with the outgoing energy redistribution and the quadrupole moment of the boundary work. In the ordered flat-space limit, later boundary controls leave the first event's waveform unchanged, despite cancelling residual gravitational modes at finite AdS radius.

一句话导读：在 AdS4 线性化引力中由标量源制备激发态，并在平直空间极限下提取 Bondi shear/news 与超平移引力记忆。

相关度：medium — 涉及 AdS/CFT 线性化引力与平直空间 BMS/超平移记忆，属于全息字典和经典引力交叉；不直接涉及所列全息凝聚态、超流、探针膜等支线。

AdS/CFT 平直极限中的希尔伯特空间 II. 无质量粒子

Hilbert space in the flat limit of AdS/CFT. II. Massless particles

Kevin Nguyen · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：承接本系列第一部分（其中描述了大质量散射态的出现），本文展示固定维度 Δ 和任意整数自旋 s 的共形群幺正最低权表示 D(Δ,s) 如何收缩为无质量 Poincaré 粒子表示。这需要将 Inönü–Wigner 收缩 so(2,3)→iso(1,3) 与无穷大 boost 结合，此时无质量散射态自然以 Carrollian 基出现。对于 Δ>s+1，D(Δ,s) 收缩为所有自旋 s'≤s 的无质量粒子的直和。在幺正界，低自旋态变为零范数态，因此 D(s+1,s) 收缩为携带两个最大螺旋度 J=±s 的单一无质量粒子表示。两种情形下，内积在一次与螺旋度无关的重标度后收敛到 Poincaré 不变内积，因此整个收缩过程保持幺正性。

English: Building on Part I of this series, where we described the emergence of massive scattering states, we demonstrate how unitary lowest-weight representations $\mathcal D(\Delta,s)$ of the conformal group with fixed dimension $\Delta$ and arbitrary integer spin $s$ contract to massless Poincar\'e particle representations. This requires combining the In\"on\"u--Wigner contraction $\mathfrak{so}(2,3) \to \mathfrak{iso}(1,3)$ with an infinite boost, in which case massless scattering states naturally emerge in a carrollian basis. For $\Delta>s+1$, we find that $\mathcal D(\Delta,s)$ contracts to the direct sum of massless particles of all spins $s'\leq s$. At the unitarity bound, lower-spin states become null so that $\mathcal D(s+1,s)$ contracts to a single massless particle representation carrying the two maximal helicities $J=\pm s$. In both cases, the inner product converges to the Poincar\'e-invariant one after a single helicity-independent rescaling, so that unitarity is preserved throughout the contraction.

一句话导读：通过 so(2,3)→iso(1,3) 收缩与无穷 boost，证明共形群最低权表示在 AdS/CFT 平直极限中给出无质量 Poincaré 粒子并保持幺正性。

相关度：medium — 属于 AdS/CFT 平直极限与群论表示的基础研究，涉及全息希尔伯特空间和字典；但不直接涉及用户重点列出的超流超导、相变/气泡成核、QNM、探针膜等具体支线。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

脐点观测站

Umbilic Observatories

Dionysios Anninos, Ra\'ul Arias, Dami\'an A Galante · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：本文研究四维广义相对论中最简单的脐点边界例子——嵌入四维 Minkowski 时空的三维 de Sitter 超曲面上的引力动力学。脐点面的诱导度规与外曲率成比例。我们考虑在类时超曲面上施加共形边界条件和 Dirichlet 边界条件两种情形。两种情形下均未发现虚频率随角动量增长的线性模式。共形边界条件除体激发外还允许一个动力学边界自由度；脐点性质使边界 Weyl 模式与体动力学线性解耦，从而得到线性稳定的动力学。相反，Dirichlet 问题没有边界模式。因此脐点超曲面可能为在存在类时边界时定义引力动力学提供更好的起点。分析中还将结构与渐近 Minkowski 时空的空间无穷及其更有限的实现进行类比。

English: We study gravitational dynamics for the simplest example of an umbilic boundary in four-dimensional general relativity --- a three-dimensional de Sitter hypersurface embedded in a four-dimensional Minkowski world. Generally speaking, an umbilic surface has the property that its induced metric is proportional to its extrinsic curvature. We consider the case of both conformal boundary conditions, as well as Dirichlet boundary conditions imposed at the timelike hypersurface. For both cases, we find that there are no linearised modes whose imaginary frequency grows with angular momentum. The conformal boundary conditions allow for a dynamical boundary degree of freedom, in addition to the bulk excitations. The umbilic property linearly decouples the boundary Weyl mode from the bulk dynamics, yielding linearly stable dynamics. In contrast, the Dirichlet problem has no boundary mode. The umbilic hypersurfaces may thus constitute an improved starting point for defining gravitational dynamics in the presence of a timelike boundary. Throughout our analysis, we draw parallels to the structure of spatial infinity for asymptotically Minkowski spacetimes and a more finite realisation thereof.

一句话导读：研究四维 Minkowski 中嵌入的三维 dS 脐点边界上的线性引力动力学与边界条件稳定性。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，讨论类时边界与线性稳定性；不涉及全息超流、p波、multi-trace等用户重点全息方向。

AdS黑洞的共形边界与光子球

Conformal boundaries and the photon sphere of AdS black holes

Ra\'ul Arias, Valentino Di Pierro, Silvia Georgescu · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：本文研究具有有限类时边界、并满足共形边界条件的四维Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的线性化引力扰动。这些边界条件固定诱导度规的共形类与平均曲率，同时允许边界Weyl模涨落。光子球在此问题中具有特殊地位：位于该半径的静态边界是全脐的，标量问题可精确分离为独立的体扇区与边界扇区。作者证明，对于二阶及更高阶球谐，不存在指数增长的标量与矢量模式；并确定当边界趋近全脐时，大角动量不稳定性如何被抑制。在视界附近，他们恢复了通用的Rindler谱，包括声学与剪切激发。随后数值追踪这些流体模式随边界向外移动的演化，发现它们趋近渐近AdS4边界处的标准引力拟正规模。对于大黑洞，这些渐近端点对应对偶三维共形场论中的声学与剪切极点。文章还研究了AdS4黑膜扰动，并在没有中间全脐边界的情况下发现流体模式的类似延拓。

English: We study linearized gravitational perturbations of four-dimensional Schwarzschild-AdS black holes with finite timelike boundaries subject to conformal boundary conditions. These fix the conformal class of the induced metric and the mean curvature, while allowing the boundary Weyl mode to fluctuate. The photon sphere plays a distinguished role in this problem: a static boundary at this radius is totally umbilic, and the scalar problem separates exactly into independent bulk and boundary sectors. We prove the absence of exponentially growing scalar and vector modes for spherical harmonics of degree two and higher. We determine how large angular momentum instabilities are suppressed as the boundary approaches umbilicity. Near the horizon, we recover the universal Rindler spectrum, including sound and shear excitations. We numerically follow these fluid modes as the boundary is moved outwards and find that they approach standard gravitational quasinormal modes at the asymptotic AdS$_4$ boundary. For large black holes, these asymptotic endpoints correspond to the sound and shear poles of the dual three-dimensional conformal field theory. We also study perturbations of the AdS$_4$ black brane, where we find an analogous continuation of the fluid modes in the absence of an intermediate umbilic boundary.

一句话导读：研究有限共形边界下Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的线性引力扰动，揭示光子球全脐边界、稳定性、Rindler流体模式与对偶CFT声学/剪切极点之间的联系。

相关度：high — 论文直接涉及AdS黑洞线性引力扰动、拟正规模与流体/输运模式，并连接经典引力与全息对偶，匹配用户对QNM与经典引力支线的关注。

QNM与非线性演化经典引力/广义相对论

AdS$_5\times$SE$_5$中的巨引力子与环面箭图规范理论的超共形指数

Giant Gravitons in AdS$_5\times$SE$_5$ and the Superconformal Index of Toric Quiver Gauge Theories

Evan Deddo, Sabarenath Jayaprakash, James T. Liu, Leopoldo A. Pando Zayas · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-11-02

中文摘要：我们通过在IIB型背景AdS$_5\times SE_5$中量子化探针D3膜，导出巨引力子对超共形指数的贡献。对于$SE_5=T^{1,1}$和$Y^{p,q}$，我们确定最大巨引力子的标量、矢量与费米子涨落，并提取其BPS谱。所得单字母指数与对偶环面箭图规范理论的巨引力子展开预言一致。这些结果表明，有限$N$超对称计数函数中的单巨引力子项可直接由量子化D3膜自由度恢复，并指向巨引力子展开在超引力侧具有普适的世界体起源。我们将结果视为一种内禀引力框架（类似开-闭-开三元性）的一步，用于处理通常外包给对偶场论的超对称微观态计数问题。

English: We derive giant-graviton contributions to superconformal indices by quantizing probe D3-branes in type IIB backgrounds $\mathrm{AdS}_{5}\times SE_{5}$. For $SE_{5}=T^{1,1}$ and $Y^{p,q}$, we determine the scalar, vector, and fermionic fluctuations of maximal giant gravitons and extract their BPS spectra. The resulting single-letter indices agree with those predicted by the giant-graviton expansions of the dual toric quiver gauge theories. These results show that the single-giant terms in finite-$N$ supersymmetric counting functions can be recovered directly from quantized D3-brane degrees of freedom and point toward a universal worldvolume origin of giant-graviton expansions directly on the supergravity side. We view the results as a step in an intrinsically gravitational framework, much like the open-closed-open triality, for addressing supersymmetric microstate-counting problems that are conventionally outsourced to the dual field theory.

一句话导读：量子化AdS$_5\times$SE$_5$中的探针D3膜，从超引力侧直接重现巨引力子对环面箭图规范理论超共形指数的单巨引力子贡献。

相关度：low — 主题属于AdS/CFT基础、超共形指数与巨引力子，方法上涉及探针D3膜量子化；但与用户重点列出的全息超流超导、p波与多分量序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、multi-trace、相变与气泡成核、QNM与非线性演化、冷原子/凝聚态实验启发及经典引力等具体支线无直接重合，仅探针膜技术有一般性关联。
